globalThis.GinjoEngine=(()=>{// Existing Office Script parser; reused unchanged.
function parseAgentRecords(
rawText        ,
allStatusConfigs                ,
idRegexGlobal        ,
idRegexSingle        ,
nameMap                        
)                {
const lines = rawText
.split("\n")
.map(v => v.trim())
.filter(v => v !== "")

const records                = []
const seenByStatus                              = {}

for (const config of allStatusConfigs) {
    seenByStatus[statusKey(config.statusName)] = new Set        ()
}

for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const line = lines[i]
    idRegexGlobal.lastIndex = 0
    const ids = line.match(idRegexGlobal)

    if (!ids) continue

    for (const id of ids) {
        const blockLines           = [line]

        for (let j = i + 1; j < lines.length && j <= i + 6; j++) {
            if (idRegexSingle.test(lines[j])) break
            blockLines.push(lines[j])
        }

        const matchedConfig = findMatchedStatusConfig(blockLines, allStatusConfigs)
        if (!matchedConfig) continue

        const matchedStatusKey = statusKey(matchedConfig.statusName)
        const idKey = normalizeId(id)

        if (seenByStatus[matchedStatusKey].has(idKey)) continue

        const statusIndex = findStatusLineIndex(blockLines, matchedConfig)
        const durationText = findDurationText(blockLines.slice(0, statusIndex))
        const name = nameMap[idKey] || ""

        seenByStatus[matchedStatusKey].add(idKey)
        records.push({
            id,
            idKey,
            name,
            statusName: matchedConfig.statusName,
            statusKeyValue: matchedStatusKey,
            durationText: durationText === "" ? "시간 확인 불가" : formatDurationText(durationText)
        })
    }
}

return records
}

// (84) 시프트는 주말 2일만 출근합니다. 평일 데일리에서는 근무로 계산하지 않습니다.
function weekendOnlyOff(shiftText, date) {
 if (!/\(\s*84\s*\)/.test(String(shiftText || ""))) return false
 const d = new Date(String(date || "") + "T00:00:00Z").getUTCDay()
 return Number.isFinite(d) && d >= 1 && d <= 5
}

function parseShiftWindowForWorkFlag(shiftText        , workFlag        )                     {
const parsed = parseShiftDefinition(shiftText)
if (!parsed) return null

const workHours = parseScheduledWorkHours(workFlag)
// (모성) 단축 근무와 30분이 붙은 시프트는 근무구분 숫자와 관계없이 시프트에 적힌 시각 그대로 근무합니다.
// 예: NE1200-1900(모성), 근무구분 6 -> 12:00~19:00
if (workHours !== null && (/모성/.test(normalizeText(shiftText)) || parsed.halfHour)) {
    return { startMinutes: parsed.startMinutes, endMinutes: parsed.endMinutes }
}
if (workHours !== null) {
    // J열의 실근무시간과 시프트의 시작 시각으로 종료 시각을 계산합니다.
    const mealMinutes = workHours === parsed.shiftHours ? parsed.mealMinutes : workHours >= 8 ? 60 : 0
    return {
        startMinutes: parsed.startMinutes,
        endMinutes: parsed.startMinutes + Math.max(1, Math.round(workHours * 60)) + mealMinutes
    }
}

const halfDayType = getHalfDayType(workFlag)
if (halfDayType === null) return { startMinutes: parsed.startMinutes, endMinutes: parsed.endMinutes }

// 반차 계산 전에 4.5시간 구간의 가장자리 30분을 제외합니다.
// 코드(105) 유무와 관계없이 18:30~23:00은 19:00~23:00 기준입니다.
let startMinutes = parsed.startMinutes
let endMinutes = parsed.endMinutes
if (parsed.mealMinutes === 0 && endMinutes - startMinutes === 270) {
    if (startMinutes % 60 === 30 && endMinutes % 60 === 0) startMinutes += 30
    else if (startMinutes % 60 === 0 && endMinutes % 60 === 30) endMinutes -= 30
}

// 반차는 최대 8시간 근무의 절반(4시간)을 기준으로 계산합니다.
const workMinutes = endMinutes - startMinutes - parsed.mealMinutes
const halfWorkMinutes = Math.round(Math.min(workMinutes, 480) / 2)

if (halfDayType === 2) {
    // 반차2: 앞 절반 미출근. 식사 시간이 있으면 출근 경계에 더합니다.
    // 12:00~22:00 -> 12:00 + 4시간 + 식사 1시간 = 17:00 출근.
    return {
        startMinutes: startMinutes + halfWorkMinutes + parsed.mealMinutes,
        endMinutes
    }
}

// 반차1: 뒤 절반 미출근. 앞 절반 근무에는 식사를 포함하지 않습니다.
// 12:00~22:00 -> 16:00 퇴근, 18:30~23:00 -> 19:00~21:00 근무.
return {
    startMinutes,
    endMinutes: startMinutes + halfWorkMinutes
}
}

function parseShiftDefinition(shiftText        )                               {
const clean = normalizeText(shiftText).replace(/（/g, "(").replace(/）/g, ")")
const range = clean.match(/(?:^|[^0-9])(\d{2})(\d{2})\s*[-–—~～]\s*(\d{2})(\d{2})(?!\d)/)
if (range) {
    const startHour = Number(range[1])
    const startMinute = Number(range[2])
    const endHour = Number(range[3])
    const endMinute = Number(range[4])
    if (!isValidHourMinute(startHour, startMinute)) return null
    // 종료 시각 24~29시는 다음 날 00~05시입니다(예: 2000-2900).
    if (endHour < 0 || endHour > 29 || endMinute < 0 || endMinute > 59) return null
    let startMinutes = startHour * 60 + startMinute
    let endMinutes = endHour * 60 + endMinute
    if (endMinutes < startMinutes) endMinutes += 1440
    if (endMinutes === startMinutes || endMinutes - startMinutes > 1440) return null
    // 시프트의 30분은 휴식입니다. 시작이 30분이면 +30분이 출근, 끝이 30분이면 -30분이 퇴근입니다.
    // 예: 1830-2300(105) -> 19:00~23:00, 1100-1530(105) -> 11:00~15:00
    let halfHour = false
    if (startMinute === 30) { startMinutes += 30; halfHour = true }
    if (endMinute === 30) { endMinutes -= 30; halfHour = true }
    if (endMinutes <= startMinutes) return null
    const codeMatch = clean.match(/\(\s*(209|105)\s*\)/)
    const code = codeMatch ? codeMatch[1] : ""
    if (code === "105") {
        // 4.5시간 구간의 끝 30분 또는 시작 30분을 휴무로 제외합니다.
        if (endMinutes - startMinutes === 270) {
            if (startMinute === 30 && endMinute === 0) startMinutes += 30
            else if (startMinute === 0 && endMinute === 30) endMinutes -= 30
        }
        const workMinutes = endMinutes - startMinutes
        if (startMinutes >= 1440) { startMinutes -= 1440; endMinutes -= 1440 }
        // 105 단축도 4시간을 넘을 수 있으므로 표시된 종료 시각까지 근무합니다.
        return { startMinutes, endMinutes, shiftHours: workMinutes / 60, mealMinutes: 0, halfHour }
    }
    const elapsedMinutes = endMinutes - startMinutes
    // (209) 코드보다 명시된 출퇴근 시각을 우선합니다.
    // ne1100-1700(209) 같은 단축 근무도 해당 구간 그대로 인정합니다.
    // 식사시간은 기존처럼 9시간 이상 구간에서만 1시간 제외합니다.
    const mealMinutes = elapsedMinutes >= 540 ? 60 : 0
    const shiftHours = (elapsedMinutes - mealMinutes) / 60
    return { startMinutes, endMinutes, shiftHours, mealMinutes, halfHour }
}

// 기존 5시8H 형식도 계속 지원합니다.
let match = clean.match(/(\d{1,2})\s*시\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*h/i)
if (!match) match = clean.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})\s*[:：]\s*00.*?(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*h/i)
if (!match) return null
const startHour = Number(match[1])
const shiftHours = Number(match[2])
if (!isValidHourMinute(startHour, 0) || !Number.isFinite(shiftHours) || shiftHours <= 0) return null
const startMinutes = startHour * 60
const mealMinutes = shiftHours >= 8 ? 60 : 0
return { startMinutes, endMinutes: startMinutes + Math.round(shiftHours * 60) + mealMinutes, shiftHours, mealMinutes }
}

function parseScheduledWorkHours(value        )                {
const text = normalizeText(value)
if (!/^(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)?|\.\d+)$/.test(text)) return null
const hours = Number(text)
return Number.isFinite(hours) && hours > 0 ? hours : null
}

function parseHourStartMinutes(timeText        , timeValue                           )                {
const clean = normalizeText(timeText)
const compact=clean.match(/^(\d{1,2})([0-5]\d)$/)
if(compact&&Number(compact[1])<=28)return (Number(compact[1])%24)*60+Number(compact[2])

// 숫자 시각, 운영시간 24~28시와 '28시(04)' 표기를 지원합니다.
const operationHour = clean.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:\s*시)?(?:\s*[（(]\s*\d{1,2}\s*[）)])?$/)
if (operationHour) {
    const hour = Number(operationHour[1])
    if (hour >= 0 && hour <= 28) return (hour % 24) * 60
}
const lateHour = clean.match(/^(2[4-8])\s*(?:[:：]\s*(\d{1,2})|시(?:\s*(\d{1,2})\s*분)?)$/)
if (lateHour) {
    const minute = Number(lateHour[2] || lateHour[3] || 0)
    if (minute >= 0 && minute < 60) return (Number(lateHour[1]) % 24) * 60 + minute
}

let match = clean.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s*[:：]\s*(\d{1,2})(?:\s*[:：]\s*\d{1,2})?$/)

if (match) {
    const hour = Number(match[1])
    const minute = Number(match[2])
    if (isValidHourMinute(hour, minute)) return hour * 60 + minute
}

match = clean.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s*시(?:\s*(\d{1,2})\s*분)?$/)

if (match) {
    const hour = Number(match[1])
    const minute = match[2] ? Number(match[2]) : 0
    if (isValidHourMinute(hour, minute)) return hour * 60 + minute
}

if (typeof timeValue === "number" && !isNaN(timeValue)) {
    const dayFraction = timeValue - Math.floor(timeValue)
    const totalMinutes = Math.round(dayFraction * 1440)
    const normalizedMinutes = ((totalMinutes % 1440) + 1440) % 1440
    return normalizedMinutes
}

return null
}

function getHalfDayType(workFlag        )               {
const key = statusKey(workFlag)
// 반차1: 앞 근무(뒤 절반 휴무), 반차2: 뒤 근무(앞 절반 휴무)
if (key === "반차1") return 1
if (key === "반차2") return 2
return null
}

function isValidHourMinute(hour        , minute        )          {
if (isNaN(hour) || isNaN(minute)) return false
if (hour < 0 || hour > 23) return false
if (minute < 0 || minute > 59) return false
return true
}

function normalizeText(text        )         {
return String(text ?? "")
.replace(/\u00A0/g, " ")
.replace(/[\u200B\u200C\u200D]/g, "")
.replace(/\s+/g, " ")
.trim()
}

function normalizeId(id        )         {
return normalizeText(id).toLowerCase()
}

function normalizeNameKey(name        )         {
return normalizeText(name).replace(/\s+/g, "")
}

function statusKey(text        )         {
return normalizeText(text).replace(/\s+/g, "")
}

function findMatchedStatusConfig(lines          , statusConfigs                )                           {
for (const line of lines) {
const lineKey = statusKey(line)

    for (const config of statusConfigs) {
        for (const matchName of config.matchNames) {
            if (lineKey === statusKey(matchName)) return config
        }
    }
}

return undefined
}

function findStatusLineIndex(lines          , config              )         {
for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
const lineKey = statusKey(lines[i])

    for (const matchName of config.matchNames) {
        if (lineKey === statusKey(matchName)) return i
    }
}

return lines.length
}

function findDurationText(lines          )         {
// 입력 형식은 기본적으로 다음 3줄 구조입니다.
// 1) 사번 2) 경과시간 3) 상태값
// 이 함수에는 상태값 직전까지만 전달되므로, 사번 다음 줄을 그대로 우선 사용합니다.
for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
const clean = normalizeText(lines[i])
if (clean === "") continue

    // "3시 2분 5초", "1시간 20분", "45초"처럼 시간 단위가 포함된 줄을
    // 일부만 잘라내지 않고 줄 전체 그대로 반환합니다.
    if (/\d+\s*(?:일|시간|시|분|초)/.test(clean)) {
        return clean
    }
}

// 시간 단위 표기가 조금 달라도 사번과 상태값 사이의 첫 줄을 우선 사용합니다.
for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const clean = normalizeText(lines[i])
    if (clean !== "") return clean
}

// 사번과 시간이 같은 줄에 붙어 들어오는 예외 형식도 처리합니다.
if (lines.length > 0) {
    const firstLine = normalizeText(lines[0])
    const withoutId = normalizeText(
        firstLine.replace(/\b(?:cssp-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|[Kk](?:[Ee])?\d+)\b/g, "")
    )
    if (withoutId !== "") return withoutId
}

return ""
}

function formatDurationText(value        )         {
const clean = normalizeText(value)
const clock = clean.match(/^(\d+):([0-5]?\d):([0-5]?\d)$/)
if (clock) return `${Number(clock[1])}:${pad2(Number(clock[2]))}:${pad2(Number(clock[3]))}`
const units = /(\d+)\s*(일|시간|시|분|초)/g
let totalSeconds = 0
let found = false
let match                        
while ((match = units.exec(clean)) !== null) {
    found = true
    const amount = Number(match[1])
    const unit = match[2]
    totalSeconds += amount * (unit === "일" ? 86400 : unit === "시간" || unit === "시" ? 3600 : unit === "분" ? 60 : 1)
}
if (!found || clean.replace(units, "").trim() !== "") return clean
const hours = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600)
const minutes = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60) % 60
const seconds = totalSeconds % 60
return `${hours}:${pad2(minutes)}:${pad2(seconds)}`
}

function pad2(value        )         {
return value < 10 ? `0${value}` : `${value}`
}

function isNowInShift(nowMinutes        , window             )          {
// 반차2처럼 시작 시각도 자정을 넘는 구간을 함께 처리합니다.
const duration = window.endMinutes - window.startMinutes
if (duration <= 0) return false
const elapsed = ((nowMinutes - window.startMinutes) % 1440 + 1440) % 1440
return elapsed < duration
}

function isMinuteInWindow(nowMinutes        , startMinutes        , endMinutes        )          {
if (endMinutes > startMinutes) {
    return nowMinutes >= startMinutes && nowMinutes < endMinutes
}

// 종료시간이 시작시간보다 이르면 자정을 넘기는 구간으로 처리합니다.
return nowMinutes >= startMinutes || nowMinutes < endMinutes
}

function isWorkTarget(value        )          {
const key = statusKey(value)
return parseScheduledWorkHours(value) !== null || key === "교육" || getHalfDayType(value) !== null
}

function formatShiftForDisplay(shiftText        )         {
const clean = normalizeText(shiftText)
const range = clean.match(/(?:^|[^0-9])(\d{4})\s*[-–—~～]\s*(\d{4})(?!\d)/)
return range ? range[1] + "-" + range[2] : clean.replace(/[（(][^）)]*[）)]/g, "").trim()
}

const VERSION='20261117.web1';

const ID=/\b(?:cssp-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|[Kk](?:[Ee])?\d+)\b/g;
const round=n=>Math.round(n*1e8)/1e8;
const unique=items=>[...new Set(items)];

const key=value=>normalizeNameKey(value).toLowerCase();
const mod=(n,m)=>((n%m)+m)%m;
function limits(goal){goal=Number(goal);if(!Number.isFinite(goal)||goal<=0)throw Error('목표 인원을 확인하세요.');const index=Array.from({length:14},(_,i)=>i+1).findIndex(n=>goal<=n);return index<0?{low:.95,high:1.05}:{low:LOW[index],high:HIGH[index]};}
function metrics(minutes,target){
 const {low,high}=limits(target),lower=round(target*60*low),upper=round(target*60*high),shortage=round(Math.max(0,lower-minutes)),excess=round(Math.max(0,minutes-upper));
 return {minutes,target,low,high,lower,upper,rate:minutes/(target*60),status:shortage>0?'미달':excess>0?'과투입':'정상',add:Math.ceil(shortage),remove:Math.ceil(excess),shortage,excess,untilLow:Math.floor(round(minutes-lower))+1,untilHigh:Math.floor(round(upper-minutes))+1};
}
function businessClock(iso=new Date().toISOString()){
 const ms=Date.parse(iso);if(!Number.isFinite(ms))throw Error('기준 시각을 확인하세요.');const local=new Date(ms+9*3600000),hour24=local.getUTCHours(),date=new Date(local);if(hour24<5)date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate()-1);
 return {iso:new Date(ms).toISOString(),ms,date:date.toISOString().slice(0,10),hour:hour24<5?hour24+24:hour24,minute:local.getUTCMinutes(),clock:hour24*60+local.getUTCMinutes(),serial:ms/86400000+25569+9/24};
}
function timeAt(date,clock){if(!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)||!/^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(clock))throw Error('날짜와 시각을 확인하세요.');return businessClock(date+'T'+clock+':00+09:00');}
function time(value){const result=parseHourStartMinutes(String(value??''),value);return result===null?-1:result;}
function exclusionTime(value){if(typeof value==='number'&&Number.isFinite(value)&&value>=0&&value<2){const minutes=Math.round(value*1440);return minutes<300?minutes+1440:minutes;}const m=ginjoNormalizeClock(value).match(/^(\d{1,2}):([0-5]\d)$/);if(!m||Number(m[1])>29||(Number(m[1])===29&&Number(m[2])>0))return -1;const minutes=Number(m[1])*60+Number(m[2]);return minutes<300?minutes+1440:minutes;}
function within(minute,start,end){return [minute,minute+1440,minute-1440].some(value=>value>=start&&value<end);}
// 투입률에 반영되는 근무 상태: 대화 가능·자리에 없음·교육·식사·휴식. 회의·오프라인·그 외 상태는 근무가 아닙니다.
const WORK_STATUSES=['대화 가능','자리에 없음','교육','식사','휴식'];
function isPresent(row){return WORK_STATUSES.includes(row.statusName);}
function decodeHours(hours){
 const serialTimes=hours.some(row=>typeof row[2]==='number'&&row[2]>0&&row[2]<2&&!Number.isInteger(row[2])),known=hours.filter(row=>row[2]!==1).map(row=>time(row[2])),ones=hours.flatMap((row,i)=>row[2]===1?[i]:[]);
 const adjacentMidnight=ones.find(i=>{const previous=hours[(i+hours.length-1)%hours.length]?.[2],next=hours[(i+1)%hours.length]?.[2];return time(previous)===1380||(next!==1&&time(next)===60);});
 const midnightOne=known.includes(0)?-1:adjacentMidnight??((known.includes(60)||serialTimes)?(ones[0]??-1):-1);
 const hourly=hours.map((row,i)=>{const minutes=i===midnightOne?0:time(row[2]);return {hour:minutes<0?-1:minutes/60<5?minutes/60+24:minutes/60,target:row[0],dailyCount:row[4]};});
 return hourly;
}
function parseForecast(list){
 return (Array.isArray(list)?list:[]).slice(0,9).filter(item=>item&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(item.date||''))).map(item=>{
  const staff=Array.isArray(item.staff)?item.staff.slice(0,300).map(r=>Array.isArray(r)?[r[0]??'',r[1]??'',r[2]??'']:['','','']):null;
  if(item.error||!Array.isArray(item.values))return {date:item.date,sourceSheet:item.sheetName||'',error:String(item.error||'자료 없음'),hourly:[],staff};
  try{
   const hourly=decodeHours(item.values).filter(r=>Number.isInteger(r.hour)&&r.hour>=5&&r.hour<=28),hasOT=Array.isArray(item.otValues)&&item.otValues.length===24;
   if(hasOT)for(const r of hourly){const row=item.otValues[r.hour-5]||[],names=row.slice(4).flatMap(v=>String(v??'').split(/[\n,，/]+/)).map(v=>String(v).trim()).filter(Boolean);r.otCount=names.reduce((n,x)=>n+otEntry(x,(r.hour%24)*60).len/60,0);}
   return {date:item.date,sourceSheet:item.sheetName||'',hasOT,hourly,staff};
  }
  catch(error){return {date:item.date,sourceSheet:item.sheetName||'',error:'시간대 목표 해석 실패',hourly:[]};}
 });
}
// 데일리 OT 칸의 짧은 OT 표기: 홍길동(20) · 홍길동(20분) · 홍길동 20분 -> 그 시간 처음 20분만 OT
// 홍길동(23:30-23:50) · 홍길동(2330-2350) -> 적힌 시각만 OT. 표기가 없으면 그 시간 60분 전체가 OT입니다.
function otEntry(token,rowMinute){
 const s=String(token??'').trim();let m=s.match(/^(.*?)\s*\(?\s*(\d{1,2}):?(\d{2})\s*[-~～]\s*(\d{1,2}):?(\d{2})\s*\)?$/);
 if(m&&m[1].trim()){const a=Number(m[2])*60+Number(m[3]),b=Number(m[4])*60+Number(m[5]);if(a<=1800&&b<=1800){const start=a%1440,len=((b-a)%1440+1440)%1440;if(len>0&&len<=180)return {name:m[1].trim(),start,len,partial:true};}}
 m=s.match(/^(.*?)\s*\(\s*(\d{1,3})\s*분?\s*\)$/)||s.match(/^(.*?)\s*(\d{1,3})\s*분$/);
 if(m&&m[1].trim()){const n=Number(m[2]);if(n>0&&n<60)return {name:m[1].trim(),start:rowMinute,len:n,partial:true};}
 return {name:s,start:rowMinute,len:60,partial:false};
}
function fromBlocks(packet){
 if(!packet||!Array.isArray(packet.blocks))throw Error('데일리 JSON의 blocks가 없습니다.');const blocks=new Map(packet.blocks.map(block=>[String(block.address).replace(/^([A-Z]+6:[A-Z]+)140$/,'$1180'),block.values]));
 const ids=blocks.get('C6:C180'),people=blocks.get('E6:G180'),flags=blocks.get('I6:K180'),absence=blocks.get('L6:M180'),reason=blocks.get('R6:R180'),hours=blocks.get('AY6:BC29'),ot=blocks.get('BL6:CD29');
 if(!ids||!people||!flags||!absence||!reason||!hours||!ot)throw Error('데일리 내보내기의 필수 7개 범위를 확인하세요. 최신 데일리 내보내기 스크립트가 필요합니다.');
 const schedule=[];for(let i=0;i<ids.length;i++){const found=String(ids[i]?.[0]??'').match(ID)??[];for(const id of found)schedule.push({id,name:txt(people[i]?.[0]),shift:txt(people[i]?.[2]),leader:txt(flags[i]?.[0]),flag:txt(flags[i]?.[1]),absenceStart:absence[i]?.[0]??'',absenceEnd:absence[i]?.[1]??'',reason:txt(reason[i]?.[0]),sourceRow:i+6});}
 // 사번이 '-'(또는 빈칸)인 행의 L·M은 이미 결점이 있는 사람의 추가 결점 시간입니다. 사람 없이 그 시간만큼 차감합니다.
 const deductions=[];for(let i=0;i<ids.length;i++){const c=txt(ids[i]?.[0]);if((String(ids[i]?.[0]??'').match(ID)??[]).length||(c&&c!=='-'))continue;const a=absence[i]?.[0],b=absence[i]?.[1];if(a===null||a===undefined||String(a).trim()===''||b===null||b===undefined||String(b).trim()==='')continue;deductions.push({absenceStart:a,absenceEnd:b,reason:txt(reason[i]?.[0]),sourceRow:i+6});}
 const hourly=decodeHours(hours);
 // OT 표 시간이 엑셀 시각(소수)으로 저장된 경우, 24:00은 숫자 1로 들어옵니다. 이때 1을 01시가 아니라 자정으로 읽습니다.
 const otSerial=ot.some(row=>typeof row[0]==='number'&&row[0]>0&&row[0]<1);
 const otTime=v=>otSerial&&typeof v==='number'&&Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0&&v<=2?Math.round((v%1)*1440)%1440:time(v);
 // OT 표(BL6:CD29)는 05시부터 한 줄에 한 시간씩 24줄입니다. 05~23시 줄 시각이 순서대로 맞으면,
 // 자정 이후 줄은 표기(24:00·1·0:00 등)와 관계없이 줄 순서로 시간을 정합니다.
 const byIndex=ot.length===24&&ot.slice(0,19).filter((row,i)=>otTime(row[0])===(5+i)*60).length>=15;
 const overtime=ot.flatMap((row,i)=>{const minute=byIndex?((5+i)%24)*60:otTime(row[0]);return minute<0?[]:[{minute,names:row.slice(4).flatMap(v=>String(v??'').split(/[\n,，/]+/)).map(txt).filter(Boolean)}];});
 const staffRows=people.map((p,i)=>[p?.[0]??'',p?.[1]??'',p?.[2]??'','',flags[i]?.[0]??'',flags[i]?.[1]??'']);
 return normalizeDaily({type:'ginjo-daily',version:1,date:packet.date,capturedAt:packet.capturedAt,sourceSheet:packet.sourceSheet,staffRows,schedule,hourly,ot:overtime,deductions,forecast:parseForecast(packet.forecast),next:packet.next&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(packet.next.date||''))&&Array.isArray(packet.next.blocks)?{date:packet.next.date,sourceSheet:String(packet.next.sourceSheet||''),blocks:packet.next.blocks,capturedAt:packet.capturedAt,forecast:(Array.isArray(packet.forecast)?packet.forecast:[]).filter(f=>String(f?.date||'')>packet.next.date)}:null,...(packet.advanced?{advanced:true}:{})});
}
function normalizeDaily(input){
 if(input?.blocks)return fromBlocks(input);
 if(input?.type!=='ginjo-daily'||!Array.isArray(input.schedule)||!Array.isArray(input.hourly)||!Array.isArray(input.ot))throw Error(APP_CONFIG.name+' 데일리 JSON 파일이 아닙니다.');
 if(!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(input.date??''))throw Error('데일리 운영일이 없습니다.');
 if(input.schedule.length>10000)throw Error('데일리 명단이 너무 큽니다.');
 const hourly=input.hourly.map(row=>{let hour;if(typeof row.hour==='number'&&Number.isInteger(row.hour)&&row.hour>=0&&row.hour<=28)hour=row.hour<5?row.hour+24:row.hour;else{const minute=time(row.hour);hour=minute<0?-1:minute/60<5?minute/60+24:minute/60;}if(!Number.isInteger(hour)||hour<5||hour>28)throw Error('데일리 시간행은 05~28시 또는 00~04시 구간이어야 합니다.');return {...row,hour};});
 return {...input,hourly,schedule:input.schedule.map(r=>({...r,id:txt(r.id),idKey:normalizeId(r.id),name:txt(r.name),nameKey:key(r.name),flag:txt(r.flag),shift:txt(r.shift),window:weekendOnlyOff(txt(r.shift),input.date)?null:parseShiftWindowForWorkFlag(txt(r.shift),txt(r.flag)),weekendOnly:/\(\s*84\s*\)/.test(txt(r.shift)),leaveStart:time(r.absenceStart),leaveEnd:time(r.absenceEnd)}))};
}
function automaticExclusions(input){
 const daily=normalizeDaily(input),rows=[],issues=[],seen=new Set(),extra=[];
 const stamp=minute=>{const n=((minute%1440)+1440)%1440;return String(Math.floor(n/60)).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(n%60).padStart(2,'0');};
 for(const [index,person]of daily.schedule.entries()){
  const empty=value=>value===null||value===undefined||String(value).trim()==='',sourceRow=person.sourceRow??index+6;
  if(!String(person.reason??'').includes('과투입')){
   // 과투입 외 L·M은 금일 추가 제외(차감) 시간입니다. 예: 18:08~18:18 -> 10분 차감
   if(empty(person.absenceStart)&&empty(person.absenceEnd))continue;
   const s=exclusionTime(person.absenceStart),f=exclusionTime(person.absenceEnd),e=f<s?f+1440:f;
   if(empty(person.absenceStart)||empty(person.absenceEnd)||s<0||f<0||s===e||e-s>1440||!person.idKey){issues.push({kind:'데일리 차감시간 확인',id:person.id,name:person.name,message:`L${sourceRow}·M${sourceRow} 차감 시작·종료를 확인하세요.`});continue;}
   const k='x:'+person.idKey+':'+s+':'+e;if(seen.has(k))continue;seen.add(k);
   extra.push({id:person.id,idKey:person.idKey,name:person.name||person.id,start:stamp(s),end:stamp(e),startMinute:s,endMinute:e,minutes:e-s,sourceRow,reason:String(person.reason??'')});
   continue;
  }
  if(empty(person.absenceStart)&&empty(person.absenceEnd))continue;
  const start=exclusionTime(person.absenceStart),finish=exclusionTime(person.absenceEnd),end=finish<start?finish+1440:finish;
  if(empty(person.absenceStart)||empty(person.absenceEnd)||start<0||finish<0||start===end||end-start>1440||!person.idKey){issues.push({kind:'데일리 과투입 시간 확인',id:person.id,name:person.name,message:`L${sourceRow}·M${sourceRow} 과투입 제외 시작·종료를 확인하세요.`});continue;}
  const key=person.idKey+':'+start+':'+end;if(seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);
  rows.push({id:person.id,idKey:person.idKey,name:person.name||person.id,start:stamp(start),end:stamp(end),startMinute:start,endMinute:end,sourceRow,reason:String(person.reason)});
 }
 const anon=[];
 for(const d of Array.isArray(daily.deductions)?daily.deductions:[]){
  const s=exclusionTime(d.absenceStart),f=exclusionTime(d.absenceEnd),e=f<s?f+1440:f;
  if(s<0||f<0||s===e||e-s>1440){issues.push({kind:'데일리 차감시간 확인',id:'-',name:'-',message:`L${d.sourceRow}·M${d.sourceRow} 차감 시작·종료를 확인하세요.`});continue;}
  anon.push({id:'-',idKey:'',name:'-',start:stamp(s),end:stamp(e),startMinute:s,endMinute:e,minutes:e-s,sourceRow:d.sourceRow,reason:String(d.reason??''),anon:true});
 }
 return {rows,issues,extra,anon};
}
function anonOverlap(anon,hour){let n=0;for(const r of anon||[])for(let m=hour*60;m<hour*60+60;m++)if(within(m,r.startMinute,r.endMinute))n++;return n;}

function calculate(input,raw,options={}){
 const daily=normalizeDaily(input),clock=businessClock(options.at),diagnostics=[];
 if(daily.date!==clock.date)throw Error(`데일리 운영일이 다릅니다: 자료 ${daily.date} / 기준 ${clock.date}`);
 if(!String(raw??'').trim())throw Error('A1 상태 원문을 붙여넣으세요.');if(raw.length>32767)throw Error('A1 원문은 32,767자까지 입력할 수 있습니다.');
 const nameMap={};for(const r of daily.schedule)if(r.idKey&&!nameMap[r.idKey])nameMap[r.idKey]=r.name;
 const parsed=parseAgentRecords(raw,configs,new RegExp(ID.source,'g'),new RegExp(ID.source),nameMap);
 if(!parsed.length)throw Error('원문에서 사번과 상태를 읽지 못했습니다. 사번·경과시간·상태가 포함된 원문인지 확인하세요.');
 const dictionary=new Map(),names=new Map();for(const r of [...daily.schedule,...parsed]){const id=r.idKey;if(!id)continue;if(!dictionary.has(id))dictionary.set(id,{id:r.id,name:r.name||nameMap[id]||r.id});const nk=key(r.name||nameMap[id]||'');if(nk){const set=names.get(nk)??new Set();set.add(id);names.set(nk,set);}}
 const resolve=value=>{const search=normalizeId(key(value));if(dictionary.has(search))return [search];return [...(names.get(key(value))??[])];};
 const training=new Set(daily.schedule.filter(r=>key(r.flag)==='교육').map(r=>r.nameKey));for(const n of options.training??[])if(key(n))training.add(key(n));
 const exceptions=new Set((options.exceptions??[]).map(key).filter(Boolean));
 const automatic=automaticExclusions(daily);diagnostics.push(...automatic.issues);
 const exclusions=[...automatic.rows,...automatic.extra].map(row=>({id:row.idKey,start:row.startMinute,end:row.endMinute}));for(const [i,row]of (options.exclusions??[]).entries()){
  if(!txt(row.name)&&!txt(row.start)&&!txt(row.end))continue;const ids=resolve(row.name),start=exclusionTime(row.start),finish=exclusionTime(row.end),end=finish<start?finish+1440:finish;
  if(!txt(row.name)||ids.length!==1||start<0||finish<0||end===start||end-start>1440)throw Error(`제외 입력 ${i+1}번: 이름·시작·종료를 확인하세요. 동명이인은 사번을 입력하세요.`);
  exclusions.push({id:ids[0],start,end});
 }
 const excluded=(id,minute)=>exclusions.some(r=>r.id===id&&within(minute,r.start,r.end));
 const excludedMinutes=(id,hour)=>Array.from({length:60},(_,i)=>hour*60+i).filter(m=>excluded(id,m)).length;
 const ot=[];for(const row of daily.ot)for(const token of row.names??[]){const e=otEntry(token,row.minute),name=e.name,ids=resolve(name);if(ids.length!==1){diagnostics.push({kind:'OT 이름 확인',id:'',name,message:ids.length?'동명이인 OT: 사번 확인':'OT 이름이 근무표에 없음'});continue;}ot.push({id:ids[0],start:e.start,len:e.len,partial:e.partial});}
 const adj=[];for(const a of options.adjust??[]){const ids=resolve(a.name),s=exclusionTime(a.start),f=exclusionTime(a.end);if(ids.length!==1||s<0||f<0)continue;const len=((f-s)%1440+1440)%1440;if(!len||len>180)continue;adj.push({id:ids[0],start:mod(s,1440),len});}
 for(const a of adj){for(let i=ot.length-1;i>=0;i--){const r=ot[i];if(r.id!==a.id)continue;if(Math.floor(r.start/60)===Math.floor(a.start/60)||Array.from({length:r.len},(_,k)=>mod(r.start+k,1440)).some(m=>mod(m-a.start,1440)<a.len))ot.splice(i,1);}ot.push({id:a.id,start:a.start,len:a.len,partial:true,confirmed:true});}
 const adjAt=(id,minute)=>adj.some(a=>a.id===id&&mod(minute-a.start,1440)<a.len);
 const hasOT=(id,minute)=>ot.some(r=>r.id===id&&mod(minute-r.start,1440)<r.len);
 const currentOT=new Set(ot.filter(r=>mod(clock.clock-r.start,1440)<r.len).map(r=>r.id));
 const regularAt=(id,minute)=>daily.schedule.some(r=>r.idKey===id&&isWorkTarget(r.flag)&&r.window&&isNowInShift(mod(minute,1440),r.window));
 const onLeave=(id,minute)=>automatic.rows.some(r=>r.idKey===id&&within(minute,r.startMinute,r.endMinute))||daily.schedule.some(r=>r.idKey===id&&!String(r.reason??'').includes('과투입')&&r.leaveStart>=0&&r.leaveEnd>=0&&r.leaveStart!==r.leaveEnd&&isMinuteInWindow(mod(minute,1440),r.leaveStart,r.leaveEnd));
 const hourStart=clock.hour*60;
 const halfInactive=id=>daily.schedule.some(r=>r.idKey===id&&getHalfDayType(r.flag))&&!regularAt(id,hourStart)&&!currentOT.has(id);
 const isTraining=id=>training.has(key(dictionary.get(id)?.name??''));
 const chosen=new Map();for(const row of parsed){const id=row.idKey,old=chosen.get(id);if(!old||(isPresent(row)&&!isPresent(old)))chosen.set(id,row);}
 const people=[...chosen].map(([id,row])=>{
  const half=halfInactive(id),education=isTraining(id),present=isPresent(row)&&!half,base=present&&!education?60:0,deduct=excludedMinutes(id,clock.hour),expected=regularAt(id,hourStart)||currentOT.has(id),restored=base===0&&row.statusName==='회의'&&!half&&!education&&expected&&excluded(id,hourStart+clock.minute);let credit=base?base-deduct:restored?60-deduct:0;
  let adjNote='';{const hr=Array.from({length:60},(_,k)=>hourStart+k),aw=adj.find(a=>a.id===id&&hr.some(m=>mod(m-a.start,1440)<a.len));
   if(aw&&base){const el=mod(clock.clock-aw.start,1440),over=el>=aw.len&&el<aw.len+300;if(over){adjNote='보정 OT 종료 후 계속 로그인 → 1시간 인정';}else{credit=hr.filter(m=>mod(m-aw.start,1440)<aw.len&&!excluded(id,m)).length;adjNote='보정 OT '+credit+'분 인정';}}}
  const roster=daily.schedule.find(r=>r.idKey===id);
  return {adjNote,id:row.id,idKey:id,name:dictionary.get(id)?.name??id,shift:formatShiftForDisplay(roster?.shift??''),status:row.statusName,duration:row.durationText,present,education,half,expected,ot:currentOT.has(id),base,excluded:deduct,restored,minutes:credit};
 });
 const metric=hour=>{const rows=daily.hourly.filter(r=>r.hour===hour);if(rows.length!==1)throw Error(`${hour}시 데일리 시간행이 ${rows.length}개입니다.`);const target=Number(rows[0].target);limits(target);return {...rows[0],target};};
 // 60분 미만 OT: 지금 로그인한 사람은 위 people에서 보정 분만 인정.
 // 지금은 로그인 안 했어도 이번 시간에 OT 근무로 확인된 적이 있으면(OT 현황 '실제 와서 근무') 보정 분만큼 인정 — 정상 퇴근한 경우.
 // 한 번도 확인 안 됐으면(지각·미출근) 0분.
 const seenOT=new Set((options.otSeen||[]).map(x=>String(x).toLowerCase()));
 const adjCredit=adj.filter(a=>!people.some(p=>p.idKey===a.id&&p.present)&&seenOT.has(clock.hour+'|'+String(a.id).toLowerCase())).reduce((n,a)=>n+Array.from({length:60},(_,k)=>hourStart+k).filter(m=>mod(m-a.start,1440)<a.len&&!onLeave(a.id,m)&&!excluded(a.id,m)).length,0);
 const anonNow=anonOverlap(automatic.anon,clock.hour),currentRow=metric(clock.hour),current=metrics(Math.max(0,people.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.minutes,0)+adjCredit-anonNow),currentRow.target),login=people.filter(r=>r.base>0).length;
 Object.assign(current,{hour:clock.hour,login,dailyCount:currentRow.dailyCount,baseMinutes:people.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.base,0),excludedMinutes:people.filter(r=>r.base>0).reduce((n,r)=>n+r.excluded,0),restoredMinutes:people.filter(r=>r.restored).reduce((n,r)=>n+r.minutes,0),anonMinutes:anonNow,adjMinutes:adjCredit});
 const missing=[];for(const [id,who]of dictionary){if(!regularAt(id,hourStart)&&!currentOT.has(id))continue;if(people.some(r=>r.idKey===id&&r.present)||exceptions.has(key(who.name))||onLeave(id,clock.clock))continue;missing.push({...who,idKey:id,shift:formatShiftForDisplay(daily.schedule.find(r=>r.idKey===id)?.shift??''),status:chosen.get(id)?.statusName??'원문 없음',ot:currentOT.has(id),reason:'지각 확인필요'});}
 const shiftCount=[...dictionary.keys()].filter(id=>{for(let m=hourStart;m<hourStart+60;m++)if(regularAt(id,m))return true;return false;}).length;
 const off=people.filter(r=>!exceptions.has(key(r.name))&&!onLeave(r.idKey,clock.clock)&&((r.present&&!regularAt(r.idKey,hourStart)&&!currentOT.has(r.idKey))||(r.half&&r.status!=='오프라인')));

 const nowMin=hourStart+clock.minute,planRows=[];let planMinutes=0;
 const shiftOf=id=>formatShiftForDisplay(daily.schedule.find(r=>r.idKey===id)?.shift??'');
 for(const [id,who]of dictionary){
  if(isTraining(id)||exceptions.has(key(who.name)))continue;
  let minutes=0;for(let m=hourStart;m<hourStart+60;m++){if(onLeave(id,m)||excluded(id,m))continue;if(regularAt(id,m)||hasOT(id,mod(m,1440)))minutes++;}
  planMinutes+=minutes;
  const workNow=regularAt(id,nowMin)||hasOT(id,mod(nowMin,1440)),leaveNow=onLeave(id,nowMin),exNow=excluded(id,nowMin),planned=workNow&&!leaveNow&&!exNow;
  const person=people.find(r=>r.idKey===id),actual=Boolean(person&&person.present&&!person.education&&!exNow)||(adjAt(id,mod(nowMin,1440))&&!exNow);
  if(planned===actual)continue;
  const status=person?.status??'원문 없음';let reason;
  if(planned){
   reason=status==='원문 없음'?'데일리상 근무인데 원문에 없음 (미로그인·사번 확인)':status==='회의'?'회의 상태 → 로그인 집계 제외 (회의 승인 여부 확인)':status==='오프라인'?'오프라인 상태 (지각·조퇴 확인)':person?.half?'반차 구간으로 처리됨 (근무구분 확인)':status+' 상태 확인';
   if(hasOT(id,mod(nowMin,1440))&&!regularAt(id,nowMin))reason=status==='원문 없음'?'OT 시간인데 원문에 없음 (OT 미출근 확인)':'OT 시간인데 '+status+' 상태';
  }else{
   const work=m=>regularAt(id,m)||hasOT(id,mod(m,1440)),lastEnd=Array.from({length:180},(_,i)=>nowMin-1-i).find(work),nextStart=Array.from({length:180},(_,i)=>nowMin+1+i).find(work),hm=m=>String(Math.floor(mod(m,1440)/60)).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(mod(m,60)).padStart(2,'0');
   reason=leaveNow?'데일리상 휴가·외출 시간인데 로그인':lastEnd!==undefined&&(nextStart===undefined||nowMin-lastEnd<=nextStart-nowMin)?(regularAt(id,lastEnd)?`${hm(lastEnd+1)} 퇴근인데 아직 로그인 (퇴근 확인)`:`${hm(lastEnd+1)} OT 끝났는데 아직 로그인 (OT 종료 확인)`):nextStart!==undefined?`${hm(nextStart)} 출근 예정인데 먼저 로그인 (조기 출근)`:daily.schedule.some(r=>r.idKey===id)?'근무 예정 외 로그인 (시프트·OT 반영 확인)':'근무표에 없는 사번';
  }
  planRows.push({id:who.id,idKey:id,name:who.name,shift:shiftOf(id),status,planned,actual,minutes,reason});
 }
 for(const p of people){if(dictionary.has(p.idKey)||!p.present||p.education)continue;planRows.push({id:p.id,idKey:p.idKey,name:p.name,shift:'',status:p.status,planned:false,actual:true,minutes:0,reason:'근무표에 없는 사번'});}
 const plan=metrics(Math.max(0,planMinutes-anonNow),currentRow.target);
 const actualNowCount=people.filter(p=>p.present&&!p.education&&!excluded(p.idKey,nowMin)).length+adj.filter(a=>adjAt(a.id,mod(nowMin,1440))&&!excluded(a.id,nowMin)&&!people.some(p=>p.idKey===a.id&&p.present&&!p.education)).length;
 Object.assign(plan,{count:actualNowCount-planRows.filter(r=>r.actual).length+planRows.filter(r=>r.planned).length,actualCount:actualNowCount,rows:planRows.sort((a,b)=>Number(a.actual)-Number(b.actual)||String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name),'ko'))});
 // 지각 체크·미출근 확인도 지금 시각 기준(데일리상 근무 vs 실제 로그인)으로 맞춥니다.
 {const planById=new Map(planRows.map(r=>[r.idKey,r]));
  missing.length=0;
  for(const r of planRows)if(r.planned&&!exceptions.has(key(r.name)))missing.push({id:r.id,idKey:r.idKey,name:r.name,shift:r.shift,status:r.status,ot:hasOT(r.idKey,mod(nowMin,1440))&&!regularAt(r.idKey,nowMin),reason:r.reason});
  const offNow=[];
  for(const p of people){
   if(p.education||exceptions.has(key(p.name)))continue;
   const pr=planById.get(p.idKey);
   if(pr&&pr.actual&&!pr.planned){offNow.push({...p,reason:pr.reason});continue;}
   if(p.half&&p.status!=='오프라인')offNow.push({...p,reason:'반차 구간 로그인 확인'});
  }
  off.splice(0,off.length,...offNow);}
 let next=null,nextPeople=[];if(clock.hour<28){const nextRow=metric(clock.hour+1);for(const [id,who]of dictionary){let regular=0,total=0;for(let minute=(clock.hour+1)*60;minute<(clock.hour+2)*60;minute++){if(isTraining(id)||exceptions.has(key(who.name))||onLeave(id,minute)||excluded(id,minute))continue;const work=regularAt(id,minute),overtime=hasOT(id,mod(minute,1440));if(work)regular++;if(work||overtime)total++;}nextPeople.push({...who,idKey:id,regular,minutes:total,otMinutes:total-regular});}next=metrics(Math.max(0,nextPeople.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.minutes,0)-anonOverlap(automatic.anon,clock.hour+1)),nextRow.target);Object.assign(next,{hour:clock.hour+1,otMinutes:nextPeople.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.otMinutes,0),otPeople:nextPeople.filter(r=>r.otMinutes>0).length});}
 const sourceIds=new Set(daily.schedule.map(r=>r.idKey));for(const r of daily.schedule){if(daily.schedule.filter(x=>x.idKey===r.idKey).length>1)diagnostics.push({kind:'근무표 중복',id:r.id,name:r.name,message:'동일 사번이 여러 행: 시프트 확인'});if(!r.window)diagnostics.push({kind:'시프트 확인',id:r.id,name:r.name,message:'근무시간 해석 불가'});}
 for(const r of people)if(!sourceIds.has(r.idKey))diagnostics.push({kind:'원문만 존재',id:r.id,name:r.name,message:'근무표에 없는 사번'});
 for(const r of missing)diagnostics.push({kind:'지각 확인필요',id:r.id,name:r.name,message:r.status==='원문 없음'?'현재 근무 대상이나 원문 누락':r.status+' 상태 확인'});
 const duplicateIds=unique((raw.match(ID)??[]).map(normalizeId)).filter(id=>(raw.match(ID)??[]).filter(v=>normalizeId(v)===id).length>1);for(const id of duplicateIds)diagnostics.push({kind:'원문 중복',id,name:dictionary.get(id)?.name??id,message:'같은 사번이 여러 번 등장'});
 for(const r of exclusions)if(excludedMinutes(r.id,clock.hour)>0&&!chosen.has(r.id))diagnostics.push({kind:'제외 인원 확인',id:r.id,name:dictionary.get(r.id)?.name??r.id,message:'원문에 없어 인정분 0: 출근 확인'});
 const gap=currentRow.dailyCount===''||currentRow.dailyCount===null||currentRow.dailyCount===undefined?NaN:Number(currentRow.dailyCount)-login,candidates=[];if(Number.isFinite(gap))for(const [id,who]of dictionary){const regular=regularAt(id,hourStart)?1:0,person=people.find(r=>r.idKey===id),actual=person?.base>0?1:0,effect=actual-regular;if(effect*(-gap)<=0)continue;const state=person?.status??'원문 없음',trainingFlag=person?.education;
  const reason=effect>0?(currentOT.has(id)?'정규근무 외 OT 로그인: 데일리 OT 반영 확인':'정규근무 대상 외 로그인: 시프트·예외 확인'):state==='원문 없음'?'정규근무 대상이나 원문에 없음':state==='회의'?'회의 상태로 로그인 집계 제외':trainingFlag?'신입 교육 제외 인원으로 로그인 집계 제외':state==='오프라인'?'오프라인 상태로 로그인 집계 제외':'반차·근무구분과 현재 상태 확인';
  candidates.push({...who,status:state,effect,reason,score:effect>0?(currentOT.has(id)?100:90):state==='원문 없음'?100:state==='회의'?95:trainingFlag?90:85});
 }
 candidates.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
 return {type:'ginjo-result',version:1,sample:Boolean(daily.sample),mode:'web',at:clock.iso,date:clock.date,hour:clock.hour,sourceAt:daily.capturedAt,shiftCount,otEntries:ot.map(r=>({id:r.id,start:r.start,len:r.len,name:dictionary.get(r.id)?.name||r.id})),current,plan,next,people,missing,off,meetings:people.filter(r=>r.status==='회의'),diagnostics:unique(diagnostics.map(r=>JSON.stringify(r))).map(JSON.parse),candidates,nextPeople,gap,rawCount:parsed.length};
}
function forecastDay(input,options={}){
 const daily=normalizeDaily(input);
 const dictionary=new Map(),names=new Map(),rowsById=new Map();
 for(const r of daily.schedule){const id=r.idKey;if(!id)continue;if(!dictionary.has(id))dictionary.set(id,{id:r.id,name:r.name||r.id});const nk=key(r.name||'');if(nk){const set=names.get(nk)??new Set();set.add(id);names.set(nk,set);}if(!rowsById.has(id))rowsById.set(id,[]);rowsById.get(id).push(r);}
 const resolve=value=>{const search=normalizeId(key(value));if(dictionary.has(search))return [search];return [...(names.get(key(value))??[])];};
 const training=new Set(daily.schedule.filter(r=>key(r.flag)==='교육').map(r=>r.nameKey));for(const n of options.training??[])if(key(n))training.add(key(n));
 const exceptions=new Set((options.exceptions??[]).map(key).filter(Boolean));
 const automatic=automaticExclusions(daily);
 const exclusions=[...automatic.rows,...automatic.extra].map(row=>({id:row.idKey,start:row.startMinute,end:row.endMinute}));
 for(const row of options.exclusions??[]){
  if(!txt(row.name)&&!txt(row.start)&&!txt(row.end))continue;
  const ids=resolve(row.name),start=exclusionTime(row.start),finish=exclusionTime(row.end),end=finish<start?finish+1440:finish;
  if(!txt(row.name)||ids.length!==1||start<0||finish<0||end===start||end-start>1440)continue;
  exclusions.push({id:ids[0],start,end});
 }
 const exById=new Map();for(const r of exclusions){if(!exById.has(r.id))exById.set(r.id,[]);exById.get(r.id).push(r);}
 const autoById=new Map();for(const r of automatic.rows){if(!autoById.has(r.idKey))autoById.set(r.idKey,[]);autoById.get(r.idKey).push(r);}
 const otById=new Map();for(const row of daily.ot)for(const token of row.names??[]){const e=otEntry(token,row.minute),ids=resolve(e.name);if(ids.length!==1)continue;if(!otById.has(ids[0]))otById.set(ids[0],[]);otById.get(ids[0]).push(e);}
 const excluded=(id,minute)=>(exById.get(id)??[]).some(r=>within(minute,r.start,r.end));
 const onLeave=(id,minute)=>(autoById.get(id)??[]).some(r=>within(minute,r.startMinute,r.endMinute))||(rowsById.get(id)??[]).some(r=>!String(r.reason??'').includes('과투입')&&r.leaveStart>=0&&r.leaveEnd>=0&&r.leaveStart!==r.leaveEnd&&isMinuteInWindow(mod(minute,1440),r.leaveStart,r.leaveEnd));
 const regularAt=(id,minute)=>(rowsById.get(id)??[]).some(r=>isWorkTarget(r.flag)&&r.window&&isNowInShift(mod(minute,1440),r.window));
 for(const a of options.adjust??[]){const ids=resolve(a.name),s=exclusionTime(a.start),f=exclusionTime(a.end);if(ids.length!==1||s<0||f<0)continue;const len=((f-s)%1440+1440)%1440;if(!len||len>180)continue;const st=mod(s,1440),list=(otById.get(ids[0])??[]).filter(e=>!(Math.floor(e.start/60)===Math.floor(st/60)||Array.from({length:e.len},(_,k)=>mod(e.start+k,1440)).some(m=>mod(m-st,1440)<len)));list.push({start:st,len});otById.set(ids[0],list);}
 const hasOT=(id,minute)=>(otById.get(id)??[]).some(e=>mod(minute-e.start,1440)<e.len);
 const people=[...dictionary].filter(([id,who])=>!training.has(key(who.name))&&!exceptions.has(key(who.name))).map(([id])=>id);
 const hours=[],seen=new Set();
 for(const row of daily.hourly){
  const hour=row.hour,target=Number(row.target);if(!Number.isInteger(hour)||hour<5||hour>28||seen.has(hour)||!(target>0))continue;seen.add(hour);
  let minutes=0;
  for(const id of people)for(let minute=hour*60;minute<(hour+1)*60;minute++){if(onLeave(id,minute)||excluded(id,minute))continue;if(regularAt(id,minute)||hasOT(id,mod(minute,1440)))minutes++;}
  let heads=0;const m0=hour*60;for(const id of people)if(!onLeave(id,m0)&&!excluded(id,m0)&&(regularAt(id,m0)||hasOT(id,mod(m0,1440))))heads++;
  hours.push({hour,dailyCount:row.dailyCount,heads,...metrics(Math.max(0,minutes-anonOverlap(automatic.anon,hour)),target)});
 }
 return {date:daily.date,hours:hours.sort((a,b)=>a.hour-b.hour)};
}
function actionText(metric){if(!metric)return '다음 운영일 데일리가 필요합니다.';return metric.add>0?`미달 · 최소 ${metric.add}분 추가 확보 필요`:metric.remove>0?`과투입 · 최소 ${metric.remove}분 제외 필요`:'정상 범위 · 추가 조치 없음';}
function forecastText(metric){if(!metric)return ['다음 운영일','데일리 확인 필요'];return [metric.add>0?`미달 · ${metric.add}분 확보 필요`:`${metric.untilLow}분 비면 미달`,metric.remove>0?`과투입 · ${metric.remove}분 제외 필요`:`${metric.untilHigh}분 더하면 과투입`];}
function makeSample(at=new Date().toISOString()){
 const clock=businessClock(at),start=(clock.hour%24)*60;const schedule=Array.from({length:12},(_,i)=>({id:'K'+(9000+i),name:'예시상담사 '+String(i+1).padStart(2,'0'),shift:clock.hour>=20?'2000-2900':'0500-2400',flag:'8',absenceStart:'',absenceEnd:'',reason:''}));
 // An explicit 24-hour work allowance keeps the demo stable at any viewing hour.
 for(const r of schedule){r.shift='0500-2900';r.flag='24';}
 const hh=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');schedule[0].shift=hh(clock.hour%24)+'00-'+hh(clock.hour%24+1)+'00';schedule[0].flag='1';
 const hourly=Array.from({length:24},(_,i)=>({hour:5+i,target:11.4,dailyCount:12}));
 return {daily:{type:'ginjo-daily',version:1,sample:true,date:clock.date,capturedAt:at,sourceSheet:'가상 데일리',schedule,hourly,ot:[{minute:mod(start+60,1440),names:[schedule[0].name]}]},raw:schedule.map((r,i)=>`${r.id}\n00:${String(2+i).padStart(2,'0')}:00\n${i===0?'회의':'대화 가능'}`).join('\n'),at};
}

return {VERSION,limits,metrics,businessClock,timeAt,normalizeDaily,fromBlocks,automaticExclusions,calculate,forecastDay,otEntry,actionText,forecastText,makeSample};})();
globalThis.GinjoBridge=(()=>{function decodeJSON(text){
 if(String(text).length>8*1024*1024)throw Error('JSON 파일은 8MB까지 읽을 수 있습니다.');
 let value;try{value=JSON.parse(String(text).replace(/^\uFEFF/,''));if(typeof value==='string')value=JSON.parse(value);}catch{throw Error('JSON 파일 형식이 아닙니다. 엑셀 내보내기의 전체 JSON을 선택하세요.');}
 if(!value||typeof value!=='object'||!['ginjo-daily','ginjo-daily-blocks','ginjo-result','ginjo-error','ginjo-config'].includes(value.type))throw Error(APP_CONFIG.name+' 데일리·상태출력 결과·연결 설정 JSON이 아닙니다.');
 return value;
}
function validateRequest(input){
 if(!String(input.raw??'').trim())throw Error('A1 원문을 먼저 붙여넣으세요.');if(input.raw.length>32767)throw Error('원문은 32,767자까지 입력할 수 있습니다.');
 if(!Array.isArray(input.exclusions)||input.exclusions.length>10)throw Error('제외 입력은 10명까지 가능합니다.');
 for(const [i,e]of input.exclusions.entries()){if(!e.name&&!e.start&&!e.end)continue;if(!String(e.name).trim()||!/^\d{1,2}:[0-5]\d$/.test(ginjoNormalizeClock(e.start))||!/^\d{1,2}:[0-5]\d$/.test(ginjoNormalizeClock(e.end))||ginjoNormalizeClock(e.start)===ginjoNormalizeClock(e.end))throw Error(`제외 입력 ${i+1}번의 이름·시작·종료를 확인하세요.`);}
 if(input.training.length>26||input.exceptions.length>10)throw Error('교육 26명, 일반 예외 10명까지 입력할 수 있습니다.');
 if(!Number.isFinite(Date.parse(input.calculationAt)))throw Error('기준 날짜와 시각을 확인하세요.');
 return input;
}
function isMatchingResult(result,requestId){return Boolean(result&&result.requestId===requestId&&['ginjo-result','ginjo-error'].includes(result.type));}
function sharePointSource(value){
 const url=new URL(String(value).trim());
 if(url.protocol!=='https:'||!url.hostname.endsWith('.sharepoint.com')||url.username||url.password)throw Error('HTTPS SharePoint JSON 주소만 연결할 수 있습니다.');
 const path=url.searchParams.get('id')||decodeURIComponent(url.pathname);
 if(!path.startsWith('/')||!path.toLowerCase().endsWith('.json'))throw Error('데일리.json 파일의 주소 또는 파일이 선택된 SharePoint 목록 주소를 입력하세요.');
 const site=path.match(/^\/(?:sites|teams)\/[^/]+/i)?.[0];
 if(!site)throw Error('SharePoint 사이트의 JSON 주소인지 확인하세요.');
 const direct=new URL(path.split('/').map(encodeURIComponent).join('/'),url.origin);
 const literal=encodeURIComponent(path.replaceAll("'","''")).replaceAll("'",'%27');
 const rest=new URL(`${site}/_api/web/GetFileByServerRelativePath(decodedUrl='${literal}')/$value`,url.origin);
 return {direct:direct.href,rest:rest.href};
}
class FolderBridge {
 constructor(mode,onData,onError,onState){this.mode=mode;this.onData=onData;this.onError=onError;this.onState=onState;this.root=null;this.source=null;this.pending=null;this.busy=false;this.last='';this.timer=null;}
 get fileName(){return this.mode==='excel'?'상태출력-결과.json':'데일리.json';}
 async connectURL(value){
  const source=sharePointSource(value);this.stop();this.urlSource=source;this.last='';this.urlVersion=(this.urlVersion||0)+1;
  this.onState('SharePoint JSON 연결 확인 중 · 30초마다 확인');this.start();await this.pollURL(true);
 }
 async pollURL(force=false){
  if(this.urlBusy||!this.urlSource)return;this.urlBusy=true;
  const version=this.urlVersion,source=this.urlSource,attempts=[];
  try{
   let data,text;
   for(const endpoint of [source.rest,source.direct]){
    const controller=new AbortController(),timeout=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),15000);
    try{
     const url=new URL(endpoint);url.searchParams.set('_ginjo',String(Date.now()));
     const response=await fetch(url.href,{credentials:'include',cache:'no-store',signal:controller.signal});
     if(!response.ok)throw Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
     text=await response.text();if(/^\s*</.test(text))throw Error('JSON 대신 로그인·미리보기 HTML이 반환되었습니다.');
     data=decodeJSON(text);
     if(this.mode==='web'&&!['ginjo-daily','ginjo-daily-blocks'].includes(data.type))throw Error('데일리 JSON 형식이 아닙니다.');
     if(this.mode==='excel'&&!['ginjo-result','ginjo-error'].includes(data.type))throw Error('상태출력 결과 JSON 형식이 아닙니다.');
     break;
    }catch(error){attempts.push({method:endpoint===source.rest?'SharePoint REST':'파일 직접 읽기',message:error.name==='AbortError'?'15초 응답 시간 초과':error.message});}
    finally{clearTimeout(timeout);}
   }
   if(version!==this.urlVersion)return;
   if(!data){const error=Error('SharePoint JSON을 읽지 못했습니다. 회사 로그인·파일 읽기 권한 또는 미리보기의 통신 제한을 확인하세요. 오류 상세에서 진단을 볼 수 있습니다.');error.details={attempts,pageOrigin:globalThis.origin??'',pageURL:globalThis.location?.href??'',secureContext:globalThis.isSecureContext??null,lastSuccess:this.lastSuccess??null};throw error;}
   if(force||text!==this.last){this.onData(data,false);this.last=text;}
   this.lastSuccess=new Date().toISOString();this.onHealthy?.();this.onState('SharePoint 연결됨 · 마지막 확인 '+new Date(this.lastSuccess).toLocaleTimeString('ko-KR')+' · 30초마다 확인');
  }catch(error){if(version===this.urlVersion){this.onState('SharePoint 연결 실패 · 마지막 성공 '+(this.lastSuccess?new Date(this.lastSuccess).toLocaleTimeString('ko-KR'):'없음')+' · 30초 후 재시도');this.onError(error);}}
  finally{this.urlBusy=false;}
 }
 async chooseFolder(){
  this.stop();
  if(!globalThis.showDirectoryPicker)throw Error('이 브라우저에서는 폴더 자동 연결을 지원하지 않습니다. JSON 파일 불러오기를 사용하세요.');
  this.root=await showDirectoryPicker({id:'ginjo-'+this.mode,mode:this.mode==='excel'?'readwrite':'read'});this.source=null;this.last='';
  this.onState('폴더 연결됨 · '+this.root.name);await this.poll(true);this.start();
 }
 async chooseFile(){
  this.stop();
  if(!globalThis.showOpenFilePicker)throw Error('JSON 파일 불러오기를 사용하세요.');const [handle]=await showOpenFilePicker({multiple:false,types:[{description:APP_CONFIG.name+' JSON',accept:{'application/json':['.json']}}]});this.source=handle;this.root=null;this.last='';await this.poll(true);this.start();
 }
 start(){clearInterval(this.timer);this.timer=setInterval(()=>this.urlSource?this.pollURL(false):this.poll(false),this.urlSource?30000:5000);}
 stop(){clearInterval(this.timer);this.timer=null;this.root=null;this.source=null;this.urlSource=null;this.urlVersion=(this.urlVersion||0)+1;this.lastSuccess=null;this.pending=null;this.onState('자동 연결 해제됨');}
 async poll(force=false){
  if(this.busy||(!this.root&&!this.source))return;this.busy=true;
  try{
   let handle;
   if(this.pending&&this.root){try{const folder=await this.root.getDirectoryHandle('결과');handle=await folder.getFileHandle('result-'+this.pending.requestId+'.json');}catch(error){if(error.name==='NotFoundError'){this.onState('엑셀 처리 대기 · '+this.pending.requestId.slice(-8));return;}throw error;}}
   else handle=this.source??await this.root.getFileHandle(this.fileName);
   const file=await handle.getFile(),signature=file.name+':'+file.lastModified+':'+file.size;
   if(!force&&signature===this.last)return;const data=decodeJSON(await file.text());
   if(this.pending&&!isMatchingResult(data,this.pending.requestId))throw Error('다른 요청의 결과입니다. 현재 요청 결과를 기다립니다.');
   if(this.mode==='excel'&&!['ginjo-result','ginjo-error'].includes(data.type))throw Error('엑셀 연결형에는 상태출력 결과 JSON을 선택하세요.');
   if(this.mode==='web'&&!['ginjo-daily','ginjo-daily-blocks'].includes(data.type))throw Error('웹 계산형에는 데일리 JSON을 선택하세요.');
   if(this.pending)this.pending=null;this.last=signature;this.onData(data,false);this.onState('파일 갱신 확인 · '+file.name);
  }catch(error){if(error.name==='NotFoundError')this.onState(this.fileName+' 파일 대기 중');else this.onError(error);}
  finally{this.busy=false;}
 }
 async submit(input){
  validateRequest(input);if(!this.root)throw Error('먼저 SharePoint 동기화 폴더를 연결하세요. 폴더 연결이 불가능하면 접수 JSON 다운로드를 사용하세요.');
  return this.enqueue({...input,type:'ginjo-request',version:1,requestId:crypto.randomUUID(),createdAt:new Date().toISOString()});
 }
 async submitRead(){return this.enqueue({type:'ginjo-request',version:1,action:'read',requestId:crypto.randomUUID(),createdAt:new Date().toISOString()});}
 async enqueue(request){
  if(!this.root)throw Error('먼저 SharePoint 동기화 폴더를 연결하세요.');
  if(this.pending)throw Error('이전 요청의 처리 결과를 기다리고 있습니다. 중복 접수하지 않았습니다.');
  const folder=await this.root.getDirectoryHandle('요청',{create:true}),file=await folder.getFileHandle('req-'+request.requestId+'.json',{create:true}),stream=await file.createWritable();
  try{await stream.write(JSON.stringify(request));await stream.close();}catch(error){try{await stream.abort();}catch{}throw error;}
  this.pending=request;this.last='';this.onState('접수파일 저장됨 · 회사 엑셀 결과 대기');this.start();return request;
 }
}

return {decodeJSON,validateRequest,isMatchingResult,sharePointSource,FolderBridge};})();
globalThis.GinjoDailyEditor=(()=>{
 const specs=[['C6:C180',6,3,175,1],['E6:G180',6,5,175,3],['I6:K180',6,9,175,3],['L6:M180',6,12,175,2],['R6:R180',6,18,175,1],['AY6:BC29',6,51,24,5],['BL6:CD29',6,64,24,19]];
 const clone=value=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
 const letter=n=>{let s='';while(n>0){n--;s=String.fromCharCode(65+n%26)+s;n=Math.floor(n/26);}return s;};
 const column=s=>[...s].reduce((n,c)=>n*26+c.charCodeAt(0)-64,0);
 const address=(c,r)=>letter(c)+r;
 const editable=(c,r)=>r>=6&&((r<=180&&[3,5,7,10,12,13,18].includes(c))||(r<=29&&([51,55,64].includes(c)||(c>=68&&c<=82))));
 const scalar=value=>value===null||value===undefined?'':value;
 function locate(cell){const m=String(cell).match(/^([A-Z]{1,2})(\d{1,3})$/);if(!m)throw Error('잘못된 셀 주소입니다.');const c=column(m[1]),r=Number(m[2]),spec=specs.find(s=>r>=s[1]&&r<s[1]+s[3]&&c>=s[2]&&c<s[2]+s[4]);return {c,r,spec};}
 function get(packet,cell){const {c,r,spec}=locate(cell);if(!spec)return '';return scalar(packet.blocks.find(b=>b.address===spec[0])?.values[r-spec[1]]?.[c-spec[2]]);}
 function put(packet,cell,value){const {c,r,spec}=locate(cell);if(!spec||!editable(c,r))throw Error(cell+' 칸은 직접 수정할 수 없습니다.');packet.blocks.find(b=>b.address===spec[0]).values[r-spec[1]][c-spec[2]]=value;}
 const timeColumns=new Set([12,13,53,64]);
 function display(packet,cell){const value=get(packet,cell),{c,r}=locate(cell);if(timeColumns.has(c)&&typeof value==='number'&&Number.isFinite(value)){if(c===53&&value===1&&globalThis.GinjoEngine.normalizeDaily(packet).hourly[r-6]?.hour===24)return '00:00';const serialCol=(c===64||c===53)&&(packet.blocks.find(b=>b.address===(c===64?'BL6:CD29':'AY6:BC29'))?.values||[]).some(row=>{const v=row[c===64?0:2];return typeof v==='number'&&v>0&&v<1;});if(serialCol&&value>=0&&value<=2){const total=Math.round(value*1440);return String(Math.floor(total/60)).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(total%60).padStart(2,'0');}if(Number.isInteger(value)&&value>=0&&value<=28)return String(value).padStart(2,'0')+':00';const minute=Math.round((value-Math.floor(value))*1440)%1440;return String(Math.floor(minute/60)).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(minute%60).padStart(2,'0');}return String(value);}
 function convert(cell,value){const {c}=locate(cell);let text=String(value??'');if([51,55].includes(c)){text=text.trim().replaceAll(',','');if(!text)return '';const n=Number(text);if(!Number.isFinite(n)||n<0)throw Error(cell+'에는 0 이상의 숫자를 입력하세요.');return n;}if(timeColumns.has(c))text=ginjoNormalizeClock(text);if(text.length>4000)throw Error(cell+'의 내용이 너무 깁니다.');return text;}
 function packet(input){
  const out={type:'ginjo-daily-blocks',version:1,date:input.date,capturedAt:input.capturedAt,sourceSheet:input.sourceSheet,blocks:specs.map(s=>({address:s[0],values:Array.from({length:s[3]},()=>Array(s[4]).fill(''))}))};
  if(Array.isArray(input.blocks)){for(const block of out.blocks){const original=input.blocks.find(b=>b.address===block.address||String(b.address).replace(/^([A-Z]+6:[A-Z]+)140$/,'$1180')===block.address);if(!original||!Array.isArray(original.values))throw Error('데일리 필수 범위가 없습니다: '+block.address);for(let r=0;r<block.values.length;r++)for(let c=0;c<block.values[r].length;c++)block.values[r][c]=scalar(original.values[r]?.[c]);}}
  else{
   input=globalThis.GinjoEngine.normalizeDaily(input);
   if(!Array.isArray(input.schedule)||!Array.isArray(input.hourly)||!Array.isArray(input.ot)||input.schedule.length>175||input.ot.length>24)throw Error('데일리를 편집 가능한 범위로 변환하지 못했습니다.');
   input.schedule.forEach((v,i)=>{for(const [c,key]of [[3,'id'],[5,'name'],[7,'shift'],[10,'flag'],[12,'absenceStart'],[13,'absenceEnd'],[18,'reason']])put(out,address(c,6+i),scalar(v[key]));});
   for(const v of input.hourly){const r=v.hour+1;if(r<6||r>29)throw Error('데일리 구간을 확인하세요.');put(out,address(51,r),scalar(v.target));put(out,address(55,r),scalar(v.dailyCount));const b=out.blocks.find(b=>b.address==='AY6:BC29');b.values[r-6][2]=String(v.hour%24).padStart(2,'0')+':00';}
   input.ot.forEach((v,i)=>{put(out,address(64,6+i),String(Math.floor(v.minute/60)%24).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(v.minute%60).padStart(2,'0'));if(v.names.length>15)throw Error('OT 명단은 한 줄에 최대 15명입니다.');v.names.forEach((name,j)=>put(out,address(68+j,6+i),name));});
  }
  return out;
 }
 function validateEdits(value){
  if(!value||value.version!==1||!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value.date??'')||!Array.isArray(value.cells)||value.cells.length>1500)throw Error('수기 데일리 저장 형식을 확인하세요.');
  const seen=new Set(),cells=value.cells.map(item=>{
   if(!item||typeof item!=='object')throw Error('수기 데일리 셀 형식이 잘못되었습니다.');const {c,r}=locate(item.cell);if(!editable(c,r)||seen.has(item.cell))throw Error('수기 데일리 셀 주소가 중복되었거나 허용 범위 밖입니다.');seen.add(item.cell);
   for(const value of [item.before,item.value])if(!['string','number'].includes(typeof value)||(typeof value==='number'&&!Number.isFinite(value))||String(value).length>4000)throw Error('수기 데일리 셀 값의 형식을 확인하세요.');
   return {cell:item.cell,before:item.before,value:item.value};
  });return {version:1,date:value.date,cells};
 }
 function changes(base,draft){const cells=[];for(const s of specs)for(let i=0;i<s[3];i++)for(let j=0;j<s[4];j++){const c=s[2]+j,r=s[1]+i;if(!editable(c,r))continue;const cell=address(c,r),before=get(base,cell),value=get(draft,cell);if(before!==value&&display(base,cell)!==display(draft,cell))cells.push({cell,before,value});}return validateEdits({version:1,date:base.date,cells});}
 function overlay(base,edits,strict=true){
  const saved=validateEdits(edits);if(saved.date!==base.date)throw Error('수기 데일리의 운영일이 원본과 다릅니다.');const out=clone(base),conflicts=[];
  for(const patch of saved.cells){const current=get(base,patch.cell);if(strict&&current!==patch.before&&current!==patch.value)conflicts.push(patch.cell);put(out,patch.cell,patch.value);}
  if(conflicts.length){const error=Error('원본 데일리도 변경된 칸이 있습니다: '+conflicts.slice(0,8).join(', ')+' · 데일리 수기 수정 탭에서 확인 후 다시 반영하세요.');error.details={cells:conflicts};throw error;}return out;
 }
 const validTime=value=>/^(?:[01]?\d|2[0-8]):[0-5]\d$/.test(value);
 function validateDraft(base,draft,engine){
  if(base.date!==draft.date)throw Error('수기 데일리 운영일이 바뀌었습니다.');const edits=changes(base,draft),edited=new Set(edits.cells.map(v=>v.cell)),seen=new Set();
  for(let r=6;r<=180;r++){
   const id=String(get(draft,'C'+r)).trim(),ids=id.match(/\b(?:cssp-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|[Kk](?:[Ee])?\d+)\b/g)??[];
   if(!id){if([5,7,10,12,13,18].some(c=>edited.has(address(c,r))&&String(get(draft,address(c,r))).trim()))throw Error('C'+r+' 사번을 먼저 입력하세요.');continue;}
   if(!ids.length)throw Error('C'+r+' 사번 형식을 확인하세요.');for(const item of ids){const key=item.toLowerCase();if(seen.has(key))throw Error('C'+r+' 사번이 다른 행과 중복됩니다.');seen.add(key);}
   if(edited.has('C'+r)&&!String(get(draft,'E'+r)).trim())throw Error('E'+r+' 이름을 입력하세요.');
   for(const c of [12,13])if(edited.has(address(c,r))&&get(draft,address(c,r))!==''&&!validTime(display(draft,address(c,r))))throw Error(address(c,r)+' 시간을 HH:MM 형식으로 입력하세요.');
   if((edited.has('L'+r)||edited.has('M'+r))&&Boolean(get(draft,'L'+r)!=='')!==Boolean(get(draft,'M'+r)!==''))throw Error('L'+r+'·M'+r+' 부재 시작과 종료를 함께 입력하세요.');
  }
  for(let r=6;r<=29;r++){
   for(const c of [51,55]){const v=get(draft,address(c,r));if(v!==''&&(!Number.isFinite(Number(v))||Number(v)<0))throw Error(address(c,r)+' 숫자를 확인하세요.');}
   if(Array.from({length:15},(_,i)=>get(draft,address(68+i,r))).some(v=>String(v).trim())&&get(draft,'BL'+r)==='')throw Error('BL'+r+' OT 시작 시각을 입력하세요.');
   if(edited.has('BL'+r)&&get(draft,'BL'+r)!==''&&!validTime(display(draft,'BL'+r)))throw Error('BL'+r+' OT 시각을 HH:MM 형식으로 입력하세요.');
  }
  const daily=engine.normalizeDaily(draft);
  for(let r=6;r<=180;r++)if(edited.has('G'+r)&&String(get(draft,'G'+r)).trim()){
   const id=String(get(draft,'C'+r)).match(/\b(?:cssp-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|[Kk](?:[Ee])?\d+)\b/)?.[0];if(!daily.schedule.find(p=>p.id===id)?.window)throw Error('G'+r+' 시프트를 0900-1800처럼 입력하세요.');
  }
  return {daily,edits};
 }
 function parseTSV(text){const rows=[[]];let value='',quoted=false;for(let i=0;i<text.length;i++){const c=text[i];if(c==='"'){if(quoted&&text[i+1]==='"'){value+='"';i++;}else if(quoted||value==='')quoted=!quoted;else value+=c;}else if(!quoted&&(c==='\t'||c==='\r'||c==='\n')){rows.at(-1).push(value);value='';if(c!=='\t'){if(c==='\r'&&text[i+1]==='\n')i++;rows.push([]);}}else value+=c;}if(quoted)throw Error('붙여넣은 표의 따옴표를 확인하세요.');rows.at(-1).push(value);if(rows.length>1&&rows.at(-1).length===1&&rows.at(-1)[0]==='')rows.pop();return rows;}
 function paste(base,start,text,group){const groups={staff:[3,18,180],hours:[51,55,29],ot:[64,82,29]},bounds=groups[group],{c,r}=locate(start),rows=parseTSV(text);if(!bounds||r<6||r+rows.length-1>bounds[2]||c<bounds[0]||rows.some(row=>c+row.length-1>bounds[1]))throw Error('붙여넣기 범위가 표 밖으로 넘어갑니다. 시작 칸과 복사 범위를 확인하세요.');const result=clone(base);rows.forEach((row,i)=>row.forEach((value,j)=>{if(editable(c+j,r+i))put(result,address(c+j,r+i),convert(address(c+j,r+i),value));}));return result;}
 return {specs,clone,letter,column,address,editable,locate,get,put,display,convert,packet,validateEdits,changes,overlay,validateDraft,parseTSV,paste};
})();

globalThis.GinjoShared=(()=>{const text=(value,label,max)=>{if(typeof value!=='string'||value.length>max)throw Error(label+' 형식 또는 길이를 확인하세요.');return value;};
function normalize(input){
 if(!input||input.type!=='ginjo-shared-input'||input.version!==1)throw Error('공유 입력 형식이 아닙니다. HTML의 저장할 내용 복사 버튼으로 만든 내용을 저장하세요.');
 const raw=text(input.raw,'상태 원문',32767);if(!raw.trim()&&!input.dailyEdits&&!(Array.isArray(input.trainees)&&input.trainees.length)&&!(Array.isArray(input.edu)&&input.edu.length)&&!input.eduReport&&!input.ot&&!input.hourLog&&!(Array.isArray(input.adjust)&&input.adjust.length)&&!(Array.isArray(input.exclusions)&&input.exclusions.some(r=>r&&String(r.name||'').trim())))throw Error('공유 입력에 저장할 내용이 없습니다.');
 const calculationAt=text(input.calculationAt,'상태 기준 시각',64);
 if(!Number.isFinite(Date.parse(calculationAt))||!/(Z|[+-]\d{2}:\d{2})$/.test(calculationAt))throw Error('공유 입력의 상태 기준 시각을 확인하세요.');
 const date=text(input.date,'운영일',10);if(!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date))throw Error('공유 입력 운영일을 확인하세요.');
 if(!Array.isArray(input.exclusions)||input.exclusions.length>10)throw Error('공유 제외인원은 최대 10명입니다.');
 const exclusions=input.exclusions.map((row,i)=>{
  if(!row||typeof row!=='object')throw Error('공유 제외인원 '+(i+1)+'번 형식을 확인하세요.');
  const result={name:text(row.name??'','제외 이름',200).trim(),start:ginjoNormalizeClock(text(row.start??'','제외 시작',5)),end:ginjoNormalizeClock(text(row.end??'','제외 종료',5))};
  if(!result.name&&!result.start&&!result.end)return result;
  const valid=v=>/^(?:[01]?\d|2[0-8]):[0-5]\d$/.test(v)||v==='29:00';
  if(!result.name||!valid(result.start)||!valid(result.end)||result.start===result.end)throw Error('공유 제외인원 '+(i+1)+'번의 이름·시간을 확인하세요.');return result;
 });
 const names=(rows,label,max)=>{if(!Array.isArray(rows)||rows.length>max)throw Error(label+' 명단 길이를 확인하세요.');return rows.map(row=>text(row,label,200).trim()).filter(Boolean);};
 const dailyEdits=input.dailyEdits?globalThis.GinjoDailyEditor.validateEdits(input.dailyEdits):null;if(dailyEdits&&dailyEdits.date!==date)throw Error('수기 데일리와 공유 입력의 운영일이 다릅니다.');
 const traineeRows=input.trainees??[];if(!Array.isArray(traineeRows)||traineeRows.length>30)throw Error('교육생 명단은 최대 30명입니다.');
 const trainees=traineeRows.map((row,i)=>{
  if(!row||typeof row!=='object')throw Error('교육생 '+(i+1)+'번 형식을 확인하세요.');
  const name=text(row.name??'','교육생 이름',100).trim(),start=text(row.start??'','교육 시작일',10),end=text(row.end??'','교육 종료일',10);
  if(!name&&!start&&!end)return null;
  if(!name||!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(start)||!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(end)||start>end)throw Error('교육생 '+(i+1)+'번의 이름·기간을 확인하세요.');
  return {name,start,end};
 }).filter(Boolean);
 const eduRows=input.edu??[];if(!Array.isArray(eduRows)||eduRows.length>400)throw Error('이번달 교육 기록은 최대 400건입니다.');
 const edu=eduRows.map((row,i)=>{
  if(!row||typeof row!=='object')throw Error('교육 기록 '+(i+1)+'번 형식을 확인하세요.');
  const name=text(row.name??'','교육 인원 이름',100).trim(),day=text(row.date??'','교육 날짜',10),kind=text(row.kind??'','교육 구분',4),hours=Number(row.hours);
  if(!name||!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(day)||!Number.isFinite(hours)||hours<=0||hours>24||!['','8H','주말','기타'].includes(kind))throw Error('교육 기록 '+(i+1)+'번의 이름·날짜·시간을 확인하세요.');
  return {name,date:day,hours,kind};
 });
 let eduReport=null;if(input.eduReport){const r=input.eduReport;
  if(typeof r!=='object'||!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(String(r.month||''))||!Array.isArray(r.teams)||r.teams.length>20)throw Error('이번달 교육시간 형식을 확인하세요.');
  const kinds={};for(const [k,v]of Object.entries(r.kinds||{}).slice(0,500))if(['8H','주말','기타'].includes(v))kinds[text(k,'교육 구분 사번',60).toLowerCase()]=v;
  const leaders={};for(const [k,v]of Object.entries(r.leaders||{}).slice(0,500))leaders[text(k,'교육 팀장 사번',60).toLowerCase()]=text(v,'담당 팀장',30);
  eduReport={month:r.month,pastedAt:text(r.pastedAt??'','붙여넣은 시각',40),kinds,leaders,teams:r.teams.map((team,i)=>{
   if(!team||!Array.isArray(team.people)||team.people.length>200)throw Error('교육시간 '+(i+1)+'번 팀 형식을 확인하세요.');
   return {team:text(team.team??'','팀 이름',40),people:team.people.map(p=>{const s=Number(p?.seconds),d=p?.days===null||p?.days===undefined?null:Number(p.days);if(!p||!text(p.id??'','교육 사번',60)||!Number.isFinite(s)||s<0||s>31*86400||(d!==null&&!(d>=0&&d<=31)))throw Error('교육시간 값을 확인하세요.');return {id:String(p.id),seconds:s,days:d};})};
  })};}
 let ot=null;if(input.ot){const o=input.ot,clean=m=>{const out={};for(const [k,v]of Object.entries(m||{}).slice(0,600)){const n=Number(v);if(/^\d{1,2}\|[^|]{1,60}$/.test(k)&&Number.isFinite(n))out[k]=n;}return out;};if(typeof o!=='object'||!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(o.date||'')))throw Error('OT 현황 형식을 확인하세요.');ot={date:o.date,seen:clean(o.seen),done:clean(o.done),...(o.man?{man:clean(o.man)}:{})};}
 let hourLog=null;if(input.hourLog){const o=input.hourLog,clean=m=>{const out={};for(const [h,v]of Object.entries(m||{}).slice(0,30)){const n=Number(h);if(!Number.isInteger(n)||n<5||n>28||!Array.isArray(v))continue;const at=Number(v[1]);out[n]=[text(String(v[0]??''),'투입률 공유 문구',300),Number.isFinite(at)?at:0];}return out;};if(typeof o!=='object'||!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(o.date||'')))throw Error('투입률 공유 기록 형식을 확인하세요.');hourLog={date:o.date,auto:clean(o.auto),edit:clean(o.edit)};}
 const adjustRows=input.adjust??[];if(!Array.isArray(adjustRows)||adjustRows.length>20)throw Error('60분 미만 OT는 최대 20건입니다.');
 const adjust=adjustRows.map((row,i)=>{if(!row||typeof row!=='object')throw Error('60분 미만 OT '+(i+1)+'번 형식을 확인하세요.');const name=text(row.name??'','60분 미만 OT 이름',100).trim(),start=text(row.start??'','시작',5),end=text(row.end??'','종료',5);if(!name||!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(start)||!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(end))throw Error('60분 미만 OT '+(i+1)+'번의 이름·시간을 확인하세요.');return {name,start,end};});
 let changes=null;if(input.changes&&typeof input.changes==='object'&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(input.changes.date||''))&&Array.isArray(input.changes.list)){changes={date:input.changes.date,list:input.changes.list.slice(0,150).filter(x=>Array.isArray(x)&&Number.isFinite(Number(x[0]))).map(x=>[Number(x[0]),String(x[1]??'').slice(0,20),String(x[2]??'').slice(0,160)])};}
 let rt=null;{const src=Array.isArray(input.rt)?input.rt:input.rt?[input.rt]:[];const normSnap=sn=>({manual:Boolean(sn.manual),at:Number(sn.at)||0,who:String(sn.who??'').slice(0,20),hash:String(sn.hash??'').slice(0,40),dailyAt:String(sn.dailyAt??'').slice(0,40),warnings:(Array.isArray(sn.warnings)?sn.warnings:[]).slice(0,6).map(x=>String(x).slice(0,600)),hours:sn.hours.slice(0,30).map(h=>({hour:Number(h.hour)||0,present:Number(h.present)||0,mealCount:Number(h.mealCount)||0,delay:(Array.isArray(h.delay)?h.delay:[]).slice(0,20).map(x=>String(x).slice(0,20)),meal:(Array.isArray(h.meal)?h.meal:[]).slice(0,30).map(x=>String(x).slice(0,20))})),shiftCounts:(Array.isArray(sn.shiftCounts)?sn.shiftCounts:[]).slice(0,40).map(x=>({start:Number(x.start)||0,end:Number(x.end)||0,hours:Number(x.hours)||0,count:Number(x.count)||0}))});rt=src.filter(it=>it&&typeof it==='object'&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(it.date||''))&&it.snap&&typeof it.snap==='object'&&Array.isArray(it.snap.hours)).slice(0,4).map(it=>({date:it.date,snap:normSnap(it.snap)}));if(!rt.length)rt=null;}
 let extMap=null;if(Array.isArray(input.extMap)){extMap=input.extMap.slice(0,1500).filter(r=>Array.isArray(r)&&r[0]).map(r=>[String(r[0]).slice(0,30),String(r[1]??'').slice(0,60),Number(r[2])||0,r[3]?1:0]);if(!extMap.length)extMap=null;}
 let extSrc=null;if(input.extSrc&&typeof input.extSrc==='object'&&typeof input.extSrc.text==='string')extSrc={text:input.extSrc.text.slice(0,8000),at:Number(input.extSrc.at)||0};
 let macros=null;if(Array.isArray(input.macros)){macros=input.macros.slice(0,1500).filter(r=>Array.isArray(r)&&r[0]).map(r=>[String(r[0]).slice(0,30),String(r[1]??'').slice(0,20),String(r[2]??'').slice(0,200),Number(r[3])||0,r[4]?1:0,String(r[5]??'').slice(0,20)]);if(!macros.length)macros=null;}
 let handover=null;if(Array.isArray(input.handover)){handover=input.handover.slice(0,200).filter(r=>Array.isArray(r)&&r[0]).map(r=>[String(r[0]).slice(0,30),String(r[1]??'').slice(0,10),String(r[2]??'').slice(0,40),String(r[3]??'').slice(0,10),String(r[4]??'').slice(0,500),String(r[5]??'').slice(0,30),String(r[6]??'').slice(0,200),r[7]?1:0,Number(r[8])||0,r[9]?1:0,Number(r[10])||0]);if(!handover.length)handover=null;} let hoLeaders=null;if(input.hoLeaders&&Array.isArray(input.hoLeaders.list)){hoLeaders={at:Number(input.hoLeaders.at)||0,list:input.hoLeaders.list.slice(0,30).filter(r=>Array.isArray(r)&&r[0]).map(r=>[String(r[0]).slice(0,20),String(r[1]??'').slice(0,20),r[2]?1:0])};if(!hoLeaders.list.length)hoLeaders=null;}
 let roster=null;if(input.roster&&typeof input.roster==='object'&&Array.isArray(input.roster.rows)){roster={at:Number(input.roster.at)||0,rows:input.roster.rows.slice(0,300).filter(r=>Array.isArray(r)).map(r=>[0,1,2,3,4,5].map(i=>String(r[i]??'').slice(0,30)).concat([String(r[6]??'').slice(0,20)]))};}
 let devLog=null;if(input.devLog&&typeof input.devLog==='object'&&Array.isArray(input.devLog.rows)){devLog={clr:Number(input.devLog.clr)||0,rows:input.devLog.rows.slice(0,80).filter(r=>Array.isArray(r)&&r[0]).map(r=>[String(r[0]).slice(0,24),Number(r[1])||0,String(r[2]??'').slice(0,12),r[3]==='share'?'share':'error',String(r[4]??'').slice(0,12),String(r[5]??'').slice(0,40),String(r[6]??'').slice(0,160)])};}
 let rtRules=null;if(input.rtRules&&typeof input.rtRules==='object'&&Array.isArray(input.rtRules.rows)){rtRules={at:Number(input.rtRules.at)||0,rows:input.rtRules.rows.slice(0,40).map(r=>(Array.isArray(r)?r:[]).slice(0,6).map(x=>String(x??'').slice(0,60))),preg:(Array.isArray(input.rtRules.preg)?input.rtRules.preg:[]).slice(0,20).map(x=>String(x).slice(0,20)),list:(Array.isArray(input.rtRules.list)?input.rtRules.list:[]).slice(0,40).map(r=>(Array.isArray(r)?r:[]).slice(0,3).map(x=>String(x??'').slice(0,10)))};}
 let stamps=null;if(input.stamps&&typeof input.stamps==='object'){const m=(o,lim)=>{const out={};for(const [k,v]of Object.entries(o||{}).slice(0,lim)){if(typeof k==='string'&&k.length<=140&&Array.isArray(v)&&Number.isFinite(Number(v[0])))out[k]=[Number(v[0]),v[1]?1:0];}return out;};const at={};for(const f of ['raw','training','exceptions','eduReport']){const n=Number(input.stamps.at?.[f]);if(Number.isFinite(n))at[f]=n;}stamps={ex:m(input.stamps.ex,200),tr:m(input.stamps.tr,200),aj:m(input.stamps.aj,200),at};}
 return {type:'ginjo-shared-input',version:1,date,calculationAt,raw,exclusions,training:names(input.training,'교육',26),exceptions:names(input.exceptions,'일반 예외',10),trainees,edu,...(eduReport?{eduReport}:{}),...(ot?{ot}:{}),...(hourLog?{hourLog}:{}),...(stamps?{stamps}:{}),...(changes?{changes}:{}),...(rt?{rt}:{}),...(rtRules?{rtRules}:{}),...(macros?{macros}:{}),...(handover?{handover}:{}),...(hoLeaders?{hoLeaders}:{}),...(roster?{roster}:{}),...(devLog?{devLog}:{}),...(extMap?{extMap}:{}),...(extSrc?{extSrc}:{}),...(adjust.length?{adjust}:{}),...(dailyEdits?{dailyEdits}:{})};
}
function parseSharedPayload(value){
 if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return null;
 let input=value;if(typeof value==='string'){
  if(value.length>60000)throw Error('공유 입력이 너무 큽니다. 저장 내용을 확인하세요.');
  try{input=JSON.parse(value.replace(/^\uFEFF/,''));}catch{throw Error('공유 저장 내용이 손상되었습니다. 여러 줄 텍스트의 일반 텍스트 칸에 전체 내용을 저장하세요.');}
 }
 return normalize(input);
}
function makeSharedPayload(input){
 const result=normalize({...input,type:'ginjo-shared-input',version:1});let json=JSON.stringify(result);if(json.length>60000&&result.devLog){const r0=JSON.parse(json);delete r0.devLog;json=JSON.stringify(r0);}if(json.length>60000&&result.roster){const r2={...result};delete r2.roster;json=JSON.stringify(r2);try{globalThis.__roDropped=Date.now();}catch{}}if(json.length>60000&&(result.handover||result.hoLeaders)){const r3=JSON.parse(json);delete r3.handover;delete r3.hoLeaders;json=JSON.stringify(r3);}
 if(json.length>60000&&result.macros){const r4=JSON.parse(json);delete r4.macros;json=JSON.stringify(r4);try{globalThis.__mcDropped=Date.now();}catch{}}
 if(json.length>60000)throw Error('저장할 내용이 60,000자를 넘습니다. 원문 또는 수기 수정 범위를 줄여주세요.');return json;
}
function sharedFormURL(value){
 if(!value)return '';const url=new URL(value);
 if(url.protocol!=='https:'||!url.hostname.endsWith('.sharepoint.com')||url.username||url.password)throw Error('공유 입력창 주소는 HTTPS SharePoint 주소여야 합니다.');
 url.pathname=url.pathname.replace(/\/DispForm\.aspx$/i,'/EditForm.aspx');
 if(url.searchParams.get('PageType')==='4')url.searchParams.set('PageType','6');return url.href;
}
function readSharedRow(row){
 if(!row||typeof row!=='object')throw Error('공유 항목을 불러오지 못했습니다. Power Automate의 항목 가져오기를 확인하세요.');
 let input=null,payloadColumn='Payload';
 if(row.Payload!==null&&row.Payload!==undefined&&row.Payload!=='')input=parseSharedPayload(row.Payload);
 else{
  const candidates=[];let malformed=false;
  for(const [column,value]of Object.entries(row)){
   if(column==='Payload')continue;
   if(typeof value==='string'&&value.includes('ginjo-shared-input')){
    let decoded;try{decoded=JSON.parse(value.replace(/^\uFEFF/,''));}catch{if(value.trim().startsWith('{')||value.trim().startsWith('<'))malformed=true;continue;}
    if(decoded?.type==='ginjo-shared-input')candidates.push({column,input:parseSharedPayload(decoded)});
   }else if(value&&typeof value==='object'&&value.type==='ginjo-shared-input')candidates.push({column,input:parseSharedPayload(value)});
  }
  if(candidates.length>1)throw Error('공유 저장 내용이 여러 열에서 발견되었습니다. 현재 사용할 저장 내용 한 개만 남겨주세요.');
  if(candidates.length===1){input=candidates[0].input;payloadColumn=candidates[0].column;}
  else if(malformed)throw Error('공유 저장 내용을 읽지 못했습니다. 여러 줄 일반 텍스트 칸에 저장할 내용 전체를 붙여넣었는지 확인하세요.');
  else if(!Object.hasOwn(row,'Payload')&&(!Number.isInteger(Number(row.ID))||Number(row.ID)<=0))throw Error('공유 목록의 저장 열을 찾지 못했습니다. 항목 가져오기의 출력과 저장 내용을 확인하세요.');
 }
 return {input,payloadColumn,modified:typeof row.Modified==='string'?row.Modified:'',editor:typeof row.Editor?.DisplayName==='string'?row.Editor.DisplayName:'',formURL:sharedFormURL(row['{Link}']??'')};
}

return {parseSharedPayload,makeSharedPayload,sharedFormURL,readSharedRow};})();
function ginjoCurrentBoundaryHint(metric){
 if(!metric)return '';
 const low=Number(metric.untilLow),high=Number(metric.untilHigh);
 if(!Number.isFinite(low)||!Number.isFinite(high)||low<=0||high<=0)return '';
 const minutes=Math.min(low,high);
 if(minutes>=15)return '';
 return low<=high?minutes+'분 더 비면 미달':minutes+'분 더하면 과투입';
}
function ginjoCurrentStatusHint(c){
 if(!c)return '';
 const n=v=>Math.max(0,Math.ceil(Number(v)||0));
 if(c.status==='미달')return '<div class="mh-main bad">미달 · '+n(c.add)+'분 부족</div>';
 if(c.status==='과투입')return '<div class="mh-main bad">과투입 · '+n(c.remove)+'분 초과</div>';
 const low=Number(c.untilLow),high=Number(c.untilHigh);
 if(!Number.isFinite(low)||!Number.isFinite(high))return '';
 return '<div class="mh-main '+(low<15?'warn':'ok')+'">정상 · '+low+'분 빠지면 미달</div><div class="mh-sub">'+high+'분 더하면 과투입</div>';
}

 if(data.type==='ginjo-config'){
  for(const field of ['dailyUrl','statusUrl'])if(data[field]){const url=new URL(data[field]);if(url.protocol!=='https:'||!url.hostname.endsWith('.sharepoint.com'))throw Error('회사 연결 주소는 HTTPS SharePoint 주소만 사용할 수 있습니다.');APP_CONFIG[field]=url.href;}

 }

 if(MODE==='web'&&['ginjo-daily','ginjo-daily-blocks'].includes(data.type)){

 }
 throw Error(MODE==='web'?'웹 계산형에는 데일리 JSON을 불러오세요.':'엑셀 연결형에는 상태출력 결과 JSON을 불러오세요.');
}

}
