function render(){
 const result=state.result,current=result?.current,next=result?.next;
  const boundaryHint=ginjoCurrentStatusHint(current);$('current-minute-hint').innerHTML=boundaryHint;$('current-minute-hint').classList.toggle('hidden',!boundaryHint);
 $('current-rate').innerHTML=(current?percentage(current.rate):'—')+'<small>%</small>';
 $('current-band').textContent=current?`허용 ${format(current.low*100,0)}~${format(current.high*100,0)}% · ${format(current.lower,0)}~${format(Math.floor(current.upper),0)}분`:'';
 const plan=result?.plan;$('plan-rate').innerHTML=(plan?percentage(plan.rate):'—')+'<small>%</small>';
 const bc=Number(current?.dailyCount);$('plan-count').textContent=plan?`와야 할 인원 ${format(plan.count,0)}명`+(Number.isFinite(bc)?` (데일리 ${format(bc,0)}명)`:'')+` · ${format(plan.minutes,0)}분`:'—';$('plan-count').classList.toggle('mismatch',Boolean(plan&&Number.isFinite(bc)&&Math.round(bc)!==plan.count));
 $('real-count').textContent=plan?`지금 출근 ${format(plan.actualCount,0)}명 · ${format(current.minutes,0)}분`:'—';
 const diffN=plan?plan.actualCount-plan.count:0;$('real-count').classList.toggle('mismatch',Boolean(plan&&diffN!==0));if(plan&&diffN!==0)$('real-count').textContent+=` (${diffN>0?'+':''}${diffN}명)`;$('current-hour').textContent=result?`${result.hour}~${result.hour+1}시`:'—';
 $('current-detail').textContent=current?`인정 ${format(current.minutes)}분 / 목표 ${format(current.target)}명 · 로그인 ${format(current.login,0)}명`:'A1 상태 원문과 데일리 데이터가 필요합니다.';
 $('current-action').textContent=current?E.actionText(current):'계산 결과가 없습니다.';$('current-action').classList.toggle('bad',Boolean(current&&(current.add||current.remove)));
 $('gauge-band').style.left=current?Math.min(100,current.low/1.5*100)+'%':'0';$('gauge-band').style.width=current?Math.max(0,Math.min(100,current.high/1.5*100)-Math.min(100,current.low/1.5*100))+'%':'0';$('gauge-actual').style.width=current?Math.min(100,current.rate/1.5*100)+'%':'0';$('gauge-actual').style.background=current&&(current.add||current.remove)?'#c88742':'#3b8569';
 $('next-rate').innerHTML=(next?percentage(next.rate):'—')+'<small>%</small>';$('next-hour').textContent=next?`${next.hour}~${next.hour+1}시`:result?.hour===28?'새 운영일':'—';
 $('next-detail').textContent=next?`예정 ${format(next.minutes)}분 / 목표 ${format(next.target)}명 · OT ${format(next.otPeople??0,0)}명 ${format(next.otMinutes??0)}분 포함`:'다음 운영일 근무표를 확인하세요.';
 $('next-action').textContent=next?E.actionText(next):'—';$('next-action').classList.toggle('bad',Boolean(next&&(next.add||next.remove)));
 const [lower,upper]=E.forecastText(next);$('lower-boundary').textContent=lower;$('upper-boundary').textContent=upper;$('band-detail').textContent=next?`허용 ${format(next.low*100)}~${format(next.high*100)}% · 1분 단위로 표시`:'허용률 경계와 같으면 정상입니다.';
 const dailyAt=state.daily?.capturedAt,hasDailyTime=Number.isFinite(Date.parse(dailyAt??''));
 const dailyLabel=state.daily?' · 마지막 데일리 가져옴 '+(hasDailyTime?new Date(dailyAt).toLocaleString('ko-KR',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',second:'2-digit',hour12:false}):'생성 시각 없음'):'';
 $('asof').textContent=(result?`운영일 ${result.date} · ${state.statusFromShared?'저장된 상태 기준':'상태 기준'} ${new Date(Date.parse(result.at)+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16)}`:state.daily?`데일리 운영일 ${state.daily.date} · ${$('raw').value.trim()?'계산 대기':'상태 원문 입력 대기'}`:'운영일과 상태 기준 시각을 확인하세요.')+dailyLabel+(state.manualDailyEdits?.cells.length?' · 수기 데일리 '+state.manualDailyEdits.cells.length+'칸 적용':'');
 $('status-strip').innerHTML=['대화 가능','회의','휴식','오프라인'].map(status=>`<div class="status-item">${status==='회의'?'회의중':status}<b>${result?result.people.filter(p=>p.status===status).length:'—'}</b></div>`).join('');
 if($('count-people'))$('count-people').textContent=result?.people?.length??'—';
 $('footer-source').textContent=state.demo?'샘플 · 회사 데이터 연결 전':state.daily?`데일리: ${state.daily.sourceSheet??state.daily.date} · 마지막 읽기 ${new Date(state.lastRead).toLocaleTimeString('ko-KR')}`:result?`엑셀 상태 기준 ${new Date(result.at).toLocaleString('ko-KR',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul'})}`:'회사 파일 연결 대기';
 dailyStatus();renderAutomaticExclusions();if(state.view==='daily')renderDailyEditor();if(state.view==='rt')setTimeout(()=>{try{renderRT();}catch{}},0);renderOverview();updateBadge();renderTable();renderTodayOver();updateTitleAndStale();try{renderHourLog();}catch(error){}try{renderSharedSrc();}catch(error){}try{recordOTSeen();renderTraineeSoon();if(state.view==='ot')renderOT();if(slotAlert)checkSlotAlert();}catch(error){}if(state.view==='forecast')renderForecast();if(state.view==='edu')renderEdu();
}
function overviewGroups(result,search=''){
 const people=result?.people??[],query=search.trim().toLowerCase();
 const matches=row=>!query||[row.name,row.id,row.status,...(row.reasons??[]),...(row.tags??[])].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(query);
 const seconds=value=>{const t=String(value??'').match(/(\d+):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);return !t?0:t[3]!==undefined?Number(t[1])*3600+Number(t[2])*60+Number(t[3]):Number(t[1])*60+Number(t[2]);};
 const groups={};for(const status of ['자리에 없음','교육','식사']){const all=people.filter(p=>p.status===status).sort((a,b)=>seconds(b.duration)-seconds(a.duration)||String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name),'ko'));groups[status]={total:all.length,rows:all.filter(matches)};}
 const byId=new Map(people.map(p=>[String(p.idKey??p.id).toLowerCase(),p])),checks=new Map();let globalIndex=0;
 function add(row,kind,reason,rank){
  const id=String(row.idKey??row.id??'').toLowerCase(),key=id||'global-'+globalIndex++,person=byId.get(id)??{};
  const previous=checks.get(key)??{...person,id:row.id||person.id||'',name:row.name||person.name||'자료 확인',shift:row.shift||person.shift||'',status:row.status||person.status||'—',tags:[],reasons:[],rank};
  if(!previous.tags.includes(kind))previous.tags.push(kind);if(reason&&!previous.reasons.includes(reason))previous.reasons.push(reason);previous.rank=Math.min(previous.rank,rank);checks.set(key,previous);
 }
 for(const row of result?.missing??[])add(row,'지각 확인',row.ot?'OT 예정 인원 · 현재 상태와 출근 여부 확인':row.reason||'근무 예정 인원 · 현재 상태와 출근 여부 확인',0);
 for(const row of result?.off??[])add(row,'근무 확인',row.half?'반차 구간 로그인 확인':row.reason||'근무 예정 구간 외 로그인 · OT 반영 확인',1);
 for(const row of result?.candidates??[])add(row,'인원 차이 후보',row.reason||'데일리 인원과 로그인 인원의 차이 확인',2);
 for(const row of result?.diagnostics??[])add(row,'자료 확인',row.message||row.kind||'데일리 자료 확인',3);
 const allChecks=[...checks.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.rank-b.rank||String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name),'ko'));
 return {groups,checks:allChecks.filter(matches),checkTotal:allChecks.length};
}
function renderAutomaticExclusions(){
 const data=state.daily?E.automaticExclusions(state.daily):{rows:[],issues:[]};
 $('auto-exclusion-count').textContent=state.daily?new Set(data.rows.map(row=>row.idKey)).size+'명':'—';
 {const g=new Map();for(const row of data.rows){const k=row.idKey||row.id;if(!g.has(k))g.set(k,{name:row.name,id:row.id,times:[],src:[]});const x=g.get(k);x.times.push(row.start.replace(':','')+'~'+row.end.replace(':',''));x.src.push('L'+row.sourceRow+'~M'+row.sourceRow);}
 $('auto-exclusion-body').innerHTML=g.size?[...g.values()].map(x=>`<div class="issue-item auto" title="${esc(x.id+' · '+x.src.join(', '))}"><div><b>${esc(x.name)}</b> <span>${esc(x.times.join(', '))}</span></div></div>`).join(''):'<div class="issue-empty">없음</div>';}
 $('auto-exclusion-note').textContent=data.issues.length?data.issues.map(row=>row.message).join(' / '):'';$('auto-exclusion-note').hidden=!data.issues.length;
 $('auto-exclusion-note').classList.toggle('auto-exclusion-warning',data.issues.length>0);
 const ex=[...(data.extra||[]),...(data.anon||[])].sort((a,b)=>a.startMinute-b.startMinute),c=state.result?E.businessClock(state.result.at):null,now=c?c.hour*60+c.minute:-1;
 if($('extra-ex-count')){$('extra-ex-count').textContent=state.daily?ex.length+'건 · '+ex.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.minutes,0)+'분':'—';
  $('extra-ex-body').innerHTML=ex.length?ex.map(r=>{const active=now>=0&&[now,now-1440,now+1440].some(m=>m>=r.startMinute&&m<r.endMinute);return `<tr class="${active?'now':''}"><td><b>${esc(r.anon?'-':r.name)}</b><small>${r.anon?'사번 없음 · '+r.sourceRow+'행':esc(r.id)}</small></td><td>${esc(r.reason||'—')}</td><td>${esc(r.start.replace(':',''))}</td><td>${esc(r.end.replace(':',''))}</td><td>${r.minutes}분${active?' <span class="pill amber">지금</span>':''}</td></tr>`;}).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="5" class="auto-exclusion-empty">'+(state.daily?'금일 추가 제외시간 없음':'데일리 연결 후 자동 표시')+'</td></tr>';}
}
function renderOverview(){
 const result=leaderFiltered(state.result),overview=overviewGroups(result,state.search),query=state.search.trim().toLowerCase();
 const hit=row=>!query||[row.name,row.id,row.status,row.shift,row.message,row.reason].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(query);
 const secs=value=>{const m=String(value??'').match(/(\d+):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);return !m?0:m[3]!==undefined?Number(m[1])*3600+Number(m[2])*60+Number(m[3]):Number(m[1])*60+Number(m[2]);};
 const shiftCell=row=>{let s=row.shift;if(!s&&state.daily){const k=String(row.idKey??row.id).toLowerCase(),r=(state.daily.schedule||[]).find(x=>String(x.idKey??x.id).toLowerCase()===k);s=r?.shift||'';}return `<td class="shift-cell">${esc(s||'—')}</td>`;};
 const person=(row,noOT)=>`<td title="${esc((row.shift||'')+' · '+row.id)}"><b>${esc(row.name||row.id)}</b>${row.ot&&!noOT?'<span class="pill ot mini">OT</span>':''}<small>${esc(row.id)}</small></td>`;
 const none=!result?'원문 입력 후 계산하세요.':state.search?'검색 결과가 없습니다.':'해당 인원 없음';
 const empty=(cols,text)=>`<tr><td colspan="${cols}" class="compact-empty">${text}</td></tr>`;
 const away=overview.groups['자리에 없음'];
 $('priority-away-count').textContent=!result?'—':state.search?away.rows.length+' / '+away.total:String(away.total);
 $('priority-away-body').innerHTML=away.rows.length?away.rows.map(row=>`<tr>${person(row)}${shiftCell(row)}<td class="elapsed">${esc(row.duration||'—')}</td></tr>`).join(''):empty(3,none);
 const edu=overview.groups['교육'],meal=overview.groups['식사'],restAll=(result?.people??[]).filter(p=>p.status==='휴식'&&secs(p.duration)>=600),rest=restAll.filter(hit);
 const isLong=r=>r.kind==='meal'?secs(r.duration)>=3600:r.kind==='rest';
 const mixed=[...edu.rows.map(r=>({...r,kind:'training'})),...meal.rows.map(r=>({...r,kind:'meal'})),...rest.map(r=>({...r,kind:'rest'}))].map(r=>({...r,long:isLong(r)})).sort((a,b)=>Number(b.long)-Number(a.long)||secs(b.duration)-secs(a.duration)||String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name),'ko'));
 const mixedTotal=edu.total+meal.total+restAll.length;
 $('priority-training-count').textContent=!result?'—':state.search?mixed.length+' / '+mixedTotal:String(mixedTotal);
 $('priority-training-body').innerHTML=mixed.length?mixed.map(row=>`<tr class="row-${row.kind}${row.long?' long':''}">${person(row)}${shiftCell(row)}<td><span class="pill ${row.kind==='training'?'purple':row.kind==='meal'?'meal':'rest'}">${row.kind==='training'?'교육':row.kind==='meal'?(row.long?'식사 60분+':'식사'):'휴식 10분+'}</span></td><td class="elapsed">${esc(row.duration||'—')}</td></tr>`).join(''):empty(4,none);
 const people=result?.people??[],otAll=[];const seenOT=new Set();
 for(const p of people)if(p.ot){otAll.push({...p,absent:false});seenOT.add(String(p.idKey??p.id).toLowerCase());}
 for(const m of result?.missing??[])if(m.ot&&!seenOT.has(String(m.idKey??m.id).toLowerCase()))otAll.push({...m,absent:true});
 const otRows=otAll.filter(hit).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.absent)-Number(b.absent)||String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name),'ko'));
 $('priority-meal-count').textContent=!result?'—':state.search?otRows.length+' / '+otAll.length:String(otAll.length);
 $('priority-meal-body').innerHTML=otRows.length?otRows.map(row=>`<tr class="row-ot">${person(row,true)}${shiftCell(row)}<td><span class="pill ${row.absent?'red':statusClass(row.status)}">${esc(row.absent?'미출근':row.status||'원문 없음')}</span></td></tr>`).join(''):empty(3,!result?'원문 입력 후 계산하세요.':state.search?'검색 결과가 없습니다.':'지금 OT 인원 없음');
 $('priority-ot-foot').textContent=!result?'—':result.next?`다음 시간(${result.next.hour}~${result.next.hour+1}시) OT ${format(result.next.otPeople??0,0)}명 · ${format(result.next.otMinutes??0)}분`:'다음 시간 정보 없음';
 const late=(result?.missing??[]).filter(hit);
 const extraAll=result?.off??[],extra=extraAll.filter(hit),hiddenOther=0;
 $('late-count').textContent=!result?'—':String(late.length);$('extra-count').textContent=!result?'—':String(extra.length);
 $('work-check-count').textContent=!result?'—':String(late.length+extra.length);
 $('work-check-summary').textContent=!result?'원문 입력 후 지각·근무·인원 차이를 함께 확인합니다.':`데일리(F5) ${format(result.current.dailyCount,0)}명 · 로그인(G5) ${format(result.current.login,0)}명`+(hiddenOther>0?` · 그 외 상태 로그인 ${hiddenOther}명은 숨김`:'');
 const none2=!result?'계산하면 확인할 인원을 표시합니다.':state.search?'검색 결과가 없습니다.':'확인 대상 없음';
 try{renderRtDuty();}catch{}
 try{checkUrgent(Boolean(result)&&(late.length+extra.length)>0);}catch{}
 $('late-body').innerHTML=late.length?late.map(row=>`<tr>${person(row)}<td>${esc(row.shift||'—')}</td><td><span class="pill ${statusClass(row.status)}">${esc(row.status||'원문 없음')}</span></td><td class="elapsed">${esc(row.duration||'—')}</td></tr>`).join(''):empty(4,none2);
 $('extra-body').innerHTML=extra.length?extra.map(row=>`<tr>${person(row)}<td>${esc(row.shift||'—')}</td><td><span class="pill ${statusClass(row.status)}">${esc(row.status||'원문 없음')}</span></td><td class="check-reason">${esc(row.half?'반차 구간 로그인 확인':row.reason||'근무 예정 구간 외 로그인 · OT 반영 확인')}</td></tr>`).join(''):empty(4,none2);
 const diag=(result?.diagnostics??[]).filter(hit);$('data-check').hidden=!diag.length;$('data-check-count').textContent=String(diag.length);
 $('data-check-body').innerHTML=diag.map(row=>`<tr><td><span class="pill amber">${esc(row.kind||'자료 확인')}</span></td><td class="check-person"><b>${esc(row.name||'—')}</b><small>${esc(row.id||'')}</small></td><td class="check-reason">${esc(row.message||'')}</td></tr>`).join('');
 const history=[...state.journal.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.date.localeCompare(a.date)||b.hour-a.hour);
 $('overview-history-body').innerHTML=history.length?history.map(row=>`<tr><td>${esc(row.date)}</td><td>${row.hour}~${row.hour+1}시</td><td>${percentage(row.rate)}%</td><td>${format(row.minutes)}분</td><td>${esc(row.action)}</td></tr>`).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="5" class="compact-empty">아직 계산한 이력이 없습니다.</td></tr>';
 renderIssues();
}

function getRows(){const r=state.result;if(!r)return [];const rows=state.view==='history'?[...state.journal.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date)||a.hour-b.hour):r[state.view]??r.people;return rows.filter(row=>{if(state.filter!=='전체'&&row.status!==state.filter)return false;const hay=[row.name,row.id,row.kind,row.status,row.reason,row.message].join(' ').toLowerCase();return !state.search||hay.includes(state.search);});}
function renderTable(){
 if(['settings','daily','issues','forecast','edu','ot','trainee'].includes(state.view))return;const titles={people:'전체 상태 명단',missing:'지각 확인필요',off:'근무 외 로그인',candidates:'F5 · G5 차이의 우선 확인 후보',diagnostics:'데일리 · 원문 점검',history:'이번 화면의 시간대 이력'};
 $('table-title').textContent=titles[state.view];const r=state.result,rows=getRows();$('filters').innerHTML=state.view==='people'?['전체','대화 가능','회의','자리에 없음','교육','식사','휴식','오프라인'].map(s=>`<button data-filter="${s}" class="${state.filter===s?'active':''}">${s}</button>`).join(''):'';
 $('table-summary').textContent=!r?'자료를 불러오고 원문을 입력하면 명단이 표시됩니다.':state.view==='candidates'?`데일리(F5) ${format(r.current.dailyCount,0)}명 / 로그인(G5) ${format(r.current.login,0)}명 · 차이 방향이 맞는 후보 ${rows.length}명`:state.view==='diagnostics'?`확인 항목 ${rows.length}건 · 문제를 임의로 정상 처리하지 않습니다.`:`${rows.length}명 표시 · 운영일 ${r.date}`;
 let heads,body;
 if(state.view==='diagnostics'){heads=['구분','이름 / 사번','확인 내용'];body=rows.map(row=>`<tr><td><span class="pill amber">${esc(row.kind)}</span></td><td class="person">${esc(row.name||'—')}<span class="id">${esc(row.id)}</span></td><td class="reason">${esc(row.message)}</td></tr>`);}
 else if(state.view==='candidates'){heads=['순서','이름 / 사번','현재 상태','차이','확인 이유'];body=rows.map((row,i)=>`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td class="person">${esc(row.name)}<span class="id">${esc(row.id)}</span></td><td><span class="pill ${statusClass(row.status)}">${esc(row.status)}</span></td><td class="credit">${row.effect>0?'+':''}${format(row.effect,0)}명</td><td class="reason">${esc(row.reason)}</td></tr>`);}
 else if(state.view==='history'){heads=['운영일','구간','투입률','인정분','지각 확인','조치'];body=rows.map(row=>`<tr><td>${esc(row.date)}</td><td>${row.hour}~${row.hour+1}시</td><td>${percentage(row.rate)}%</td><td>${format(row.minutes)}분</td><td>${format(row.missing,0)}명</td><td class="reason">${esc(row.action)}</td></tr>`);}
 else{heads=['이름 / 사번','시프트','현재 상태',state.view==='people'?'인정 근무분':'확인 사항'];body=rows.map(row=>`<tr><td class="person">${esc(row.name||'이름 확인')}<span class="id">${esc(row.id)}</span></td><td>${esc(row.shift||'—')}</td><td><span class="pill ${statusClass(row.status)}">${esc(row.status||'원문 없음')}</span></td><td>${state.view==='people'?`<span class="credit">${format(row.minutes??0)}분</span>${row.restored?'<span class="id">회의 이중차감 방지</span>':row.excluded?`<span class="id">등록 제외 ${format(row.excluded)}분</span>`:''}`:esc(row.ot?'OT · 지각 확인필요':row.reason||row.duration||'근무구분 확인')}</td></tr>`);}
 $('table-head').innerHTML='<tr>'+heads.map(h=>'<th>'+h+'</th>').join('')+'</tr>';$('table-body').innerHTML=body.join('');$('empty').classList.toggle('hidden',rows.length>0);
 $('table-note').textContent=state.view==='candidates'?'근무표·현재 상태로 추정한 후보입니다. 데일리의 OT·수기 예외 반영 여부에 따라 실제 원인과 다를 수 있습니다.':state.view==='history'?'브라우저를 닫으면 이 화면의 이력은 사라집니다. 필요한 결과는 내보내기로 보관하세요.':'1명 = 60분. 신입 교육 제외·반차·OT·등록 제외를 반영하며, 제외 시간의 회의는 이중 차감하지 않습니다.';
}
async function loadFile(file){try{if(!file)return;const data=B.decodeJSON(await file.text());receive(data,false);toast('JSON 파일을 불러왔습니다.');}catch(error){fail(error);}finally{$('file-input').value='';}}
for(let i=0;i<10;i++)$('exclusion-rows').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<tr><td><input data-col="name" aria-label="제외 ${i+1} 이름 또는 사번" placeholder="${i+1}. 이름 / 사번" autocomplete="off"></td><td><input class="time" data-col="start" aria-label="제외 ${i+1} 시작" placeholder="1300" maxlength="5" inputmode="numeric"></td><td><input class="time" data-col="end" aria-label="제외 ${i+1} 종료" placeholder="1330" maxlength="5" inputmode="numeric"></td></tr>`);
document.querySelectorAll('textarea,input').forEach(input=>input.addEventListener('input',()=>{if(input.closest&&input.closest('#log-list'))return;if(['edu-raw','search','shared-copy-text','shared-form-config','report-text','pv-name','pv-start','pv-end','manual-name','manual-start','manual-end','adj-name','adj-start','adj-end'].includes(input.id)||input.id.startsWith('daily-'))return;state.dirty=true;if(input.id==='raw'){state.rawSample=false;useCurrentStatusTime();state.result=null;render();}if(['date','time'].includes(input.id)){state.statusFromShared=false;$('use-now').checked=false;}if(APP_CONFIG.sharedEnabled)sharedStatus('입력을 변경했습니다. 아직 공유 저장하지 않았습니다.');}));
$('shared-save-jump').addEventListener('click',()=>$('shared-panel').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'}));
$('nav').addEventListener('click',event=>{const button=event.target.closest('[data-view]');if(button)setView(button.dataset.view);});$('nav2').addEventListener('click',event=>{const button=event.target.closest('[data-view]');if(button)setView(button.dataset.view);});$('filters').addEventListener('click',event=>{const button=event.target.closest('[data-filter]');if(button){state.filter=button.dataset.filter;renderTable();}});
$('search').addEventListener('input',()=>{state.search=$('search').value.toLowerCase().trim();renderOverview();renderTable();});$('run-btn').addEventListener('click',run);$('sample-btn')?.addEventListener('click',sample);$('setup-btn').addEventListener('click',()=>setView('settings'));
$('paste-btn').addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{const raw=await navigator.clipboard.readText();if(!raw.trim())throw Error('empty clipboard');$('raw').value=raw;state.rawSample=false;state.dirty=true;useCurrentStatusTime();state.result=null;render();if(APP_CONFIG.sharedEnabled)sharedStatus('새 원문을 현재 시각 기준으로 넣었습니다. 아직 공유 저장하지 않았습니다.');toast('원문을 붙여넣고 기준 시각을 현재로 바꿨습니다.');}catch{toast('원문 칸을 클릭하고 Ctrl+V로 붙여넣으세요.');$('raw').focus();}});
$('export-btn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!state.result){toast('먼저 계산 결과를 준비하세요.');return;}const r=state.result;download(`${APP_CONFIG.name}_${r.date}_${r.hour}시_${state.demo?'샘플':'결과'}.json`,JSON.stringify(r,null,2));});
document.querySelectorAll('[data-code]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>copy(RESOURCES[button.dataset.code])));

function sharedStatus(message){if($('shared-status'))$('shared-status').textContent=message;}
function setSharedLink(value){
 const url=S.sharedFormURL(value);
 for(const id of ['shared-open-link','shared-dialog-link'])if($(id)){if(url)$(id).href=url;else $(id).removeAttribute('href');}
 if($('shared-form-address'))$('shared-form-address').textContent=url||'공유 목록의 현재 입력 항목을 회사 SharePoint에서 여세요.';
}
function restoreShared(data){
 if(!APP_CONFIG.sharedEnabled)return;
 $('shared-panel').classList.remove('hidden');
 setSharedLink(APP_CONFIG.sharedFormURL??'');
 if(!Object.hasOwn(data,'sharedInput'))throw Error('공유 항목이 HTML에 포함되지 않았습니다. 새 작성 식에 항목 가져오기 출력이 포함되어 있는지 확인하세요.');
 const saved=S.readSharedRow(data.sharedInput);if(saved.formURL)setSharedLink(saved.formURL);
 if(!saved.input){sharedStatus('아직 공유 저장된 입력이 없습니다. 원문을 입력·계산한 뒤 저장할 내용을 복사해 공유 저장창에 저장하세요.');return;}
 const input=saved.input;state.statusFromShared=true;
 if(E.businessClock(input.calculationAt).date!==input.date)throw Error('공유 입력의 운영일과 상태 기준 시각이 일치하지 않습니다.');
 if(input.date!==state.daily.date){
  setTraineesUI(input.trainees||[],true);setEduEntries(input.edu||[]);setEduReport(input.eduReport||null);state.statusFromShared=false;state.inputDay=state.daily.date;state.result=null;
  sharedStatus('운영일이 바뀌어(저장 '+input.date+' → 데일리 '+state.daily.date+') 상태 원문·수기 추가·예외 입력은 초기화되었습니다. 신입 교육 기간과 교육 인원 기록은 유지됩니다.');
  return;
 }
 $('raw').value=input.raw;state.rawSample=false;setFields(input.exclusions);$('training').value=input.training.join('\n');$('exceptions').value=input.exceptions.join('\n');fillTime(input.calculationAt);$('use-now').checked=false;setTraineesUI(input.trainees||[],true);setEduEntries(input.edu||[]);setEduReport(input.eduReport||null);if(input.ot&&input.ot.date===input.date)mergeOT(input.ot.date,input.ot);if(input.hourLog&&input.hourLog.date===state.daily.date)mergeHourLog(input.hourLog);if(input.changes)try{mergeChanges(input.changes);}catch{}try{mergeRT(input);}catch{}try{if(input.macros)mergeMacros(input.macros);}catch{}try{mergeExt(input);}catch{}try{mergeHandover(input);}catch{}try{mergeRoster(input);}catch{}try{mergeDevLog(input);}catch{}state.inputDay=input.date;state.adjust=(input.adjust||[]).slice(0,20);try{renderAdjustList();}catch{}state.lastSharedInput=input;try{adoptSharedStamps(input.date,input.stamps);}catch{}
 const stamp=Number.isFinite(Date.parse(saved.modified))?new Date(saved.modified).toLocaleString('ko-KR',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul'}):'수정 시각 없음';
 sharedStatus('공유 저장된 입력 복원 · '+stamp+(saved.editor?' · '+saved.editor:'')+' · 상태 기준은 저장 당시 시각입니다.');
 restoreDailyEdits(input);
 if(!input.raw.trim()){state.result=null;render();sharedStatus('수기 데일리를 복원했습니다. 현재 상태 원문을 넣으면 계산합니다.');return;}
 capture(E.calculate(state.daily,input.raw,{at:input.calculationAt,exclusions:input.exclusions,training:trainingNames(input.training,input.trainees||[],state.daily.date),exceptions:input.exceptions,adjust:input.adjust||[]}),false);state.dirty=false;
}
async function prepareShared(){
 try{
  if(!state.daily||state.demo)throw Error('실제 데일리가 연결된 뒤 공유 입력을 준비하세요.');
  if(state.dailyDraftDirty)throw Error('데일리 수기 수정 탭에서 수정 내용 계산 반영을 먼저 눌러주세요.');
  let at=calculationClock(),date=state.daily.date;
  if($('raw').value.trim()){
   B.validateRequest({raw:$('raw').value,calculationAt:at,exclusions:fields(),training:lines('training'),exceptions:lines('exceptions')});calculate();at=state.result.at;date=state.result.date;
  }else if(!state.manualDailyEdits&&!traineesFromUI().length&&!(state.edu||[]).length&&!state.eduReport&&!(state.adjust||[]).length&&!fields().some(r=>r.name))throw Error('상태 원문을 넣거나 저장할 입력을 먼저 등록하세요.');
  if(E.businessClock(at).date!==date)throw Error('저장 기준 시각과 데일리 운영일이 다릅니다. 기준 날짜와 시각을 확인하세요.');
  const payload=S.makeSharedPayload({date,calculationAt:at,raw:$('raw').value,exclusions:fields(),training:lines('training'),exceptions:lines('exceptions'),trainees:traineesFromUI(),edu:state.edu||[],...(state.eduReport?{eduReport:state.eduReport}:{}),...(otSharedPart()?{ot:otSharedPart()}:{}),...(hourLogSharedPart()?{hourLog:hourLogSharedPart()}:{}),...(stampsForShare()?{stamps:stampsForShare()}:{}),...(changesSharedPart()?{changes:changesSharedPart()}:{}),...(rtSharedPart()||{}),...(macroSharedPart()||{}),...(handoverSharedPart()||{}),...(rosterSharedPart()||{}),...(devSharedPart()||{}),...(extSharedPart()||{}),...((state.adjust||[]).length?{adjust:state.adjust}:{}),...(state.manualDailyEdits?{dailyEdits:state.manualDailyEdits}:{})});
  $('shared-copy-text').value=payload;$('shared-save-dialog').showModal();
  sharedStatus('저장할 내용을 준비했습니다. 수기 데일리도 포함됩니다. 아직 저장되지 않았으니 공유 저장창의 Payload 칸에 붙여넣고 저장하세요.');
  try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(payload);$('shared-copy-note').textContent='복사했습니다. 공유 저장창의 Payload 칸 전체에 붙여넣고 저장하세요. 수기 데일리도 함께 저장됩니다.';}
  catch{$('shared-copy-note').textContent='자동 복사가 제한되어 있습니다. 아래 내용을 Ctrl+A → Ctrl+C로 복사해 공유 저장창에 저장하세요.';$('shared-copy-text').focus();$('shared-copy-text').select?.();}
 }catch(error){fail(error);}
}
if($('shared-prepare-btn'))$('shared-prepare-btn').addEventListener('click',prepareShared);
if($('shared-save-close'))$('shared-save-close').addEventListener('click',()=>$('shared-save-dialog').close());
if($('shared-copy-again'))$('shared-copy-again').addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText($('shared-copy-text').value);toast('복사했습니다. 공유 저장창에서 붙여넣고 저장하세요.');}catch{$('shared-copy-text').focus();$('shared-copy-text').select?.();toast('Ctrl+A → Ctrl+C로 복사하세요.');}});
function setupDailySource(data){
 state.dailyBase=D.packet(data);state.dailyDraft=D.clone(state.dailyBase);state.manualDailyEdits=null;state.dailyDraftDirty=false;state.dailyConflicts=[];state.dailyPane='staff';
}
function dailyStatus(){
 const count=state.manualDailyEdits?.cells.length??0;
 $('daily-status').textContent=!state.dailyBase?'데일리 데이터를 먼저 불러오세요.':`운영일 ${state.dailyBase.date} · ${state.dailyBase.sourceSheet||'데일리'} · 수기 ${count}칸 적용`+(state.dailyDraftDirty?' · 아직 반영하지 않은 수정이 있습니다.':'');
 $('daily-apply').disabled=!state.dailyBase;$('daily-reset').disabled=!state.dailyBase;
}
function renderDailyEditor(){
 dailyStatus();const draft=state.dailyDraft;if(!draft){$('daily-grid').innerHTML='';return;}
 const group=state.dailyPane??'staff',start=group==='staff'?3:group==='hours'?51:64,end=group==='staff'?18:group==='hours'?55:82,last=group==='staff'?180:29;
 const labels={3:'사번',5:'이름',7:'시프트',10:'근무구분',12:'부재 시작',13:'부재 종료',18:'사유',51:'목표 인원',53:'시간 구간',55:'데일리 인원',64:'OT 시작'};
 const columns=Array.from({length:end-start+1},(_,i)=>start+i).filter(c=>group!=='staff'||![4,8,14,15,16,17].includes(c)),pending=D.changes(state.dailyBase,draft),changed=new Set(pending.cells.map(p=>p.cell));
 const search=$('daily-search').value.trim().toLowerCase(),showBlank=$('daily-blanks').checked;
 const rows=[];
 for(let row=6;row<=last;row++){
  const nameCells=group==='staff'?[3,5]:group==='ot'?Array.from({length:15},(_,i)=>68+i):[51,55];
  const nonempty=nameCells.some(c=>String(D.get(draft,D.address(c,row))).trim());
  if(group!=='hours'&&!showBlank&&!nonempty)continue;
  if(group==='staff'&&search&&![3,5,7,10,18].map(c=>D.display(draft,D.address(c,row))).join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(search))continue;
  rows.push(`<tr><th class="daily-row-number">${row}</th>${columns.map(c=>{
   const cell=D.address(c,row),value=D.display(draft,cell),canEdit=D.editable(c,row),conflict=state.dailyConflicts.includes(cell);
   return `<td class="${canEdit?'daily-editable':'daily-readonly'}${changed.has(cell)?' daily-changed':''}${conflict?' daily-conflict':''}">${canEdit?`<input data-cell="${cell}" data-group="${group}" aria-label="${cell} ${esc(labels[c]??'OT 이름')}" value="${esc(value)}" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" maxlength="4000" ${[51,55].includes(c)?'inputmode="decimal"':''}>`:`<span>${esc(value)}</span>`}</td>`;
  }).join('')}</tr>`);
 }
 $('daily-grid').innerHTML=`<table class="daily-sheet"><thead><tr><th class="daily-row-number">행</th>${columns.map(c=>`<th class="${D.editable(c,6)?'':'daily-readonly'}"><b>${D.letter(c)}</b><small>${esc(labels[c]??(c>=68?'OT 이름':D.locate(D.address(c,6)).spec?'원본 참고':'미수집'))}</small></th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows.length?rows.join(''):`<tr><td colspan="${columns.length+1}" class="empty">표시할 행이 없습니다. 빈 행 표시를 켜거나 검색어를 지우세요.</td></tr>`}</tbody></table>`;
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-daily-pane]').forEach(button=>button.classList.toggle('active',button.dataset.dailyPane===group));
}
function applyDailyDraft(){
 if(!state.dailyBase||!state.dailyDraft)throw Error('데일리를 먼저 불러오세요.');
 const checked=D.validateDraft(state.dailyBase,state.dailyDraft,E);let result=null;
 if($('raw').value.trim())result=E.calculate(checked.daily,$('raw').value,{at:calculationClock(),exclusions:fields(),training:trainingNames(lines('training'),traineesFromUI(),checked.daily.date),exceptions:lines('exceptions'),adjust:state.adjust||[]});
 state.daily=checked.daily;state.manualDailyEdits=checked.edits;state.dailyDraftDirty=false;state.dailyConflicts=[];state.dirty=true;clearError();
 if(result)capture(result,false);else{state.result=null;render();}
 renderDailyEditor();sharedStatus('수기 데일리를 계산에 반영했습니다. 다른 화면에도 유지하려면 기존 공유 저장을 완료하세요.');toast('수정한 데일리를 계산에 반영했습니다.');
}
function restoreDailyEdits(input){
 if(!input.dailyEdits)return;
 state.manualDailyEdits=D.validateEdits(input.dailyEdits);state.dailyDraft=D.overlay(state.dailyBase,state.manualDailyEdits,false);
 try{
  const merged=D.overlay(state.dailyBase,state.manualDailyEdits,true),checked=D.validateDraft(state.dailyBase,merged,E);
  state.daily=checked.daily;state.manualDailyEdits=checked.edits;state.dailyDraft=merged;state.dailyDraftDirty=false;state.dailyConflicts=[];
 }catch(error){state.result=null;state.dailyDraftDirty=true;state.dailyConflicts=error.details?.cells??[];render();throw error;}
}
$('daily-apply').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{applyDailyDraft();logChange('데일리 수정 반영');}catch(error){fail(error);}});
$('daily-reset').addEventListener('click',()=>{
 if(!state.dailyBase)return;if(!confirm('수기 데일리 수정을 지우고 현재 불러온 원본으로 되돌릴까요? 공유 목록은 아직 변경되지 않습니다.'))return;
 const previous=state.dailyDraft;state.dailyDraft=D.clone(state.dailyBase);
 try{applyDailyDraft();}catch(error){state.dailyDraft=previous;renderDailyEditor();fail(error);}
});
$('daily-tabs').addEventListener('click',event=>{const button=event.target.closest('[data-daily-pane]');if(!button)return;state.dailyPane=button.dataset.dailyPane;renderDailyEditor();});
$('daily-search').addEventListener('input',renderDailyEditor);$('daily-blanks').addEventListener('change',renderDailyEditor);
$('daily-grid').addEventListener('input',event=>{
 const input=event.target.closest('input[data-cell]');if(!input||!state.dailyDraft)return;
 try{D.put(state.dailyDraft,input.dataset.cell,D.convert(input.dataset.cell,input.value));state.dailyDraftDirty=true;state.dirty=true;input.classList.remove('daily-invalid');input.closest('td').classList.toggle('daily-changed',D.display(state.dailyBase,input.dataset.cell)!==D.display(state.dailyDraft,input.dataset.cell));dailyStatus();}
 catch(error){if(input.value.length<=4000)D.put(state.dailyDraft,input.dataset.cell,input.value);input.classList.add('daily-invalid');state.dailyDraftDirty=true;dailyStatus();fail(error);}
});
$('daily-grid').addEventListener('paste',event=>{
 const input=event.target.closest('input[data-cell]'),text=event.clipboardData?.getData('text/plain');if(!input||!text||!/[\t\r\n]/.test(text))return;event.preventDefault();
 try{const next=D.paste(state.dailyDraft,input.dataset.cell,text,input.dataset.group);state.dailyDraft=next;state.dailyDraftDirty=true;state.dirty=true;renderDailyEditor();toast('표를 붙여넣었습니다. 수정 내용 계산 반영을 눌러주세요.');}catch(error){fail(error);}
});
$('daily-grid').addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
 const input=event.target.closest('input[data-cell]');if(!input||!['ArrowUp','ArrowDown','Enter'].includes(event.key))return;event.preventDefault();
 const {c,r}=D.locate(input.dataset.cell),step=event.key==='ArrowUp'||(event.key==='Enter'&&event.shiftKey)?-1:1;
 for(let row=r+step;row>=6&&row<=180;row+=step){const target=$('daily-grid').querySelector('input[data-cell="'+D.address(c,row)+'"]');if(target){target.focus();target.select();break;}}
});

function loadEmbedded(){
 state.demo=false;state.daily=null;state.result=null;state.rawSample=false;$('raw').value='';$('run-btn').disabled=true;render();
 try{
  const encoded=APP_CONFIG.embeddedData;
  if(!encoded||encoded.startsWith('__'))throw Error('내장 데일리 대기 중입니다. Power Automate를 한 번 실행한 뒤 SharePoint 미리보기를 닫고 HTML을 다시 여세요.');
  const data=B.decodeJSON(new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(Uint8Array.from(atob(encoded),c=>c.charCodeAt(0))));
  if(data.sample)throw Error('실제 데일리 대신 샘플이 내장되어 있습니다. 내보내기 결과를 확인하세요.');
  const man=manualDailyNewer(data);const cache=lastDailyLoad();const useCache=!man&&cache&&String(cache.capturedAt||'')>String(data.capturedAt||'')&&String(cache.date||'')>=String(data.date||'');receive(man||(useCache?cache:data),false);if(man)setTimeout(()=>toast('수동으로 불러온 데일리를 쓰고 있습니다 (내장 데일리보다 최신).'),800);else if(useCache)setTimeout(()=>toast('HTML 안 데일리가 이 PC 보관본보다 오래돼서 보관본을 씁니다.'),800);
  restoreShared(data);AUTO.last=encoded;AUTO.lastShared=autoSharedJson(data);AUTO.unsaved=false;
  $('folder-state').textContent='내장 데일리 · 운영일 '+state.daily.date+' · 원본 생성 '+(state.daily.capturedAt?new Date(state.daily.capturedAt).toLocaleString('ko-KR',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul'}):'시각 없음')+' · 1분마다 자동 갱신';
 }catch(error){const m=manualDailyLoad(),c=lastDailyLoad();if(m&&(!c||String(m.data.capturedAt||'')>=String(c.capturedAt||''))){try{receive(m.data,false);$('folder-state').textContent='수동 데일리 사용 중 · 운영일 '+m.data.date;return;}catch{}}if(c&&lastDailyUse(true)){setTimeout(()=>toast('HTML 데일리를 읽지 못해 이 PC의 마지막 정상 데일리로 열었습니다.'),800);return;}fail(error);$('folder-state').textContent='내장 데일리 확인 필요';}
}
/* ===== 자동 갱신: 새로고침 없이 최신 내장 데일리 반영 ===== */
var AUTO={savedAt:0,shareDir:null,lastInput:0,ms:60000,last:'',lastShared:'null',unsaved:false,pendingData:null,running:false,fails:0,everOk:false,timer:null,checkedAt:0,mode:''};
function autoSharedJson(data){try{return JSON.stringify(data&&data.sharedInput!==undefined?data.sharedInput:null);}catch{return 'null';}}
function autoClockText(ms){const d=new Date(ms+9*3600000),p=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');return p(d.getUTCHours())+':'+p(d.getUTCMinutes())+':'+p(d.getUTCSeconds());}
function autoChip(text,warn){const chip=$('auto-chip');if(!chip)return;chip.textContent=text;chip.className='badge'+(warn?' stale':'');}
function autoDecode(encoded){return B.decodeJSON(new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(Uint8Array.from(atob(encoded),c=>c.charCodeAt(0))));}
function autoInputShape(raw,exclusions,training,exceptions,trainees=[],edu=[],eduReport=null){
 const rows=[];for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const r=exclusions[i]||{};rows.push([String(r.name||'').trim(),ginjoNormalizeClock(r.start||''),ginjoNormalizeClock(r.end||'')]);}
 return JSON.stringify({raw:String(raw||'').replace(/\r\n?/g,'\n').trim(),ex:rows,tr:training,ex2:exceptions,t2:validTrainees(trainees).map(r=>[r.name,r.start,r.end]),e3:(Array.isArray(edu)?edu:[]).map(r=>[r.name,r.date,Number(r.hours),r.kind||'']),er:eduReport?JSON.stringify({m:eduReport.month,t:eduReport.teams,k:eduReport.kinds,l:eduReport.leaders}):''});
}
function autoLocalShape(){return autoInputShape($('raw').value,fields(),lines('training'),lines('exceptions'),traineesFromUI(),state.edu||[],state.eduReport||null);}
function autoSavedShape(input){return autoInputShape(input.raw,input.exclusions,input.training,input.exceptions,input.trainees||[],input.edu||[],input.eduReport||null);}
function autoBusy(){return Boolean(state.dailyDraftDirty)||Boolean(document.querySelector('dialog[open]'));}
function autoMarkUnsaved(){AUTO.unsaved=true;AUTO.savedAt=0;AUTO.lastInput=Date.now();}
document.addEventListener('input',event=>{
 const t=event.target;if(!t)return;
 if(['raw','date','time','use-now','training','exceptions'].includes(t.id)||(t.matches&&t.matches('#exclusion-rows input')||t.matches('#trainee-rows input')))autoMarkUnsaved();
},true);
$('paste-btn')?.addEventListener('click',autoMarkUnsaved);
function autoPersistUI(){try{
 const ui={view:state.view,filter:state.filter,search:$('search').value,y:window.scrollY};
 try{if(state.daily)stampLocal(state.daily.date,snapDom());}catch{}
 ui.inp={raw:$('raw').value,ex:fields(),tr:$('training').value,ex2:$('exceptions').value,date:$('date').value,time:$('time').value,now:$('use-now').checked,sa:AUTO.savedAt||0,t2:traineesRaw(),e3:state.edu||[],er:state.eduReport||null,aj:state.adjust||[],day:bizDate()};
 sessionStorage.setItem('ginjo-ui',JSON.stringify(ui));
}catch{}}
function autoRestoreUI(){
 try{
  const saved=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('ginjo-ui')||'null');sessionStorage.removeItem('ginjo-ui');if(!saved)return;
  if(saved.view&&saved.view!==state.view)setView(saved.view);
  if(saved.filter){state.filter=saved.filter;}
  if(saved.search){$('search').value=saved.search;state.search=saved.search.toLowerCase().trim();}
  if(saved.inp&&(!saved.inp.day||saved.inp.day===bizDate())){
   const p=saved.inp,ln=s=>String(s||'').split(/\r?\n/).map(v=>v.trim()).filter(Boolean),date=state.daily?.date||bizDate();
   const L={adjust:p.aj||[],raw:p.raw||'',exclusions:(p.ex||[]).filter(r=>r&&r.name),training:ln(p.tr),exceptions:ln(p.ex2),trainees:validTrainees(p.t2||[]),eduReport:p.er||null};
   const S=state.lastSharedInput&&state.lastSharedInput.date===date?state.lastSharedInput:{raw:'',exclusions:[],training:[],exceptions:[],trainees:[],eduReport:null,adjust:[]};
   const merged=mergeInputs(L,loadStamps(date),S,S.stamps);
   applyMerged(merged.values,S,p);saveStamps(date,merged.stamps);
   const changed=fieldHash('raw',merged.values.raw)!==fieldHash('raw',S.raw)||JSON.stringify(merged.values.exclusions.map(exKey).sort())!==JSON.stringify((S.exclusions||[]).filter(r=>r.name).map(exKey).sort())||JSON.stringify(merged.values.trainees.map(trKey).sort())!==JSON.stringify((S.trainees||[]).map(trKey).sort());
   AUTO.unsaved=changed;
   try{if(state.daily&&String($('raw').value).trim())calculate();else render();}catch(error){toast('새 데일리로 다시 계산하지 못했습니다: '+(error.message||error));}
  }
  renderOverview();renderTable();
  if(Number.isFinite(saved.y))window.scrollTo(0,saved.y);
 }catch{}
}
async function autoFetch(){return await parent.__shellFetch(APP_CONFIG.dataKey);}
async function __autoFetchOld(){
 const url=new URL(location.href);url.hash='';url.searchParams.set('_ginjo',String(Date.now()));
 const controller=new AbortController(),timeout=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),20000);
 try{
  const response=await fetch(url.href,{cache:'no-store',credentials:'include',signal:controller.signal});
  if(!response.ok)throw Error('HTTP '+response.status);
  const text=await response.text();
  const found=/"embeddedData":"([A-Za-z0-9+\/=]+)"/.exec(text);
  if(!found)throw Error('새 파일에서 내장 데일리를 찾지 못했습니다(로그인 화면이 반환됐을 수 있습니다).');
  return found[1];
 }finally{clearTimeout(timeout);}
}
function autoApplyShared(data){
 restoreShared(data);AUTO.lastShared=autoSharedJson(data);AUTO.unsaved=false;AUTO.pendingData=null;
 const button=$('auto-apply-shared');if(button)button.classList.add('hidden');
}
function autoApply(encoded){
 const data=autoDecode(encoded);
 if(data.sample)throw Error('실제 데일리 대신 샘플이 내장되어 있습니다. 내보내기 결과를 확인하세요.');
 receive(data,false);
 const sharedJson=autoSharedJson(data);
 if(!AUTO.unsaved){autoApplyShared(data);}
 else if(sharedJson!==AUTO.lastShared){
  let mine=false;
  try{const saved=data.sharedInput?S.readSharedRow(data.sharedInput).input:null;mine=Boolean(saved)&&autoSavedShape(saved)===autoLocalShape();}catch{}
  if(mine)autoApplyShared(data);
  else{AUTO.pendingData=data;const button=$('auto-apply-shared');if(button)button.classList.remove('hidden');}
 }
 AUTO.last=encoded;
 const stamp=state.daily?.capturedAt&&Number.isFinite(Date.parse(state.daily.capturedAt))?autoClockText(Date.parse(state.daily.capturedAt)):'';
 $('folder-state').textContent='내장 데일리 · 운영일 '+state.daily.date+(stamp?' · 원본 생성 '+stamp:'')+' · 1분마다 자동 갱신';
 return Boolean(AUTO.pendingData);
}
async function autoCheck(manual=false){
 if(AUTO.running)return;
 if(AUTO.mode!=='fetch'){if(manual)toast('이 열기 방식에서는 자동 갱신을 쓸 수 없습니다. 파일을 다시 여세요.');return;}
 AUTO.running=true;
 try{
  let encoded;
  try{encoded=await autoFetch();AUTO.fails=0;AUTO.everOk=true;AUTO.checkedAt=Date.now();}
  catch(error){
   AUTO.fails++;
   const sandbox=error.name!=='TypeError'?'자동 갱신 실패('+(error.message||error.name)+')':AUTO.everOk?'네트워크 확인 실패 · 1분 뒤 다시 시도합니다.':'이 열기 방식에서는 자동 갱신이 막혀 있습니다. 파일을 다시 여세요.';
   autoChip(sandbox+' · 마지막 성공 '+(AUTO.checkedAt?autoClockText(AUTO.checkedAt):'없음'),true);
   if(manual)toast(sandbox);
   return;
  }
  if(encoded!==AUTO.last){
   if(autoBusy()){
    if(manual)toast('수정 중이거나 창이 열려 있어 갱신을 보류했습니다. 계산 반영 또는 창 닫기 후 다시 시도하세요.');
    autoChip('새 데이터 있음 · 수정 중이라 보류',true);return;
   }
   let pending;
   try{pending=autoApply(encoded);}catch(error){fail(error);autoChip('자동 갱신 반영 오류 · 오류 상세 확인',true);return;}
   autoChip(pending?'자동 갱신 '+autoClockText(Date.now())+' · 다른 팀장님의 새 공유 입력 있음':'자동 갱신 '+autoClockText(Date.now())+' · 새 데이터 반영',Boolean(pending));
   if(manual)toast('최신 데일리를 반영했습니다.');
  }else{
   autoChip(AUTO.pendingData?'자동 갱신 · 다른 팀장님의 새 공유 입력 있음':'자동 갱신 · 확인 '+autoClockText(AUTO.checkedAt)+' · 변경 없음',Boolean(AUTO.pendingData));
   if(manual)toast('이미 최신입니다.');
  }
 }finally{AUTO.running=false;}
}
function autoSchedule(){clearTimeout(AUTO.timer);AUTO.timer=setTimeout(async()=>{await autoTick();autoSchedule();},AUTO.ms+Math.floor(Math.random()*4000));}
async function autoTick(){
 if(AUTO.mode==='fetch'){await autoCheck(false);return;}
 if(AUTO.mode==='reload'){
  if(autoBusy()||Date.now()-AUTO.lastInput<20000){autoChip('자동 갱신 보류 · '+(autoBusy()?'창 열림/수기 수정 중':'입력 후 20초 대기')+' · 다시 시도 예정',true);return;}
  autoPersistUI();location.reload();
 }
}
function autoStart(){
 const chip=document.createElement('span');chip.id='auto-chip';chip.className='badge';chip.style.marginLeft='6px';
 const apply=document.createElement('button');apply.id='auto-apply-shared';apply.className='quiet hidden';apply.textContent='새 공유 입력 적용';apply.style.marginLeft='6px';
 $('connection-badge').after(chip,apply);
 apply.addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(!AUTO.pendingData)return;
  if(!confirm('다른 팀장님이 공유 저장한 입력으로 화면의 원문·제외인원이 바뀝니다. 지금 입력 중인 내용은 사라집니다. 적용할까요?'))return;
  try{autoApplyShared(AUTO.pendingData);autoChip('자동 갱신 '+autoClockText(Date.now())+' · 공유 입력 적용',false);toast('공유 입력을 적용했습니다.');}catch(error){fail(error);}
 });
 autoRestoreUI();
 if(location.protocol==='https:'||location.protocol==='http:'){AUTO.mode='fetch';AUTO.checkedAt=Date.now();autoChip('자동 갱신 · 1분마다 확인',false);}
 else if(location.protocol==='file:'){AUTO.mode='reload';const dz=state.daily&&state.daily.capturedAt&&Number.isFinite(Date.parse(state.daily.capturedAt))?autoClockText(Date.parse(state.daily.capturedAt)):'없음';autoChip('자동 갱신 · 파일 읽음 '+autoClockText(Date.now())+' · 원본 생성 '+dz+' · 1분마다 다시 읽음',false);}
 else{AUTO.mode='';autoChip('자동 갱신 불가 · 파일을 다시 여세요',true);return;}
 document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.visibilityState==='visible'&&AUTO.mode==='fetch'&&Date.now()-AUTO.checkedAt>30000)autoCheck(false);});
 autoSchedule();
}

/* ===== 공유 저장 자동화: 계산 시 입력/ 폴더에 공유 저장 파일을 씁니다 ===== */
function shareDb(){return new Promise((res,rej)=>{const r=indexedDB.open('ginjo-share-'+APP_CONFIG.ns,1);r.onupgradeneeded=()=>r.result.createObjectStore('h');r.onsuccess=()=>res(r.result);r.onerror=()=>rej(r.error);});}
async function shareHandleGet(){try{const db=await shareDb();return await new Promise((res,rej)=>{const q=db.transaction('h').objectStore('h').get('dir');q.onsuccess=()=>res(q.result||null);q.onerror=()=>rej(q.error);});}catch{return null;}}
async function shareHandleSet(handle){try{const db=await shareDb();await new Promise((res,rej)=>{const tx=db.transaction('h','readwrite');tx.objectStore('h').put(handle,'dir');tx.oncomplete=res;tx.onerror=()=>rej(tx.error);});return true;}catch{return false;}}
async function shareConnect(){
 try{
  if(!globalThis.showDirectoryPicker)throw Error('이 브라우저에서는 폴더 연결을 지원하지 않습니다. ① 복사 방식을 사용하세요.');
  const handle=await showDirectoryPicker({id:'ginjo-share',mode:'readwrite'});AUTO.shareDir=handle;
  const kept=await shareHandleSet(handle);setTimeout(()=>{try{refreshShareConn();}catch{}},100);
  sharedStatus('자동 저장 폴더 연결됨 · '+handle.name+(kept?' · 다음에도 기억합니다.':' · 이 브라우저는 연결을 기억하지 못해 새로고침 뒤 다시 연결해야 합니다.'));
  toast('자동 저장 폴더를 연결했습니다.');
 }catch(error){if(error.name!=='AbortError'){sharedStatus('자동 저장 폴더 연결 실패: '+(error.message||error.name)+' · ① 복사 방식을 사용하세요.');}}
}
async function shareWrite(payload){
 const handle=AUTO.shareDir||await shareHandleGet();if(!handle)return 'none';
 let perm=await handle.queryPermission({mode:'readwrite'});
 if(perm!=='granted'){
  /* 권한 요청은 사용자가 직접 누른 직후에만 가능합니다. 자동 실행(버튼 없음)에서는 요청하지 않고 '권한 없음'으로 처리합니다. */
  if(navigator.userActivation&&!navigator.userActivation.isActive)return 'denied';
  try{perm=await handle.requestPermission({mode:'readwrite'});}catch{return 'denied';}
 }
 if(perm!=='granted')return 'denied';
 AUTO.shareDir=handle;
 const dir=await handle.getDirectoryHandle('입력',{create:true}),d=new Date(Date.now()+9*3600000),p=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');
 const name='share-'+d.getUTCFullYear()+p(d.getUTCMonth()+1)+p(d.getUTCDate())+'-'+p(d.getUTCHours())+p(d.getUTCMinutes())+p(d.getUTCSeconds())+'-'+crypto.randomUUID().slice(0,8)+'.json';
 const file=await dir.getFileHandle(name,{create:true}),stream=await file.createWritable();
 try{await stream.write(payload);await stream.close();}catch(error){try{await stream.abort();}catch{}throw error;}
 return name;
}
function autoShareAfter(){return autoShareAfterRun({feature:true});}
async function autoShareAfterRun(opt){
 if(!APP_CONFIG.sharedEnabled||state.demo||!state.daily||state.dailyDraftDirty){if(opt&&opt.feature&&APP_CONFIG.sharedEnabled&&!state.demo){const why=!state.daily?'데일리를 아직 불러오지 못해 공유를 건너뜀 · 이 PC에만 저장됨':'데일리 수정 중이라 공유를 건너뜀 · 이 PC에만 저장됨';devLogAdd('share',why);try{toast(why);}catch{}}return;}
 try{
  const hasRaw=Boolean($('raw').value.trim());if(hasRaw&&!state.result){try{calculate();}catch{}}if(hasRaw&&!state.result){sharedStatus('자동 저장 실패: 상태 원문을 계산하지 못했습니다. 원문을 확인하세요.');return;}
  const at=hasRaw?state.result.at:calculationClock(),date=hasRaw?state.result.date:E.businessClock(at).date;
  if(E.businessClock(at).date!==date)throw Error('저장 기준 시각과 데일리 운영일이 다릅니다.');
  const payload=S.makeSharedPayload({date,calculationAt:at,raw:$('raw').value,exclusions:fields(),training:lines('training'),exceptions:lines('exceptions'),trainees:traineesFromUI(),edu:state.edu||[],...(state.eduReport?{eduReport:state.eduReport}:{}),...(otSharedPart()?{ot:otSharedPart()}:{}),...(hourLogSharedPart()?{hourLog:hourLogSharedPart()}:{}),...(stampsForShare()?{stamps:stampsForShare()}:{}),...(changesSharedPart()?{changes:changesSharedPart()}:{}),...(rtSharedPart()||{}),...(macroSharedPart()||{}),...(handoverSharedPart()||{}),...(rosterSharedPart()||{}),...(devSharedPart()||{}),...(extSharedPart()||{}),...((state.adjust||[]).length?{adjust:state.adjust}:{}),...(state.manualDailyEdits?{dailyEdits:state.manualDailyEdits}:{})});
  const result=await shareWrite(payload);
  if(result!=='none'&&result!=='denied'){}else{try{$('issue-save-state').textContent='공유 저장 안 됨 · 오른쪽 위 공유 저장 연결 확인';$('issue-save-state').className='bad';}catch{}}
  if(result==='none')devLogAdd('share','공유 저장 폴더가 연결되지 않음');
  if(result==='denied')devLogAdd('share','폴더 쓰기 권한이 허용되지 않음');
  if(result==='none'){sharedStatus('자동 저장 폴더가 연결되지 않았습니다. ③으로 한 번 연결하면 계산·등록할 때 자동 저장됩니다. 지금은 ① 복사 방식으로 저장하세요.');toast('자동 저장 폴더가 연결되지 않아 공유 저장하지 못했습니다. 현황 화면 아래 ③ 연결을 확인하세요.');return;}
  if(result==='denied'){sharedStatus('폴더 쓰기 권한이 허용되지 않아 자동 저장하지 못했습니다. 다시 눌러 허용하거나 ① 복사 방식으로 저장하세요.');toast('폴더 쓰기 권한이 없어 공유 저장하지 못했습니다.');return;}
  AUTO.unsaved=true;AUTO.savedAt=Date.now();
  try{$('issue-save-state').textContent='공유 저장 '+autoClockText(Date.now())+' 보냄';$('issue-save-state').className='ok';}catch{}
  sharedStatus('자동 저장 파일을 보냈습니다 · '+autoClockText(Date.now())+' · '+result+' · 반영까지 1~2분 걸립니다.');toast(hasRaw?'계산 결과를 공유 저장 파일로 보냈습니다.':'공유 저장 파일을 보냈습니다.');
 }catch(error){devLogAdd('share','공유 저장 실패: '+(error.message||error.name));sharedStatus('자동 저장 실패: '+(error.message||error.name)+' · ① 복사 방식으로 저장하세요.');toast('공유 저장 실패: '+(error.message||error.name));}
}
if($('shared-folder-btn'))$('shared-folder-btn').addEventListener('click',shareConnect);

/* ===== 교육생 기간 명단 ===== */
function kstToday(){return new Date(Date.now()+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(0,10);}
function dayDiff(a,b){return Math.round((Date.parse(a+'T00:00:00Z')-Date.parse(b+'T00:00:00Z'))/86400000);}
function validTrainees(list){return (Array.isArray(list)?list:[]).map(r=>({name:String(r?.name||'').trim(),start:String(r?.start||''),end:String(r?.end||'')})).filter(r=>r.name&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(r.start)&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(r.end)&&r.start<=r.end);}
function traineesRaw(){return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#trainee-rows .trainee-row')).map(row=>({name:row.querySelector('[data-t=name]').value.trim(),start:row.querySelector('[data-t=start]').value,end:row.querySelector('[data-t=end]').value})).filter(r=>r.name||r.start||r.end);}
function traineesFromUI(){return validTrainees(traineesRaw());}
function traineeStatus(t,today){
 if(!t.name||!t.start||!t.end)return {k:'',text:'입력 중'};
 if(t.start>t.end)return {k:'bad',text:'기간 확인'};
 if(today<t.start)return {k:'',text:'예정 · '+dayDiff(t.start,today)+'일 뒤'};
 if(today>t.end)return {k:'end',text:'종료'};
 const left=dayDiff(t.end,today);return {k:left<=3?'soon':'on',text:left===0?'제외 중 · 오늘 종료':'제외 중 · D-'+left};
}
function traineeToday(){return (state.daily&&state.daily.date)||kstToday();}
function trainingNames(manual,trainees,date){
 const out=[],seen=new Set();
 const add=value=>{const name=String(value||'').trim();if(!name)return;const k=name.toLowerCase();if(!seen.has(k)){seen.add(k);out.push(name);}};
 for(const name of manual||[])add(name);
 for(const r of validTrainees(trainees)){
  if(date<r.start||date>r.end)continue;
  const entry=r.name.toUpperCase(),row=(state.daily?.schedule||[]).find(s=>String(s.id||'').trim().toUpperCase()===entry);
  add(row&&row.name?row.name:r.name);
 }
 return out;
}
function refreshTraineeBadges(){
 const today=traineeToday();let active=0;
 document.querySelectorAll('#trainee-rows .trainee-row').forEach(row=>{
  const t={name:row.querySelector('[data-t=name]').value.trim(),start:row.querySelector('[data-t=start]').value,end:row.querySelector('[data-t=end]').value};
  const s=traineeStatus(t,today),badge=row.querySelector('.trainee-badge');badge.textContent=s.text;badge.className='trainee-badge '+s.k;
  row.style.display='';
  if(s.k==='on'||s.k==='soon')active++;
 });
 const sum=$('trainee-summary');if(sum){sum.textContent='제외 중 '+active+'명';sum.className='trainee-summary'+(active?'':' zero');}
}
function traineeRowHTML(r){
 const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/</g,'&lt;');
 return `<div class="trainee-row"><input data-t="name" aria-label="교육생 이름 또는 사번" placeholder="이름 / 사번" autocomplete="off" value="${esc(r.name)}"><span class="trainee-badge"></span><button type="button" class="trainee-del" aria-label="교육생 삭제" title="삭제">×</button><div class="trainee-dates"><input type="date" data-t="start" aria-label="교육 시작일" value="${esc(r.start)}"><span>~</span><input type="date" data-t="end" aria-label="교육 종료일" value="${esc(r.end)}"></div></div>`;
}
function setTraineesUI(list,prune){
 const box=$('trainee-rows');if(!box)return;
 let rows=(Array.isArray(list)?list:[]).filter(r=>r&&(r.name||r.start||r.end));
 if(prune){const limit=new Date(Date.parse(traineeToday()+'T00:00:00Z')-3*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10);rows=rows.filter(r=>!r.end||r.end>=limit);}
 rows=rows.slice(0,30);box.innerHTML=rows.map(traineeRowHTML).join('')+(rows.length<30?traineeRowHTML({}):'');
 refreshTraineeBadges();
}
if($('trainee-rows')){
 $('trainee-rows').addEventListener('input',()=>{state.dirty=true;refreshTraineeBadges();renderTodayOver();if(APP_CONFIG.sharedEnabled)sharedStatus('입력을 변경했습니다. 아직 공유 저장하지 않았습니다.');});
 $('trainee-rows').addEventListener('click',event=>{
  const del=event.target.closest('.trainee-del');if(!del)return;
  del.closest('.trainee-row').remove();autoMarkUnsaved();state.dirty=true;
  if(!document.querySelector('#trainee-rows .trainee-row'))$('trainee-rows').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',traineeRowHTML({}));
  refreshTraineeBadges();if(APP_CONFIG.sharedEnabled)sharedStatus('교육생을 삭제했습니다. 계산하면 공유 저장됩니다.');
 });
 $('trainee-add').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(document.querySelectorAll('#trainee-rows .trainee-row').length>=30){toast('교육생은 최대 30명입니다.');return;}
  $('trainee-rows').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',traineeRowHTML({}));refreshTraineeBadges();
  const rows=document.querySelectorAll('#trainee-rows .trainee-row');rows[rows.length-1].querySelector('[data-t=name]').focus();
 });
 setTraineesUI([],false);
}


/* ===== 문제예상 인원 ===== */
function issueRows(result){
 if(!result||!Array.isArray(result.nextPeople)||!result.nextPeople.length)return null;
 const cur=new Map((result.people||[]).map(p=>[String(p.idKey??p.id).toLowerCase(),p])),rows=[],seen=new Set();
 for(const np of result.nextPeople){
  const id=String(np.idKey??np.id).toLowerCase();seen.add(id);
  const p=cur.get(id),curMin=p?Number(p.minutes)||0:0,nextMin=Number(np.minutes)||0,diff=nextMin-curMin;
  if(Math.abs(diff)<0.5)continue;
  const status=p?p.status:'원문 없음',reasons=[];
  if(diff>0){
   if(!p)reasons.push('현재 원문에 없음(미로그인) · 다음 시간 근무 예정');
   else if(p.half)reasons.push('반차 구간 · 다음 시간 근무 복귀');
   else if(['회의','식사','휴식','교육','자리에 없음','오프라인'].includes(status))reasons.push(`현재 ${status} 상태로 인정 ${format(curMin,0)}분 · 다음 시간 근무 예정`);
   else if(Number(p.excluded)>0)reasons.push(`현재 시간 등록 제외 ${format(p.excluded,0)}분 · 다음 시간 해제`);
   else reasons.push('현재 인정 부족 · 시프트·근무 시작 시각 확인');
   if(Number(np.otMinutes)>0)reasons.push(`다음 시간 OT +${format(np.otMinutes,0)}분`);
  }else{
   if(p&&p.present&&!p.expected&&!p.ot)reasons.push('근무 예정 외 로그인(현재 인정에 포함) · 다음 시간 예정 없음');
   else if(Number(np.regular)===0&&Number(np.otMinutes)===0)reasons.push('다음 시간 시프트 종료 · 퇴근 예정');
   else if(Number(np.regular)<60)reasons.push(`다음 시간 근무 ${format(np.regular,0)}분만 예정 (중간 퇴근·휴가·제외 시간)`);
   else reasons.push('다음 시간 제외·휴가 시간 반영');
  }
  rows.push({id:np.id,name:np.name,status,curMin,nextMin,diff,reason:reasons.join(' · ')});
 }
 for(const p of result.people||[]){
  const id=String(p.idKey??p.id).toLowerCase();if(seen.has(id))continue;
  const curMin=Number(p.minutes)||0;if(curMin<0.5)continue;
  rows.push({id:p.id,name:p.name,status:p.status,curMin,nextMin:0,diff:-curMin,reason:'근무표에 없는 사번(원문만 존재) · 다음 시간 예상에서 제외'});
 }
 return rows.sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(b.diff)-Math.abs(a.diff)||String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name),'ko'));
}

function shiftSuspects(r,atMin){
 const d=state.daily;if(!d||(!r&&atMin===undefined))return [];
 const now=atMin!==undefined?atMin:(()=>{const c=E.businessClock(r.at);return c.hour*60+c.minute;})(),hm=m=>{m=((m%1440)+1440)%1440;return String(Math.floor(m/60)).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(m%60).padStart(2,'0');};
 const covers=(s,e,m)=>[m,m-1440,m+1440].some(x=>x>=s&&x<e);
 const out=[],seen=new Set();
 for(const row of d.schedule||[]){
  const m=String(row.shift||'').match(/(\d{2})(\d{2})\s*[-–—~～]\s*(\d{2})(\d{2})/);if(!m||!row.window)continue;
  let s=Number(m[1])*60+Number(m[2]),e=Number(m[3])*60+Number(m[4]);if(e<=s)e+=1440;
  const w=row.window,txt=covers(s,e,now),calc=covers(w.startMinutes,w.endMinutes,now);
  if(txt===calc)continue;const k=String(row.idKey??row.id).toLowerCase();if(seen.has(k))continue;seen.add(k);
  const span=(e-s)/60,flagN=Number(String(row.flag||'').trim());
  let reason=txt?`시프트상 지금 근무인데 근무구분 ${row.flag||'—'} 기준으로 ${hm(w.endMinutes)} 종료로 계산됨`:`시프트상 지금 근무가 아닌데 근무구분 기준으로 근무로 계산됨`;
  if(txt&&Number.isFinite(flagN)&&flagN<span&&!/모성/.test(row.shift))reason+=' · 단축 근무면 시프트 뒤에 (모성) 표기, 아니면 근무구분 확인';
  out.push({id:row.id,name:row.name||row.id,shift:row.shift,flag:row.flag,calc:hm(w.startMinutes)+'~'+hm(w.endMinutes),reason});
 }
 return out;
}

function renderDayCheck(){
 const box=$('day-check-body');if(!box)return;
 if(!state.daily){box.innerHTML='<p class="tiny">데일리를 먼저 불러오세요.</p>';$('day-check-count').textContent='—';return;}
 let fc;try{fc=E.forecastDay(state.daily,{training:trainingNames(lines('training'),traineesFromUI(),state.daily.date),exclusions:fields(),exceptions:lines('exceptions'),adjust:state.adjust||[]});}catch(error){box.textContent='점검 실패: '+(error.message||error);return;}
 const bad=fc.hours.filter(h=>Number.isFinite(Number(h.dailyCount))&&Math.round(Number(h.dailyCount))!==h.heads);
 $('day-check-count').textContent=bad.length?bad.length+'개 시간대 불일치':'모두 일치';
 box.innerHTML=fc.hours.length?`<table class="data"><thead><tr><th>시간</th><th>데일리(BC)</th><th>계산</th><th>차이</th><th>시프트 해석 확인</th></tr></thead><tbody>${fc.hours.map(h=>{const bc=Math.round(Number(h.dailyCount)),diff=h.heads-bc,sus=diff?shiftSuspects(null,h.hour*60):[];return `<tr class="${diff?'mismatch':''}"><td>${hourLabel(h.hour)}시</td><td>${Number.isFinite(bc)?bc:'—'}</td><td>${h.heads}</td><td>${diff?(diff>0?'+':'')+diff:'—'}</td><td class="reason">${diff?(sus.length?sus.map(x=>esc(x.name)+' ('+esc(x.shift)+' · 계산 '+esc(x.calc)+')').join(', '):'해석 차이 인원 없음 · OT·휴가·차감 표기 확인'):''}</td></tr>`;}).join('')}</tbody></table>`:'<p class="tiny">시간대 목표가 없습니다.</p>';
}
function renderNowIssues(){
 const sum=$('now-summary'),wrap=$('now-wrap');if(!sum||!wrap)return 0;
 const r=state.result,plan=r?.plan;
 if(!plan){sum.innerHTML='';wrap.innerHTML='<p class="tiny">계산하면 데일리상 와야 할 인원과 실제 출근 인원을 비교합니다.</p>';return 0;}
 const diff=plan.actualCount-plan.count,q=state.search.trim().toLowerCase(),bc=Number(r.current.dailyCount),bcDiff=Number.isFinite(bc)&&Math.round(bc)!==plan.count;
 sum.innerHTML=`<div class="box"><small>데일리상 와야 할 인원 (OT 포함 · 과투입 제외 반영)</small><b>${format(plan.count,0)}명</b> <span class="tiny">${percentage(plan.rate)}%</span></div><div class="box"><small>실제 출근 인원</small><b>${format(plan.actualCount,0)}명</b> <span class="tiny">${percentage(r.current.rate)}%</span></div><div class="box ${diff?'bad':'good'}"><small>차이</small><b>${diff>0?'+':''}${diff}명</b> <span class="tiny">${diff?'아래 인원 확인':'인원 일치'}</span></div>`+(bcDiff?`<div class="box bad" style="grid-column:1/-1"><small>데일리 시트 인원(BC)과 계산이 다릅니다</small><b>데일리 ${format(bc,0)}명 ↔ 계산 ${format(plan.count,0)}명</b> <span class="tiny">시프트·근무구분(J열)·OT 표기를 확인하세요. 단축 근무는 시프트 뒤 (모성)처럼 표기되어 있어야 적힌 시각 그대로 계산합니다.</span></div>`:'');
 const sus=(diff||bcDiff)?shiftSuspects(r):[];
 if(!diff&&!bcDiff){wrap.innerHTML=plan.rows.length?`<p class="tiny">인원 수는 맞지만 데일리와 다른 사람이 ${plan.rows.length}명 있습니다 (예정 ${plan.rows.filter(x=>x.planned).length}명 미출근 ↔ 예정 외 ${plan.rows.filter(x=>x.actual).length}명 출근).</p>`:'<p class="tiny">데일리상 인원과 실제 출근 인원이 같습니다.</p>';return 0;}
 const rows=plan.rows.filter(x=>!q||[x.name,x.id,x.status,x.reason,x.shift].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q));
 wrap.innerHTML=(sus.length?`<div class="sus-box"><b>시프트 해석 확인 필요 ${sus.length}명</b> <span class="tiny">데일리에 적힌 시프트 시각과 근무구분(J열)으로 계산한 근무시간이 지금 시간에서 서로 다릅니다.</span><table class="data"><thead><tr><th>이름 / 사번</th><th>시프트 (근무구분)</th><th>계산된 근무</th><th>확인 내용</th></tr></thead><tbody>${sus.map(x=>`<tr><td class="person">${esc(x.name)}<span class="id">${esc(x.id)}</span></td><td>${esc(x.shift)} <span class="tiny">(${esc(x.flag||'—')})</span></td><td>${esc(x.calc)}</td><td class="reason">${esc(x.reason)}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`:'')+`<div class="table-wrap"><table class="data"><thead><tr><th>이름 / 사번</th><th>시프트</th><th>현재 상태</th><th>구분</th><th>예상 원인</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.length?rows.map(x=>`<tr><td class="person">${esc(x.name||x.id)}<span class="id">${esc(x.id)}</span></td><td>${esc(x.shift||'—')}</td><td><span class="pill ${statusClass(x.status)}">${esc(x.status)}</span></td><td>${x.planned?'<span class="pill red">데일리O · 출근X</span>':'<span class="pill amber">데일리X · 출근O</span>'}</td><td class="reason">${esc(x.reason)}</td></tr>`).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="5" class="compact-empty">검색 결과가 없습니다.</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div>`;
 return (diff||bcDiff)?new Set([...plan.rows.map(x=>String(x.idKey??x.id).toLowerCase()),...sus.map(x=>String(x.id).toLowerCase())]).size:0;
}
function renderIssues(){try{$('zone-now-range').textContent=$('current-hour').textContent;$('zone-next-range').textContent=$('next-hour').textContent;}catch{}
 const box=$('issues-body');if(!box)return;
 const nowCount=renderNowIssues();try{renderDayCheck();}catch{}
 const result=state.result,rows=issueRows(result),c=result?.current,n=result?.next;
 if(!result||!c){$('issues-summary').innerHTML='';box.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6" class="compact-empty">계산하면 표시됩니다.</td></tr>';$('issues-count').textContent='';return;}
 if(!n||rows===null){$('issues-summary').innerHTML='';box.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6" class="compact-empty">다음 시간 예상 정보가 없어 비교할 수 없습니다.</td></tr>';$('issues-count').textContent='';return;}
 const pp=(n.rate-c.rate)*100,gapMin=n.minutes-c.minutes,big=Math.abs(pp)>=3;
 $('issues-summary').innerHTML=`<div class="box"><small>이번 시간 실제 (${result.hour}~${result.hour+1}시)</small><b>${percentage(c.rate)}%</b> <span class="tiny">인정 ${format(c.minutes,0)}분</span></div><div class="box"><small>다음 시간 예상 (${n.hour}~${n.hour+1}시)</small><b>${percentage(n.rate)}%</b> <span class="tiny">예정 ${format(n.minutes,0)}분</span></div><div class="box ${big?'bad':'good'}"><small>차이</small><b>${pp>0?'+':''}${pp.toFixed(1)}%p</b> <span class="tiny">${gapMin>0?'+':''}${format(gapMin,0)}분 · ${big?'차이가 있어 아래 인원을 확인하세요':'큰 차이 없음'}</span></div>`;
 $('issues-sub').textContent=big?`실제 ${percentage(c.rate)}% ↔ 예상 ${percentage(n.rate)}% · 차이 원인으로 예상되는 인원 ${rows.length}명`:'실제와 예상 투입률이 비슷합니다. 아래는 인원별 변동입니다.';
 $('issues-count').textContent=nowCount?String(nowCount):'';
 const q=state.search.trim().toLowerCase(),shown=rows.filter(r=>!q||[r.name,r.id,r.status,r.reason].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q));
 box.innerHTML=shown.length?shown.map(r=>`<tr><td class="person">${esc(r.name||r.id)}<span class="id">${esc(r.id)}</span></td><td><span class="pill ${statusClass(r.status)}">${esc(r.status||'원문 없음')}</span></td><td class="credit">${format(r.curMin,0)}분</td><td class="credit">${format(r.nextMin,0)}분</td><td><span class="issues-diff ${r.diff>0?'plus':'minus'}">${r.diff>0?'+':''}${format(r.diff,0)}분</span></td><td class="reason">${esc(r.reason)}</td></tr>`).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="6" class="compact-empty">차이를 만드는 인원이 없습니다.</td></tr>';
}

/* ===== 수기 추가 등록 · 과투입 예상 · 향후 3일 · 이번달 교육 · 05:00 초기화 ===== */
state.edu=state.edu||[];state.inputDay=null;
function bizDate(){return E.businessClock(new Date().toISOString()).date;}
function hourLabel(h){return String(h%24).padStart(2,'0');}
/* 수기 추가 */
function renderManualList(){
 const box=$('manual-list');if(!box)return;
 const rows=fields().map((r,i)=>({...r,i})).filter(r=>r.name);
 box.innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(r=>`<div class="issue-item manual"><div><b>${esc(r.name)}</b> <span>${esc(String(r.start||'—').replace(':',''))}~${esc(String(r.end||'—').replace(':',''))}</span></div><button type="button" class="manual-del" data-i="${r.i}" aria-label="수기 추가 삭제" title="삭제">×</button></div>`).join(''):'<div class="issue-empty">없음</div>';
}
async function registerManual(){
 try{
  const name=$('manual-name').value.trim(),start=ginjoNormalizeClock($('manual-start').value),end=ginjoNormalizeClock($('manual-end').value),ok=v=>/^(?:[01]?\d|2[0-8]):[0-5]\d$/.test(v)||v==='29:00';
  if(!name)throw Error('수기 추가: 이름 또는 사번을 입력하세요.');
  if(!ok(start)||!ok(end)||start===end)throw Error('수기 추가: 시작·종료 시간을 1300처럼 입력하세요.');
  if(!state.daily)throw Error('데일리를 먼저 불러오세요.');
  const trs=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#exclusion-rows tr')),slot=trs.find(tr=>!tr.querySelector('[data-col=name]').value.trim()&&!tr.querySelector('[data-col=start]').value.trim()&&!tr.querySelector('[data-col=end]').value.trim());
  if(!slot)throw Error('수기 추가는 최대 10명입니다. 목록에서 삭제한 뒤 등록하세요.');
  const set=(n,s,e)=>{slot.querySelector('[data-col=name]').value=n;slot.querySelector('[data-col=start]').value=s;slot.querySelector('[data-col=end]').value=e;};
  set(name,start.replace(':',''),end.replace(':',''));
  const hasRaw=Boolean($('raw').value.trim());
  if(hasRaw){try{clearError();calculate();}catch(error){set('','','');renderManualList();throw error;}}
  else{const ids=(state.daily.schedule||[]).filter(r=>String(r.id||'').trim().toUpperCase()===name.toUpperCase()||String(r.name||'').replace(/\s+/g,'')===name.replace(/\s+/g,''));if(new Set(ids.map(r=>String(r.idKey??r.id).toLowerCase())).size!==1){set('','','');renderManualList();throw Error('수기 추가: 데일리에서 '+name+'을(를) 한 명으로 찾지 못했습니다. 동명이인은 사번을 입력하세요.');}}
  $('manual-name').value='';$('manual-start').value='';$('manual-end').value='';logChange('수기 추가 등록 · '+name+' '+start+'~'+end);
  autoMarkUnsaved();state.dirty=true;renderManualList();
  if(hasRaw)await run();
  else{renderTodayOver();toast('등록했습니다. 상태 원문을 붙여넣고 계산하면 투입률에 반영됩니다.');await autoShareAfterRun();}
 }catch(error){fail(error);}
}
async function deleteManual(index){
 const tr=document.querySelectorAll('#exclusion-rows tr')[index];if(!tr)return;logChange('수기 추가 삭제 · '+tr.querySelector('[data-col=name]').value.trim());
 for(const col of ['name','start','end'])tr.querySelector('[data-col='+col+']').value='';
 autoMarkUnsaved();state.dirty=true;renderManualList();
 if($('raw').value.trim())await run();else{renderTodayOver();await autoShareAfterRun();}
}
if($('manual-add')){
 $('manual-add').addEventListener('click',registerManual);
 for(const id of ['manual-name','manual-start','manual-end'])$(id).addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Enter'){event.preventDefault();registerManual();}});
 $('manual-list').addEventListener('click',event=>{const b=event.target.closest('.manual-del');if(b)deleteManual(Number(b.dataset.i));});
 renderManualList();
}
/* 과투입 예상 구간 (오늘) */
function renderTodayOver(){
 const box=$('over-list'),sum=$('over-summary');if(!box||!sum)return;
 const wrap=$('over-box');
 if(!state.daily){box.textContent='데일리를 읽으면 자동으로 계산됩니다.';sum.textContent='—';sum.className='trainee-summary zero';wrap?.classList.add('hidden');return;}
 let fc;try{fc=E.forecastDay(state.daily,{training:trainingNames(lines('training'),traineesFromUI(),state.daily.date),exclusions:fields(),exceptions:lines('exceptions'),adjust:state.adjust||[]});}
 catch(error){box.textContent='예상 계산 실패: '+(error.message||error);sum.textContent='확인';sum.className='trainee-summary';wrap?.classList.remove('hidden');return;}
 state.forecastToday=fc;
 const now=E.businessClock(new Date().toISOString()),minHour=state.daily.date===now.date?now.hour:5;
 const over=fc.hours.filter(h=>h.hour>=minHour&&h.status==='과투입'),groups=[];
 for(const h of over){const last=groups[groups.length-1];if(last&&last[last.length-1].hour===h.hour-1)last.push(h);else groups.push([h]);}
 wrap?.classList.toggle('hidden',!over.length);sum.textContent=over.length?over.length+'개 시간대':'없음';sum.className='trainee-summary'+(over.length?'':' zero');
 box.innerHTML=groups.length?groups.map(g=>`<div class="over-item"><b>${hourLabel(g[0].hour)}~${hourLabel(g[g.length-1].hour+1)}시</b><span>${g.map(h=>`${hourLabel(h.hour)}시 ${format(h.remove,0)}분`).join(' · ')} 빼야 함</span></div>`).join(''):'<div class="tiny">현재 시간 이후 과투입 예상 구간이 없습니다.</div>';
}
/* 향후 7일 예상 */
function renderForecast(){
 const box=$('forecast-body');if(!box)return;
 if(!state.daily){box.innerHTML='<div class="tiny">데일리를 먼저 불러오세요.</div>';return;}
 const got=Array.isArray(state.daily.forecast)?state.daily.forecast:[],list=[];
 list.push(todayFcItem());for(let n=1;n<=9;n++){const d=new Date(Date.parse(state.daily.date+'T00:00:00Z')+n*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10);list.push(got.find(x=>x.date===d)||{date:d,sourceSheet:'',error:'대기',hourly:[]});}
 if(!got.length)$('forecast-sub').textContent='데일리 스크립트를 +7일 버전으로 바꾸면 표시됩니다.';
 else $('forecast-sub').textContent='기준 데일리 '+state.daily.date+' · 오늘 포함 10일 · 4일 넘는 날은 아래 스크롤 · 아직 시트가 없는 날은 일정 대기중';
 box.innerHTML=list.map(day=>{
  const dateText=day.date.slice(5).replace('-','/');
  const dow='일월화수목금토'[new Date(day.date+'T00:00:00Z').getUTCDay()]+(day.today?' · 오늘':'');
  if(day.error||!day.hourly.length){const wait=day.error==='대기'||day.error==='시트 없음'||!day.hourly.length&&!day.error;return `<div class="fc-card wait"><div class="fc-head"><b>${esc(dateText)} (${dow})</b><small>${wait?'데일리 시트가 아직 없습니다':esc(day.error)}</small></div><div class="fc-wait">${wait?'일정 대기중':'자료 확인 필요'}</div></div>`;}
  let add=0,remove=0,nu=0,no=0,maxTotal=0,maxHours=0;
  const rows=day.hourly.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.hour-b.hour).map(r=>{
   const target=Number(r.target),count=Number(r.dailyCount);
   if(!(target>0)||!Number.isFinite(count))return `<tr><td>${hourLabel(r.hour)}시</td><td>${esc(r.target??'—')}</td><td>${esc(r.dailyCount??'—')}</td><td>—</td></tr>`;
   let m;try{m=E.metrics(count*60,target);}catch{return `<tr><td>${hourLabel(r.hour)}시</td><td>${format(target)}</td><td>${format(count,0)}</td><td>—</td></tr>`;}
   if(m.add>0){add+=m.add;nu++;}if(m.remove>0){remove+=m.remove;no++;}
   const maxN=otToMax(count*60,m.upper);if(maxN>0){maxTotal+=maxN;maxHours++;}
   const cls=m.add>0?'under':m.remove>0?'over':'',room=Math.max(0,Math.floor(m.upper-count*60)),slack=Math.max(0,Math.floor(count*60-m.lower));
   const main=m.add>0?`미달 −${format(m.add,0)}분`:m.remove>0?`과투입 +${format(m.remove,0)}분`:'정상';
   const sub=m.add>0?`<small>최대 +${room}분</small>`:m.remove>0?`<small>미달까지 −${slack}분</small>`:`<small>최대 +${room}분</small><small>미달까지 −${slack}분</small>`;
   return `<tr class="${cls}"><td>${hourLabel(r.hour)}시</td><td>${format(target)}</td><td>${format(count,0)}</td><td class="fc-judge"><b>${main}</b>${sub}</td><td class="fc-max">${maxN>0?maxN+'명':'—'}</td></tr>`;
  }).join('');
  return `<div class="fc-card"><div class="fc-head"><div class="fc-title"><b>${esc(dateText)} (${dow})</b><button type="button" class="report-btn fc-recruit" data-date="${esc(day.date)}">OT 모집 문구</button></div><div class="fc-copy"><button type="button" class="report-btn fc-xls" data-date="${esc(day.date)}" title="시작시간·확보 인원 24줄">엑셀용 복사</button><button type="button" class="report-btn fc-xls-n" data-date="${esc(day.date)}" title="확보 인원 24줄">인원만 복사</button></div><small>미달 ${nu}개 시간대 · 부족 ${format(add,0)}분 / 과투입 ${no}개 · 초과 ${format(remove,0)}분</small><small>최대치까지 OT ${maxHours}개 시간대 · 총 ${Math.round(maxTotal*10)/10}명</small>${day.hasOT?`<small>복사 기준: OT 명단 ${Math.round(day.hourly.reduce((n,r)=>n+(Number(r.otCount)||0),0)*10)/10}명 뺀 상태</small>`:'<small>복사 기준: 데일리 인원 (OT 명단 없음)</small>'}</div><table class="fc-table"><thead><tr><th>시간</th><th>목표</th><th>인원</th><th>판정 · 여유</th><th>최대치</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div>`;
 }).join('');
}
/* 이번달 교육인원 */
function eduKey(name){return String(name||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').toLowerCase();}
function lookupPerson(text){
 const q=String(text||'').trim();if(!q||!state.daily)return null;const up=q.toUpperCase(),k=eduKey(q),list=state.daily.schedule||[];
 return list.find(r=>String(r.id||'').trim().toUpperCase()===up)||list.find(r=>eduKey(r.name)===k)||null;
}
function shiftSpan(shift){const m=[...String(shift||'').matchAll(/(\d{1,2}):?(\d{2})/g)];if(m.length<2)return null;const a=Number(m[0][1])*60+Number(m[0][2]),b=Number(m[1][1])*60+Number(m[1][2]);return ((b-a)%1440+1440)%1440;}
function shiftKind(row){if(!row)return '';if(/\(\s*209\s*\)/.test(row.shift||''))return '8H';if(/\(\s*84\s*\)/.test(row.shift||''))return '주말';if(/\(\s*105\s*\)/.test(row.shift||''))return '기타';return shiftSpan(row.shift)===540?'8H':'기타';}
function setEduEntries(list){
 const first=new Date(Date.parse(bizDate().slice(0,7)+'-01T00:00:00Z'));first.setUTCMonth(first.getUTCMonth()-1);const keep=first.toISOString().slice(0,10);
 state.edu=(Array.isArray(list)?list:[]).filter(r=>r&&r.name&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(r.date)&&r.date>=keep).map(r=>({name:String(r.name),date:r.date,hours:Number(r.hours)||8,kind:['8H','주말','기타'].includes(r.kind)?r.kind:''}));
 if(state.view==='edu')renderEdu();
}
function parseEduReport(text){
 const lines=String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n').map(v=>v.replace(/ /g,' ').trim()).filter(Boolean);
 const idRe=/\b(?:cssp-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|[Kk](?:[Ee])?\d+)\b/,durRe=/^(?:(\d+)\s*일\s*)?(?:(\d+)\s*시(?:간)?\s*)?(?:(\d+)\s*분\s*)?(?:(\d+)\s*초)?$/,dayOnly=/^(\d+)\s*일$/;
 const teams=[];let team=null,cur=null;
 for(const line of lines){
  if(/팀$/.test(line)&&!idRe.test(line)&&line.length<=20&&!/탭|옵션/.test(line)){team={team:line,people:[]};teams.push(team);cur=null;continue;}
  const m=line.match(idRe);
  if(!team&&m){team={team:'',people:[]};teams.push(team);}
  if(!team)continue;
  if(m){const id=m[0];if(cur&&cur.seconds===null&&cur.id.toLowerCase()===id.toLowerCase())continue;if(cur&&cur.seconds===null)team.people.pop();cur={id,seconds:null,days:null};team.people.push(cur);continue;}
  if(!cur)continue;
  const d=line.match(dayOnly);if(d&&cur.seconds!==null){cur.days=Number(d[1]);continue;}
  const t=line.match(durRe);if(t&&(t[1]||t[2]||t[3]||t[4]))cur.seconds=(cur.seconds||0)+Number(t[1]||0)*86400+Number(t[2]||0)*3600+Number(t[3]||0)*60+Number(t[4]||0);
 }
 return teams.map(tm=>{const map=new Map();for(const p of tm.people){if(p.seconds===null)continue;const k=p.id.toLowerCase(),o=map.get(k);if(o){o.seconds+=p.seconds;if(p.days!==null)o.days=(o.days||0)+p.days;}else map.set(k,{...p});}return {team:tm.team,people:[...map.values()]};}).filter(tm=>tm.people.length);
}
// 교육 인원은 날짜·월이 바뀌어도 지우지 않고, 새로 붙여넣을 때까지 유지합니다 (이 PC에도 보관).
function eduReportStore(r){try{if(r)localStorage.setItem('ginjo-edu-report',JSON.stringify(r));else localStorage.removeItem('ginjo-edu-report');}catch{}}
function eduReportLocal(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ginjo-edu-report')||'null');return v&&Array.isArray(v.teams)?v:null;}catch{return null;}}
function setEduReport(r){
 const cur=state.eduReport||eduReportLocal();
 const ok=r&&Array.isArray(r.teams)?{month:r.month||'',pastedAt:r.pastedAt||'',kinds:r.kinds||{},leaders:r.leaders||{},teams:r.teams}:null;
 if(ok&&(!cur||String(ok.pastedAt)>=String(cur.pastedAt||'')))state.eduReport=ok;else state.eduReport=cur||null;
 eduReportStore(state.eduReport);
 if(state.view==='edu')renderEdu();
}
setTimeout(()=>{if(!state.eduReport){const l=eduReportLocal();if(l){state.eduReport=l;if(state.view==='edu')renderEdu();}}},300);
function eduHM(sec){sec=Math.max(0,Math.round(sec));const h=Math.floor(sec/3600),m=Math.floor((sec%3600)/60);return h+'시간 '+String(m).padStart(2,'0')+'분';}
const EDU_LEADERS=['유진','예은','문옥','병정','승연','진아','가람'];
function leaderGroup(text){const v=String(text||'').trim();if(!v)return '미지정';return EDU_LEADERS.find(n=>v.includes(n))||v;}
function renderEdu(){
 const box=$('edu-teams');if(!box)return;
 const r=state.eduReport;
 if(!r){$('edu-summary').innerHTML='';box.innerHTML='<p class="tiny">아직 이번달 교육시간을 붙여넣지 않았습니다.</p>';$('edu-note').textContent='붙여넣으면 바로 계산·저장됩니다.';return;}
 const sched=state.daily?.schedule||[];
 const find=id=>sched.find(x=>String(x.id||'').trim().toLowerCase()===id.toLowerCase());
 const seen=new Map();for(const tm of r.teams)for(const p of tm.people){const k=p.id.toLowerCase(),o=seen.get(k);if(o){o.seconds+=p.seconds;}else seen.set(k,{...p});}
 const groups=new Map(EDU_LEADERS.map(n=>[n,[]]));let people=0,total=0,over=0,unknown=0;
 for(const p of seen.values()){
  const row=find(p.id),k=p.id.toLowerCase(),leader=leaderGroup(row?.leader||(r.leaders||{})[k]),auto=shiftKind(row),kind=r.kinds[k]||auto,left=(kind==='기타'?12:16)*3600-p.seconds;
  if(!groups.has(leader))groups.set(leader,[]);groups.get(leader).push({p,row,k,auto,kind,left});
  people++;total+=p.seconds;if(!kind)unknown++;else if(left<0)over++;
 }
 if(!groups.get('미지정')?.length)groups.delete('미지정');else{const v=groups.get('미지정');groups.delete('미지정');groups.set('미지정',v);}
 let mine='';try{mine=localStorage.getItem('ginjo-edu-team')||'';}catch{}
 if(!groups.has(mine))mine='';
 const pick=`<div class="edu-pick"><b>팀장 선택</b><select id="edu-team-pick" aria-label="팀장 선택"><option value="">팀장을 고르세요</option>${[...groups.keys()].map(n=>`<option value="${esc(n)}"${n===mine?' selected':''}>${esc(n)}${n==='미지정'?'':' 팀'}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>`;
 const card=(name,isMine)=>{
  const rows=(groups.get(name)||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.kind?a.left:1e9)-(b.kind?b.left:1e9));
  const tTotal=rows.reduce((n,x)=>n+x.p.seconds,0),tOver=rows.filter(x=>x.kind&&x.left<0).length;
  const body=rows.length?`<table class="edu-table"><thead><tr><th>인원</th><th>구분</th><th>교육</th><th>남은 시간</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(x=>`<tr class="${x.kind&&x.left<0?'over':x.kind&&x.left<=3600?'near':''}"><td><b>${esc(x.row?.name||x.p.id)}</b><small>${x.row?esc(x.p.id):''}${x.row?.shift?' · '+esc(x.row.shift):x.row?'':'오늘 데일리 없음'}</small></td><td><select class="edu-kind" data-key="${esc(x.k)}" aria-label="교육 구분"><option value="">${x.auto?x.auto+'·자동':'구분?'}</option><option value="8H"${r.kinds[x.k]==='8H'?' selected':''}>8H</option><option value="주말"${r.kinds[x.k]==='주말'?' selected':''}>주말</option><option value="기타"${r.kinds[x.k]==='기타'?' selected':''}>기타</option></select></td><td>${eduHM(x.p.seconds)}</td><td>${x.kind?`<span class="edu-left ${x.left<0?'bad':x.left<=3600?'warn':'ok'}">${x.left<0?'초과 '+eduHM(-x.left):eduHM(x.left)}</span>`:'—'}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`:'<p class="tiny" style="padding:10px 12px">이번달 교육 인원 없음</p>';
  return `<div class="edu-team${isMine?' mine':''}">${isMine?pick:''}<div class="edu-team-head"><b>${esc(name)}${name==='미지정'?' (데일리 I열 확인)':' 팀'}</b><small class="${tOver?'bad':''}">${rows.length}명 · ${eduHM(tTotal)}${tOver?' · 초과 '+tOver+'명':''}</small></div>${body}</div>`;
 };
 box.innerHTML=(mine?card(mine,true):`<div class="edu-team mine">${pick}<p class="tiny" style="padding:12px">팀장을 고르면 그 팀 교육 현황이 여기에 먼저 나옵니다. 이 PC에 기억합니다.</p></div>`)+[...groups.keys()].filter(n=>n!==mine).map(n=>card(n,false)).join('');
 $('edu-team-pick').addEventListener('change',e=>{try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-edu-team',e.target.value);}catch{}renderEdu();syncLeaderSelect();renderOverview();});
