 $('edu-summary').innerHTML=`<div class="box"><small>${esc(r.month)} 교육 인원</small><b>${people}명</b></div><div class="box"><small>이번달 총 교육시간</small><b>${eduHM(total)}</b></div><div class="box ${over?'bad':'good'}"><small>한도 초과${unknown?' · 구분 확인 '+unknown+'명':''}</small><b>${over}명</b></div>`;
 {const near=eduNearList(),nb=$('edu-notice');nb.innerHTML=near.length?`<span class="notice-item edu">교육 한도 임박·초과 · ${near.map(x=>esc(x.name)+' '+(x.left<0?'초과 '+eduHM(-x.left):'남음 '+eduHM(x.left))).join(', ')}</span>`:'';nb.classList.toggle('hidden',!near.length);}
 const at=Date.parse(r.pastedAt);$('edu-note').textContent='마지막 붙여넣기 '+(Number.isFinite(at)?new Date(at+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(5,16).replace('T',' '):'—')+' · 새로 붙여넣으면 덮어씁니다.';
}
async function applyEduRaw(){
 const text=$('edu-raw').value;if(!text.trim())return;
 const teams=parseEduReport(text);
 if(!teams.length){toast('사번과 교육시간을 찾지 못했습니다. 화면 전체를 복사했는지 확인하세요.');return;}
 const kinds=state.eduReport&&state.eduReport.month===bizDate().slice(0,7)?state.eduReport.kinds:{};
 const leaders=state.eduReport&&state.eduReport.month===bizDate().slice(0,7)?{...(state.eduReport.leaders||{})}:{};
 for(const tm of teams)for(const p of tm.people){const row=(state.daily?.schedule||[]).find(x=>String(x.id||'').trim().toLowerCase()===p.id.toLowerCase());if(row&&row.leader)leaders[p.id.toLowerCase()]=row.leader;}
 state.eduReport={month:bizDate().slice(0,7),pastedAt:new Date().toISOString(),kinds,leaders,teams};eduReportStore(state.eduReport);
 $('edu-raw').value='';autoMarkUnsaved();renderEdu();try{renderTraineeSoon();}catch{}toast(teams.reduce((n,t)=>n+t.people.length,0)+'명 교육시간을 반영했습니다.');
 await autoShareAfterRun();
}
if($('edu-raw')){
 $('edu-raw').addEventListener('paste',()=>setTimeout(()=>{logChange('교육 인원 붙여넣기');applyEduRaw();},60));
 $('edu-raw').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'&&e.ctrlKey){e.preventDefault();applyEduRaw();}});
 $('edu-clear').addEventListener('click',async()=>{if(!state.eduReport)return;state.eduReport=null;eduReportStore(null);autoMarkUnsaved();renderEdu();await autoShareAfterRun();});
 $('edu-teams').addEventListener('change',async event=>{const s=event.target.closest('.edu-kind');if(!s||!state.eduReport)return;if(s.value)state.eduReport.kinds[s.dataset.key]=s.value;else delete state.eduReport.kinds[s.dataset.key];eduReportStore(state.eduReport);autoMarkUnsaved();renderEdu();await autoShareAfterRun();});
}
/* 05:00 운영일 변경 시 일일 입력 초기화 */
function resetDayInputs(message){
 $('use-now').checked=true;state.adjust=[];try{renderAdjustList();}catch{}
 $('raw').value='';state.rawSample=false;setFields([]);$('training').value='';$('exceptions').value='';$('use-now').checked=true;fillTime();
 state.result=null;state.dirty=false;state.statusFromShared=false;AUTO.unsaved=false;AUTO.savedAt=0;state.inputDay=bizDate();
 try{sessionStorage.removeItem('ginjo-ui');}catch{}
 render();if(APP_CONFIG.sharedEnabled)sharedStatus(message);toast(message);
}
function checkDayRollover(){try{if(state.inputDay&&state.inputDay!==bizDate())resetDayInputs('05:00 운영일이 바뀌어 상태 원문·수기 추가·예외 입력을 초기화했습니다. 신입 교육 기간과 교육 인원 기록은 유지됩니다.');}catch{}}
function tryAdvanceDaily(){try{const d=state.daily;if(!d||state.demo||!d.next)return false;const bd=bizDate();if(!(d.date<bd)||d.next.date!==bd)return false;
 const pk={type:'ginjo-daily-blocks',version:1,date:d.next.date,capturedAt:d.next.capturedAt||d.capturedAt,sourceSheet:d.next.sourceSheet,blocks:d.next.blocks,forecast:d.next.forecast||[],advanced:true};
 receive(pk,false);toast('05:00 · 미리 받아 둔 '+bd.slice(5).replace('-','/')+' 데일리로 바로 넘어갔습니다. 새 데일리가 오면 자동으로 바뀝니다.');return true;}catch(e){console.error(e);return false;}}
setInterval(()=>{tryAdvanceDaily();checkDayRollover();},5000);setTimeout(tryAdvanceDaily,1500);

/* ===== 보고 복사 · 탭 제목 · 원문 오래됨 · 붙여넣기 자동 계산 · 수기 추가 미리보기 ===== */
const BASE_TITLE=document.title;
function rawAgeMin(){const r=state.result;if(!r||state.demo)return null;const ms=Date.parse(r.at);return Number.isFinite(ms)?(Date.now()-ms)/60000:null;}
function updateTitleAndStale(){
 const c=state.result?.current;
 if(!c)document.title=BASE_TITLE;
 else{const pct=percentage(c.rate)+'%';document.title=c.status==='미달'?'⚠ '+pct+' 미달 · '+BASE_TITLE:c.status==='과투입'?'⚠ '+pct+' 과투입 · '+BASE_TITLE:pct+' 정상 · '+BASE_TITLE;}
 const badge=$('stale-badge');if(!badge)return;const age=rawAgeMin();
 if(age===null||age<10){badge.className='stale-badge hidden';badge.textContent='';return;}
 badge.className='stale-badge '+(age>=20?'bad':'warn');badge.textContent='상태 원문 '+Math.floor(age)+'분 경과 · 새 원문을 붙여넣으세요';
}
setInterval(updateTitleAndStale,30000);setInterval(()=>{try{renderTraineeSoon();}catch{}},30000);
function buildReport(){
 const r=state.result,c=r?.current,n=r?.next;if(!r||!c)return '계산 결과가 없습니다. 상태 원문을 붙여넣고 계산하세요.';
 const secs=v=>{const m=String(v??'').match(/(\d+):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);return !m?0:m[3]!==undefined?Number(m[1])*3600+Number(m[2])*60+Number(m[3]):Number(m[1])*60+Number(m[2]);};
 const nm=list=>list.map(p=>p.name||p.id).join(', ');
 const hh=h=>String(h%24).padStart(2,'0');
 const L=[];
 L.push(`[${hh(r.hour)}~${hh(r.hour+1)}시 투입 현황] 기준 ${new Date(Date.parse(r.at)+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16)}`);
 L.push(`· 실제 ${percentage(c.rate)}% (${c.status}) · `+(c.status==='미달'?`${format(c.add,0)}분 부족`:c.status==='과투입'?`${format(c.remove,0)}분 초과`:`${c.untilLow}분 빠지면 미달 / ${c.untilHigh}분 더하면 과투입`));
 if(n)L.push(`· 다음 ${hh(n.hour)}~${hh(n.hour+1)}시 예상 ${percentage(n.rate)}% (${n.status})`+(n.add?` · ${format(n.add,0)}분 확보 필요`:n.remove?` · ${format(n.remove,0)}분 제외 필요`:''));
 const late=r.missing||[],extra=r.off||[];
 L.push(`· 지각 체크 필요 ${late.length}명`+(late.length?` (${nm(late)})`:''));
 L.push(`· 미출근시간 확인 필요 ${extra.length}명`+(extra.length?` (${nm(extra)})`:''));
 const people=r.people||[],meal=people.filter(p=>p.status==='식사'&&secs(p.duration)>=3600),rest=people.filter(p=>p.status==='휴식'&&secs(p.duration)>=600);
 if(meal.length)L.push(`· 식사 60분 이상 ${meal.length}명 (${nm(meal)})`);
 if(rest.length)L.push(`· 휴식 10분 이상 ${rest.length}명 (${nm(rest)})`);
 const fc=state.forecastToday;if(fc){const over=fc.hours.filter(h=>h.hour>r.hour&&h.status==='과투입');if(over.length)L.push(`· 과투입 예상 ${over.map(h=>hh(h.hour)+'시 '+format(h.remove,0)+'분').join(', ')}`);}
 const manual=fields().filter(x=>x.name);if(manual.length)L.push(`· 수기 추가 ${manual.map(x=>x.name+' '+x.start.replace(':','')+'~'+x.end.replace(':','')).join(', ')}`);
 return L.join('\n');
}
if($('report-btn')){
 $('report-btn').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('report-text').value=buildReport();$('report-dialog').showModal();$('report-text').focus();});
 $('report-close').addEventListener('click',()=>$('report-dialog').close());
 $('report-copy').addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText($('report-text').value);toast('보고 내용을 복사했습니다.');$('report-dialog').close();}catch{$('report-text').select();toast('Ctrl+C로 복사하세요.');}});
}
$('raw').addEventListener('paste',()=>{setTimeout(()=>{if(!$('raw').value.trim())return;logChange('상태 원문 붙여넣기');state.rawSample=false;state.dirty=true;useCurrentStatusTime();autoMarkUnsaved();run();},60);});
/* 수기 추가 미리보기 */
function previewManual(){
 const out=$('pv-result');out.classList.remove('hidden');
 try{
  if(!state.daily)throw Error('데일리를 먼저 불러오세요.');
  const name=$('manual-name').value.trim(),start=ginjoNormalizeClock($('manual-start').value),end=ginjoNormalizeClock($('manual-end').value),ok=v=>/^(?:[01]?\d|2[0-8]):[0-5]\d$/.test(v)||v==='29:00';
  if(!name)throw Error('이름 또는 사번을 입력하세요.');if(!ok(start)||!ok(end)||start===end)throw Error('시작·종료를 1300처럼 입력하세요.');
  const base={training:trainingNames(lines('training'),traineesFromUI(),state.daily.date),exceptions:lines('exceptions'),adjust:state.adjust||[]},cur=fields(),extra={name,start,end};
  const before=E.forecastDay(state.daily,{...base,exclusions:cur}),after=E.forecastDay(state.daily,{...base,exclusions:[...cur,extra]});
  const map=new Map(before.hours.map(h=>[h.hour,h])),rows=[];
  for(const a of after.hours){const b=map.get(a.hour);if(b&&Math.abs(b.minutes-a.minutes)>0.5)rows.push({hour:a.hour,b,a});}
  if(!rows.length)throw Error(name+'은(는) 그 시간에 근무 예정이 없어 변화가 없습니다. 이름·시간을 확인하세요.');
  let currentRow='',curBad=false;
  if(state.result&&$('raw').value.trim()){try{const r1=E.calculate(state.daily,$('raw').value,{at:state.result.at,exclusions:cur,...base}),r2=E.calculate(state.daily,$('raw').value,{at:state.result.at,exclusions:[...cur,extra],...base});if(Math.abs(r1.current.minutes-r2.current.minutes)>0.5){curBad=r2.current.status!=='정상'&&r1.current.status==='정상';}if(Math.abs(r1.current.minutes-r2.current.minutes)>0.5)currentRow=`<tr class="${r2.current.status!=='정상'&&r1.current.status==='정상'?'down':''}"><td>${hourLabel(r1.hour)}시 (지금 실제)</td><td>${percentage(r1.current.rate)}% ${r1.current.status}</td><td>${percentage(r2.current.rate)}% ${r2.current.status}</td></tr>`;}catch{}}
  const bad=curBad||rows.some(x=>x.a.status!=='정상'&&x.b.status==='정상');
  out.innerHTML=`<table class="pv-table"><thead><tr><th>시간</th><th>지금 예상</th><th>빼면</th></tr></thead><tbody>${currentRow}${rows.map(x=>`<tr class="${x.a.status!=='정상'&&x.b.status==='정상'?'down':''}"><td>${hourLabel(x.hour)}시</td><td>${percentage(x.b.rate)}% ${x.b.status}</td><td>${percentage(x.a.rate)}% ${x.a.status}${x.a.status==='미달'?' ('+format(x.a.add,0)+'분 부족)':''}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table><div class="pv-actions">${bad?'<span class="pill red">빼면 미달 또는 과투입이 되는 시간이 있습니다</span>':'<span class="pill green">빼도 정상 범위입니다</span>'}<button type="button" class="primary" id="pv-register">이대로 수기 추가 등록</button></div><div class="tiny">시간대 예상은 근무표·OT 기준입니다. 지금 실제 행은 현재 상태 원문 기준입니다.</div>`;
  $('pv-register').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('manual-name').value=name;$('manual-start').value=start.replace(':','');$('manual-end').value=end.replace(':','');registerManual();});
 }catch(error){out.textContent=error.message||String(error);}
}
if($('pv-run'))$('pv-run').addEventListener('click',previewManual);
/* 60분 미만 OT 미리보기 */
function previewAdjust(){
 const out=$('pv2-result');out.classList.remove('hidden');
 try{
  if(!state.daily)throw Error('데일리를 먼저 불러오세요.');
  const name=$('adj-name').value.trim(),start=ginjoNormalizeClock($('adj-start').value),end=ginjoNormalizeClock($('adj-end').value),ok=v=>/^(?:[01]?\d|2[0-8]):[0-5]\d$/.test(v)||v==='29:00';
  if(!name)throw Error('이름 또는 사번을 입력하세요.');
  if(!ok(start)||!ok(end)||start===end)throw Error('시작·종료를 1800처럼 입력하세요.');
  const found=(state.daily.schedule||[]).filter(r=>String(r.id||'').trim().toUpperCase()===name.toUpperCase()||String(r.name||'').replace(/\s+/g,'')===name.replace(/\s+/g,''));
  if(new Set(found.map(r=>String(r.idKey??r.id).toLowerCase())).size!==1)throw Error('데일리에서 '+name+'을(를) 한 명으로 찾지 못했습니다. 동명이인은 사번을 입력하세요.');
  const toMin=v=>{const [h,m]=v.split(':').map(Number);return h*60+m;},len=((toMin(end)-toMin(start))%1440+1440)%1440;
  if(len>=60)throw Error('60분보다 짧게 입력하세요. 60분 이상이면 데일리 OT 그대로 두면 됩니다.');
  const extra={name,start,end},adj=state.adjust||[];
  if(adj.some(r=>adjKey(r)===adjKey(extra)))throw Error('이미 등록된 시간입니다.');
  const base={training:trainingNames(lines('training'),traineesFromUI(),state.daily.date),exceptions:lines('exceptions')},cur=fields();
  const before=E.forecastDay(state.daily,{...base,exclusions:cur,adjust:adj}),after=E.forecastDay(state.daily,{...base,exclusions:cur,adjust:[...adj,extra]});
  const map=new Map(before.hours.map(h=>[h.hour,h])),rows=[];
  for(const a of after.hours){const b=map.get(a.hour);if(b&&Math.abs(b.minutes-a.minutes)>0.5)rows.push({hour:a.hour,b,a});}
  let currentRows='',bad=false;
  const hasRaw=Boolean(state.result&&$('raw').value.trim());
  if(hasRaw){
   try{
    const r1=E.calculate(state.daily,$('raw').value,{at:state.result.at,exclusions:cur,...base,adjust:adj}),r2=E.calculate(state.daily,$('raw').value,{at:state.result.at,exclusions:cur,...base,adjust:[...adj,extra]});
    const line=(label,x,y)=>{const worse=y.status!=='정상'&&x.status==='정상';if(worse)bad=true;return `<tr class="${worse?'down':''}"><td>${label}</td><td>${percentage(x.rate)}% ${x.status}</td><td>${percentage(y.rate)}% ${y.status}${y.status==='미달'?' ('+format(y.add,0)+'분 부족)':''}</td></tr>`;};
    if(Math.abs(r1.current.minutes-r2.current.minutes)>0.5)currentRows+=line(hourLabel(r1.hour)+'시 (지금 실제)',r1.current,r2.current);
    if(r1.next&&r2.next&&Math.abs(r1.next.minutes-r2.next.minutes)>0.5)currentRows+=line(hourLabel(r1.hour+1)+'시 (다음 시간 예상)',r1.next,r2.next);
   }catch{}
  }
  if(!rows.length&&!currentRows)throw Error(hasRaw?'등록해도 투입률에 변화가 없습니다. 60분 미만 OT는 그 시간에 실제 근무·로그인이 확인된 경우에만 보정됩니다. 이름·시간을 확인하세요.':'상태 원문을 붙여넣고 계산한 뒤에 미리볼 수 있습니다.');
  const bodyRows=currentRows+rows.map(x=>{const worse=x.a.status!=='정상'&&x.b.status==='정상';if(worse)bad=true;return `<tr class="${worse?'down':''}"><td>${hourLabel(x.hour)}시</td><td>${percentage(x.b.rate)}% ${x.b.status}</td><td>${percentage(x.a.rate)}% ${x.a.status}${x.a.status==='미달'?' ('+format(x.a.add,0)+'분 부족)':''}</td></tr>`;}).join('');
  out.innerHTML=`<table class="pv-table"><thead><tr><th>시간</th><th>지금</th><th>등록하면</th></tr></thead><tbody>${bodyRows}</tbody></table><div class="pv-actions">${bad?'<span class="pill red">등록하면 미달 또는 과투입이 되는 시간이 있습니다</span>':'<span class="pill green">등록해도 정상 범위입니다</span>'}<button type="button" class="primary" id="pv2-register">이대로 등록</button></div>`;
  $('pv2-register').addEventListener('click',()=>registerAdjust());
 }catch(error){out.textContent=error.message||String(error);}
}
if($('pv2-run'))$('pv2-run').addEventListener('click',previewAdjust);


/* ===== 투입률 공유 (시간대별 누적·수정) ===== */
function hourLogKey(date){return 'ginjo-hourlog-'+date;}
function loadHourLog(date){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(hourLogKey(date))||'null');if(v&&typeof v==='object')return {auto:v.auto||{},edit:v.edit||{},autoAt:v.autoAt||{},editAt:v.editAt||{}};}catch{}return {auto:{},edit:{},autoAt:{},editAt:{}};}
/* 투입률 공유 기록을 공유 저장에 함께 넣고, 받으면 시간대별로 더 최신 것을 남깁니다. */
function hourLogSharedPart(){
 const date=state.daily?.date;if(!date)return null;const v=loadHourLog(date),auto={},edit={};
 for(const [h,x]of Object.entries(v.auto))if(x)auto[h]=[String(x).slice(0,300),Number(v.autoAt[h])||0];
 for(const [h,x]of Object.entries(v.edit))edit[h]=[String(x??'').slice(0,300),Number(v.editAt[h])||0];
 return Object.keys(auto).length||Object.keys(edit).length?{date,auto,edit}:null;
}
function mergeHourLog(shared){
 if(!shared||typeof shared!=='object'||!shared.date)return;const v=loadHourLog(shared.date);let n=0;
 for(const [h,[x,at]]of Object.entries(shared.auto||{})){if(!v.auto[h]||at>(Number(v.autoAt[h])||0)){if(v.auto[h]!==x)n++;v.auto[h]=x;v.autoAt[h]=at;}}
 for(const [h,[x,at]]of Object.entries(shared.edit||{})){if(at>(Number(v.editAt[h])||0)||(!(h in v.edit)&&x)){if(v.edit[h]!==x)n++;v.edit[h]=x;v.editAt[h]=at;}}
 saveHourLog(shared.date,v);return n;
}
function saveHourLog(date,log){try{localStorage.setItem(hourLogKey(date),JSON.stringify(log));for(let i=localStorage.length-1;i>=0;i--){const k=localStorage.key(i);if(k&&k.startsWith('ginjo-hourlog-')&&k<hourLogKey(new Date(Date.parse(date+'T00:00:00Z')-7*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10)))localStorage.removeItem(k);}}catch{}}
function hourLabel2(h){return '('+String(h%24).padStart(2,'0')+'~'+String((h+1)%24).padStart(2,'0')+')';}
function hourLogLine(result){
 const c=result.current,miss=result.missing||[],people=result.people||[];
 const otIn=people.filter(p=>p.ot&&p.present).length,parts=[];
 if(c.status!=='정상')parts.push(c.status+' '+percentage(c.rate)+'%');
 for(const m of miss)parts.push((m.name||m.id)+(m.ot?' OT 미출근':' 미출근'));
 const ot=otIn?' / OT '+otIn+'명 출근':'';
 if(result.shiftCount===0||c.dailyCount===0){const otMiss=miss.filter(m=>m.ot).map(m=>'OT '+(m.name||m.id)+' 미출근');return hourLabel2(result.hour)+' 시프트없음'+(otMiss.length?' / '+otMiss.join(', '):'')+ot;}
 return hourLabel2(result.hour)+(parts.length?' 결점 있음 / '+parts.join(', ')+ot:' 결점 없음'+ot);
}
function renderHourLog(){
 const list=$('log-list');if(!list)return;
 const r=state.result,date=r?.date||state.daily?.date;
 if(!date||!state.daily){list.innerHTML='<p class="tiny">데일리를 불러오면 표시됩니다.</p>';$('log-head').textContent='—';return;}
 const log=loadHourLog(date);
 if(r&&!state.demo&&r.date===date){const line=hourLogLine(r),at=Date.parse(r.at)||Date.now();if(log.auto[r.hour]!==line&&at>=(Number(log.autoAt[r.hour])||0)){log.auto[r.hour]=line;log.autoAt[r.hour]=at;saveHourLog(date,log);}}
 if(r&&state.demo)log.auto[r.hour]=hourLogLine(r);
 const now=E.businessClock(new Date().toISOString()),last=Math.max(r&&r.date===date?r.hour:5,now.date===date?now.hour:28);
 const hasShift=h=>{const row=(state.daily.hourly||[]).find(x=>x.hour===h);return row&&Number(row.target)>0;};
 const rows=[];for(let h=5;h<=Math.min(28,last);h++){
  const auto=log.auto[h],edit=log.edit[h]||undefined,text=edit??auto??(hasShift(h)?hourLabel2(h)+' (미확인)':hourLabel2(h)+' 시프트 없음');
  const cls=edit!==undefined?'edited':!auto&&!hasShift(h)?'none':/결점 있음/.test(text)?'bad':!auto?'none':'';
  rows.push(`<div data-h="${h}" class="log-line ${cls}" contenteditable="plaintext-only" spellcheck="false" role="textbox" title="직접 고칠 수 있습니다. 지우면 자동 문구로 돌아갑니다." aria-label="${hourLabel2(h)} 투입률 공유 문구">${esc(text)}</div>`);
 }
 const hh=String(((r&&r.date===date?r.hour:now.hour)%24)).padStart(2,'0');
 $('log-head').textContent=`${date.slice(5,7)}/${date.slice(8,10)} SM ${hh}시 투입률 공유드립니다. · [${APP_CONFIG.full}]`;
 const focused=document.activeElement&&document.activeElement.closest&&document.activeElement.closest('#log-list')?document.activeElement.dataset.h:null;
 if(focused!==null&&focused!==undefined)return;
 list.innerHTML=rows.join('');list.scrollTop=list.scrollHeight;
}
function hourLogText(){
 const head=$('log-head').textContent.split(' · ')[0];
 return head+'\n\n['+APP_CONFIG.full+']\n'+[...document.querySelectorAll('#log-list .log-line')].map(i=>i.textContent.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim()).join('\n');
}
if($('log-list')){
 $('log-list').addEventListener('focusin',event=>{const el=event.target.closest('.log-line');if(el)el.dataset.before=el.textContent;});
 $('log-list').addEventListener('focusout',event=>{
  const el=event.target.closest('.log-line');if(!el)return;const date=state.result?.date||state.daily?.date;if(!date)return;
  const v=el.textContent.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();if(v===el.dataset.before)return;
  const log=loadHourLog(date),h=el.dataset.h;logChange('투입률 공유 문구 수정 · '+String(Number(h)%24).padStart(2,'0')+'시');log.edit[h]=v;log.editAt[h]=Date.now();saveHourLog(date,log);
  clearTimeout(window.__logShareTimer);window.__logShareTimer=setTimeout(()=>{autoMarkUnsaved();autoShareAfterRun();},1500);
 });
 $('log-list').addEventListener('input',()=>{AUTO.lastInput=Date.now();});
 $('log-list').addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Enter'){event.preventDefault();event.target.blur();}});
 $('log-list').addEventListener('focusout',()=>setTimeout(()=>{if(!document.activeElement?.closest?.('#log-list'))renderHourLog();},50));
 $('log-copy').addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(hourLogText());toast('투입률 공유 문구를 복사했습니다.');}catch{$('report-text').value=hourLogText();$('report-dialog').showModal();toast('Ctrl+C로 복사하세요.');}});
}

/* ===== 정각·30분 알림 · 팀장별 보기 · 신입 교육 종료 임박 · 교육 한도 임박 · OT 현황 · OT 모집 문구 ===== */
/* 3) 정각·30분 알림 */
let slotAlert=null;
function slotNow(){const c=E.businessClock(new Date().toISOString()),m=new Date(Date.now()+9*3600000).getUTCMinutes();return {key:c.date+'-'+c.hour+'-'+(m<30?0:30),start:Date.now()-((m%30)*60000)-new Date().getUTCSeconds()*1000};}
function checkSlotAlert(){
 if(!state.daily||state.demo&&!slotAlert)return;
 const s=slotNow(),r=state.result,fresh=r&&Date.parse(r.at)>=s.start-60000;
 if(fresh){if(slotAlert)closeSlotAlert();return;}
 let seen='';try{seen=sessionStorage.getItem('ginjo-slot-dismiss')||'';}catch{}
 if(seen===s.key)return;
 if(!slotAlert||slotAlert.key!==s.key){slotAlert={key:s.key};const box=$('slot-alert');const hm=new Date(s.start+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16);box.querySelector('span').textContent=hm+' · 새 상태 원문을 붙여넣어 주세요';box.classList.remove('hidden');}
}
function closeSlotAlert(dismiss){if(dismiss&&slotAlert){try{sessionStorage.setItem('ginjo-slot-dismiss',slotAlert.key);}catch{}}slotAlert=null;$('slot-alert').classList.add('hidden');updateTitleAndStale();}
let titleBlink=false;setInterval(()=>{if(!slotAlert)return;titleBlink=!titleBlink;if(titleBlink)document.title='🔔 원문 붙여넣기 시간';else updateTitleAndStale();},1200);
setInterval(checkSlotAlert,15000);setTimeout(checkSlotAlert,3000);
$('slot-alert-close').addEventListener('click',()=>closeSlotAlert(true));
$('slot-alert-go').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('raw').focus();$('raw').select();});

/* 5) 팀장별 보기 */
const LEADERS=['유진','예은','문옥','병정','승연','진아','가람'];
function leaderOfId(id){const k=String(id||'').toLowerCase(),row=(state.daily?.schedule||[]).find(x=>String(x.idKey??x.id).toLowerCase()===k);if(!row||!row.leader)return '';return LEADERS.find(n=>row.leader.includes(n))||row.leader;}
function currentLeader(){try{return localStorage.getItem('ginjo-edu-team')||'';}catch{return '';}}
function leaderFiltered(result){
 const lead=currentLeader();if(!result||!lead||!LEADERS.includes(lead))return result;
 const ok=r=>leaderOfId(r.idKey??r.id)===lead;
 return {...result,people:(result.people||[]).filter(ok),missing:(result.missing||[]).filter(ok),off:(result.off||[]).filter(ok),diagnostics:result.diagnostics};
}
function syncLeaderSelect(){const s=$('leader-filter');if(!s)return;const v=currentLeader();s.value=LEADERS.includes(v)?v:'';}
$('leader-filter').addEventListener('change',e=>{try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-edu-team',e.target.value);}catch{}renderOverview();if(state.view==='edu')renderEdu();});
syncLeaderSelect();

/* 6) 신입 교육 종료 임박 */
function renderTraineeSoon(){
 const box=$('notice-strip');if(!box)return;
 const today=traineeToday(),soon=traineesFromUI().map(t=>({...t,s:traineeStatus(t,today)})).filter(t=>t.s.k==='soon');
 const items=[];
 if(soon.length)items.push(`<span class="notice-item trainee">신입 교육 종료 임박 · ${soon.map(t=>esc(t.name)+' '+(dayDiff(t.end,today)===0?'오늘':'D-'+dayDiff(t.end,today))+' ('+t.end.slice(5).replace('-','/')+')').join(', ')}</span>`);
 const cap=Date.parse(state.daily?.capturedAt||'');if(APP_CONFIG.embedded&&Number.isFinite(cap)){const age=Math.floor((Date.now()-cap)/60000);if(age>=10)items.unshift(`<span class="notice-item stale">데일리 갱신이 ${age}분째 멈춤 · Power Automate 데일리 플로우 실행 기록을 확인하세요 (원본 생성 ${new Date(cap+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16)})</span>`);}
 try{const late=otLateList();if(late.length)items.unshift(`<span class="notice-item otlate">${esc(otLateText(late))}</span>`);const nw=nextHourWarn();if(nw)items.unshift(`<span class="notice-item nexthour${nw.over?' over':''}">⏰ ${esc(nw.text)}</span>`);}catch{}
 box.innerHTML=items.join('');box.classList.toggle('hidden',!items.length);
}
/* 7) 교육 한도 임박 */
function eduLeftMap(){
 const r=state.eduReport,map=new Map();if(!r)return map;
 const sched=state.daily?.schedule||[];
 for(const tm of r.teams)for(const p of tm.people){const k=p.id.toLowerCase(),row=sched.find(x=>String(x.id||'').trim().toLowerCase()===k),kind=r.kinds[k]||shiftKind(row);if(!kind)continue;const o=map.get(k),sec=(o?o.sec:0)+p.seconds;map.set(k,{sec,kind,name:row?.name||p.id,left:(kind==='기타'?12:16)*3600-sec});}
 return map;
}
function eduNearList(){return [...eduLeftMap().values()].filter(x=>x.left<=3600).sort((a,b)=>a.left-b.left);}
function eduTagFor(id){const x=eduLeftMap().get(String(id||'').toLowerCase());if(!x||x.left>3600)return '';return x.left<0?'<span class="pill red">한도 초과</span>':'<span class="pill amber">한도 임박</span>';}

/* 8) OT 현황 */
function otKey(date){return 'ginjo-ot-'+date;}
function loadOT(date){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(otKey(date))||'null');if(v)return {seen:v.seen||{},done:v.done||{},man:v.man||{}};}catch{}return {seen:{},done:{},man:{}};}
function saveOT(date,v){try{localStorage.setItem(otKey(date),JSON.stringify(v));}catch{}}
function otHourOf(minute){const h=Math.floor(minute/60);return h<5?h+24:h;}
function otPlan(){
 const d=state.daily;if(!d)return [];
 const sched=d.schedule||[],byHour=new Map();
 const resolve=name=>{const q=String(name).trim(),up=q.toUpperCase(),k=q.replace(/\s+/g,'');let r=sched.find(x=>String(x.id||'').trim().toUpperCase()===up)||sched.find(x=>String(x.name||'').replace(/\s+/g,'')===k);if(!r&&k.length>=2){const c=sched.filter(x=>{const n=String(x.name||'').replace(/\s+/g,'');return n.length>=2&&(k.includes(n)||n.includes(k));});if(new Set(c.map(x=>String(x.idKey??x.id).toLowerCase())).size===1)r=c[0];}return r?{id:r.id,key:String(r.idKey??r.id).toLowerCase(),name:r.name||r.id,shift:r.shift,leader:leaderOfId(r.idKey??r.id)}:{id:q,key:q.toLowerCase(),name:q,shift:'',leader:'',unknown:true};};
 for(const row of d.ot||[]){if(!(row.names||[]).length)continue;const h=otHourOf(row.minute);if(!byHour.has(h))byHour.set(h,new Map());for(const n of row.names||[]){const e=E.otEntry(n,row.minute),p=resolve(e.name);if(e.partial){const hm=m=>String(Math.floor(m/60)%24).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(m%60).padStart(2,'0');p.otLabel=e.start===row.minute?e.len+'분':hm(e.start)+'~'+hm(e.start+e.len);}byHour.get(h).set(p.key,p);}}
 return [...byHour.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]).map(([hour,m])=>({hour,people:[...m.values()]}));
}
function recordOTSeen(){
 const r=state.result;if(!r||state.demo||!state.daily||r.date!==state.daily.date)return;
 const v=loadOT(r.date);let changed=false;
 for(const p of r.people||[])if(p.ot&&p.present){const k=r.hour+'|'+String(p.idKey??p.id).toLowerCase();if(!v.seen[k]){v.seen[k]=Date.parse(r.at)||Date.now();changed=true;}}
 if(changed)saveOT(r.date,v);
}
function mergeOT(date,shared){
 if(!shared||typeof shared!=='object')return;const v=loadOT(date);
 for(const [k,ts]of Object.entries(shared.seen||{}))if(!v.seen[k])v.seen[k]=ts;
 let changed=0;for(const [k,ts]of Object.entries(shared.done||{}))if(!v.done[k]||Math.abs(ts)>Math.abs(v.done[k])){v.done[k]=ts;changed++;}
 for(const [k,ts]of Object.entries(shared.man||{}))if(!v.man[k]||Math.abs(ts)>Math.abs(v.man[k])){v.man[k]=ts;changed++;}
 saveOT(date,v);if(Object.keys(shared.done||{}).length||Object.keys(shared.seen||{}).length||Object.keys(shared.man||{}).length){try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-ot-rx-'+date,String(Date.now()));}catch{}}
}
function otSharedPart(){const date=state.daily?.date;if(!date)return null;const v=loadOT(date);const seen={},done={},man={};for(const [k,x]of Object.entries(v.seen).slice(0,600))seen[k]=x;for(const [k,x]of Object.entries(v.done).slice(0,600))done[k]=x;for(const [k,x]of Object.entries(v.man||{}).slice(0,600))man[k]=x;return Object.keys(seen).length||Object.keys(done).length||Object.keys(man).length?{date,seen,done,...(Object.keys(man).length?{man}:{})}:null;}
function renderOT(){
 const box=$('ot-body');if(!box)return;
 const d=state.daily;if(!d){box.innerHTML='<p class="tiny">데일리를 먼저 불러오세요.</p>';return;}
 recordOTSeen();try{renderOtLateBox();}catch{}
 const plan=otPlan(),v=loadOT(d.date),pend=otPendingGet(),r=state.result,lead=currentLeader(),now=E.businessClock(new Date().toISOString());
 const curHour=d.date===now.date?now.hour:(d.date<now.date?99:0);
 const presentNow=new Set((r&&r.date===d.date?r.people||[]:[]).filter(p=>p.present).map(p=>String(p.idKey??p.id).toLowerCase()));
 let cWait=0,cWork=0,cDone=0,cFuture=0;
 const rows=plan.map(({hour,people})=>{
  const list=people;
  const wait=[],work=[],done=[],future=[];
  for(const p of list){const k=hour+'|'+p.key,st=k in pend?otPendTo(pend[k]):otStoredState(v,k,hour===curHour&&presentNow.has(p.key));p._man=(v.man[k]||0)>0||pend[k]==='work';
   if(st==='done')done.push(p);else if(st==='work')work.push(p);else if(hour>curHour)future.push(p);else wait.push(p);}
  cWait+=wait.length;cWork+=work.length;cDone+=done.length;cFuture+=future.length;
  const chip=(p,kind)=>`<span class="ot-chip ${kind}${(hour+'|'+p.key) in pend?' pending':''}" title="${esc((p.id||'')+' · '+(p.shift||'')+(p.leader?' · '+p.leader+' 팀':''))}">${esc(p.name)}${p.otLabel?' <small class="ot-part">'+esc(p.otLabel)+'</small>':''}${kind==='wait'||kind==='future'?`<button type="button" class="ot-step" data-k="${esc(hour+'|'+p.key)}" data-to="work" title="왔음 → 실제 와서 근무로">✓</button>`:kind==='work'?`<button type="button" class="ot-do ot-step" data-k="${esc(hour+'|'+p.key)}" data-to="done" title="OT 취합 완료로">✓</button>${p._man?`<button type="button" class="ot-undo ot-step" data-k="${esc(hour+'|'+p.key)}" data-to="wait" title="안 옴으로 되돌리기">↺</button>`:''}`:kind==='done'?`<button type="button" class="ot-undo ot-step" data-k="${esc(hour+'|'+p.key)}" data-to="work" title="완료 취소 → 실제 와서 근무로">↺</button>`:''}</span>`;
  const lbl=String(hour%24).padStart(2,'0')+'~'+String((hour+1)%24).padStart(2,'0')+'시';
  return `<tr class="${hour===curHour?'now':''}"><td><b>${lbl}</b><small>${list.length}명${hour===curHour?' · 지금':''}</small></td><td>${future.length?future.map(p=>chip(p,'future')).join(''):''}${wait.map(p=>chip(p,'wait')).join('')||(future.length?'':'<span class="tiny">—</span>')}</td><td>${work.map(p=>chip(p,'work')).join('')||'<span class="tiny">—</span>'}${work.length?`<button type="button" class="ot-all" data-h="${hour}" title="이 시간 근무 확인 인원을 모두 취합 완료로">모두 완료</button>`:''}</td><td>${done.map(p=>chip(p,'done')).join('')||'<span class="tiny">—</span>'}</td></tr>`;
 });
 {const n=Object.keys(pend).length;$('ot-pending').textContent=n?'저장 안 된 변경 '+n+'건':otRetry?'공유 저장 안 됨 · 다시 저장하세요':'';$('ot-save').disabled=!n&&!otRetry;$('ot-save').textContent=!n&&otRetry?'다시 저장':'저장';
  if(!/^저장 (실패|\d)/.test($('ot-sync').textContent)){let rx=0,sv=0;try{rx=Number(localStorage.getItem('ginjo-ot-rx-'+d.date)||0);sv=Number(localStorage.getItem('ginjo-ot-saved-'+d.date)||0);}catch{}const hm=ms=>new Date(ms+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16);$('ot-sync').textContent=(sv?'내 저장 '+hm(sv):'내 저장 없음')+' · '+(rx?'공유 반영 '+hm(rx):'공유 반영 없음');}}
 $('ot-summary').innerHTML=`<div class="box"><small>아직 안 온 사람 (지난·현재 시간)</small><b>${cWait}명</b>${cFuture?` <span class="tiny">+ 예정 ${cFuture}명</span>`:''}</div><div class="box"><small>실제 와서 근무한 사람</small><b>${cWork}명</b></div><div class="box good"><small>OT 취합 완료</small><b>${cDone}명</b></div>`;
 $('ot-sub').textContent=`운영일 ${d.date} · 데일리 OT 기준 · 근무 확인은 계산할 때 OT 시간에 로그인한 기록입니다.`;
 box.innerHTML=plan.length?`<table class="data ot-table"><thead><tr><th>시간</th><th>아직 안 온 사람 / 예정</th><th>실제 와서 근무</th><th>OT 취합 완료</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.join('')}</tbody></table>`:'<p class="tiny">오늘 데일리에 OT 인원이 없습니다.</p>';
}
function otPendingGet(){try{const v=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('ginjo-ot-pending')||'null');if(v&&v.date===state.daily?.date)return v.map||{};}catch{}return {};}
function otPendingSet(map){try{sessionStorage.setItem('ginjo-ot-pending',JSON.stringify({date:state.daily?.date,map}));}catch{}}
function otPendTo(x){return x===true?'done':x===false?'work':x;}
function otStoredState(v,k,presentNowHit){if((v.done[k]||0)>0)return 'done';const m=v.man[k]||0,auto=v.seen[k]||(presentNowHit?Date.now():0);if(m>0)return 'work';if(m<0)return auto&&Number(auto)>-m?'work':'wait';return auto?'work':'wait';}
function setOTState(keys,to){
 const p=otPendingGet(),v=loadOT(state.daily?.date||''),r=state.result,now=E.businessClock(new Date().toISOString());
 const present=new Set((r&&r.date===state.daily?.date?r.people||[]:[]).filter(x=>x.present).map(x=>String(x.idKey??x.id).toLowerCase()));
 for(const k of keys){const [h,id]=k.split('|');const stored=otStoredState(v,k,Number(h)===now.hour&&present.has(id));if(stored===to)delete p[k];else p[k]=to;}
 otPendingSet(p);AUTO.lastInput=Date.now();renderOT();
}
function setOTDone(keys,done){setOTState(keys,done?'done':'work');}
async function saveOTPending(){
 const date=state.daily?.date;if(!date)return;const p=otPendingGet(),keys=Object.keys(p);if(!keys.length&&!otRetry)return;
 const v=loadOT(date),ts=Date.now();for(const k of keys){const to=otPendTo(p[k]);if(to==='done'){v.done[k]=ts;}else{if((v.done[k]||0)>0)v.done[k]=-ts;v.man[k]=to==='work'?ts:-ts;}}saveOT(date,v);otPendingSet({});if(keys.length)logChange('OT 취합 저장 · '+keys.map(k=>{const [h,id]=k.split('|');const row=(state.daily.schedule||[]).find(x=>String(x.idKey??x.id).toLowerCase()===String(id).toLowerCase());return (row?.name||id)+'('+String(Number(h)%24).padStart(2,'0')+'시 '+({done:'완료',work:'근무 확인',wait:'안 옴'}[otPendTo(p[k])]||'')+')';}).join(', '));
 try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-ot-saved-'+date,String(ts));}catch{}
 if($('raw').value.trim()&&!state.result){try{calculate();}catch{}}
 autoMarkUnsaved();renderOT();
 const before=$('shared-status')?.textContent||'';await autoShareAfterRun();const after=$('shared-status')?.textContent||'';
 const ok=after!==before&&/보냈습니다/.test(after);
 $('ot-sync').textContent=ok?'저장 '+new Date(ts+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16)+' · 다른 화면 반영까지 1~2분':'저장 실패 · '+(after.replace(/^자동 저장 실패: /,'')||'공유 저장 폴더(③) 연결을 확인하세요');
 $('ot-sync').style.color=ok?'':'#b54340';otRetry=!ok;renderOT();
}
let otRetry=false;
$('ot-save').addEventListener('click',saveOTPending);
$('ot-body').addEventListener('click',event=>{
 const s=event.target.closest('.ot-step'),all=event.target.closest('.ot-all');
 if(s)setOTState([s.dataset.k],s.dataset.to);
 else if(all){const keys=[...all.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.ot-do')].map(b=>b.dataset.k);setOTState(keys,'done');}
});

/* 9) OT 모집 문구 */
// OT는 상한(최대 허용률) 바로 아래까지 모집합니다. 0.1명(6분) 단위로, 상한을 넘지 않게 내림합니다.
function otToMax(minutes,upper){const room=upper-minutes;if(!(room>0))return 0;let n=Math.floor(room/6)/10;if(minutes+n*60>=upper-1e-9)n=Math.round((n-0.1)*10)/10;return Math.max(0,n);}
// OT 모집 문구: 최대치 1명 이상 시간대만, 소수점 내림. 05~08시(5·6·7시)와 27~29시(27·28시)는 문구에서 뺍니다.
const OT_TEXT_SKIP=[5,6,7,27,28];
function otRecruitText(days){
 const out=['['+APP_CONFIG.full+' OT모집]'];
 for(const day of days){
  if(day.error||!day.hourly.length)continue;
  const dow='일월화수목금토'[new Date(day.date+'T00:00:00Z').getUTCDay()],lines=[];
  for(const r of day.hourly.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.hour-b.hour)){const target=Number(r.target),count=Number(r.dailyCount);if(!(target>0)||!Number.isFinite(count))continue;let m;try{m=E.metrics(count*60,target);}catch{continue;}if(OT_TEXT_SKIP.includes(r.hour))continue;const n=Math.floor(otToMax(count*60,m.upper));if(n>=1)lines.push(`${String(r.hour%24).padStart(2,'0')}~${String((r.hour+1)%24).padStart(2,'0')}시 ${n}명`);}
  out.push('',`${day.date.slice(5,7)}/${day.date.slice(8,10)} (${dow})`,...(lines.length?lines:['모집 필요 없음']));
 }
 return out.length>1?out.join('\n'):'['+APP_CONFIG.full+' OT모집]\n\n모집이 필요한 날이 없습니다.';
}
function openRecruit(days){$('report-dialog').querySelector('h2').textContent='OT 모집 문구';$('report-text').value=otRecruitText(days);$('report-dialog').showModal();}
$('forecast-body').addEventListener('click',async event=>{
 const x=event.target.closest('.fc-xls,.fc-xls-n');if(x){const day=otSecDays().find(d=>d.date===x.dataset.date);if(!day)return;const text=x.classList.contains('fc-xls')?otSecTSV(day.date,false):otSecRows(day).map(r=>r.n).join('\n');try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);toast(day.date.slice(5).replace('-','/')+' '+(x.classList.contains('fc-xls')?'시작시간·확보 인원':'확보 인원')+' 24줄을 복사했습니다'+(day.hasOT?' (OT 없는 상태 기준).':' · 데일리 스크립트가 OT 명단을 아직 안 보내서 데일리 인원 기준입니다.'));}catch{$('report-dialog').querySelector('h2').textContent='OT 확보 (엑셀용)';$('report-text').value=text;$('report-dialog').showModal();}return;}
 const b=event.target.closest('.fc-recruit');if(!b)return;const day=[todayFcItem(),...(state.daily?.forecast||[])].find(x=>x.date===b.dataset.date);if(day)openRecruit([day]);});
$('fc-recruit-all').addEventListener('click',()=>openRecruit((state.daily?.forecast||[]).slice(0,9)));
$('report-btn').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('report-dialog').querySelector('h2').textContent='보고 복사';},true);

/* ===== 20261118.49 공유 입력 합치기(덮어쓰기 방지) =====
   항목마다 마지막으로 바꾼 시각을 기록해 공유 저장에 같이 보냅니다.
   받으면 수기 추가·신입 교육은 사람(줄)별로, 원문·일반 예외·수기 신입 교육·이번달 교육은 칸별로 더 최근 것을 남깁니다. */
function stampsKey(d){return 'ginjo-stamps-'+d;}
function loadStamps(d){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(stampsKey(d))||'null');if(v)return {ex:v.ex||{},tr:v.tr||{},aj:v.aj||{},at:v.at||{},h:v.h||{}};}catch{}return {ex:{},tr:{},aj:{},at:{},h:{}};}
function saveStamps(d,s){try{localStorage.setItem(stampsKey(d),JSON.stringify(s));for(let i=localStorage.length-1;i>=0;i--){const k=localStorage.key(i);if(k&&k.startsWith('ginjo-stamps-')&&k<stampsKey(new Date(Date.parse(d+'T00:00:00Z')-3*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10)))localStorage.removeItem(k);}}catch{}}
function exKey(r){return [String(r.name||'').trim().toLowerCase(),ginjoNormalizeClock(r.start||''),ginjoNormalizeClock(r.end||'')].join('|');}
function trKey(r){return [String(r.name||'').trim().toLowerCase(),r.start||'',r.end||''].join('|');}
const MERGE_FIELDS=['raw','training','exceptions','eduReport'];
function fieldHash(f,v){return JSON.stringify(f==='raw'?String(v||'').replace(/\r\n?/g,'\n').trim():f==='eduReport'?(v?{m:v.month,t:v.teams,k:v.kinds,l:v.leaders}:null):(v||[]));}
function snapDom(){return {adjust:(state.adjust||[]).slice(),raw:$('raw').value,exclusions:fields().filter(r=>r.name),training:lines('training'),exceptions:lines('exceptions'),trainees:traineesFromUI(),eduReport:state.eduReport||null};}
function stampLocal(date,snap){
 if(!date)return null;const s=loadStamps(date),now=Date.now();
 const sync=(map,list,keyf)=>{const keys=new Set(list.map(keyf));for(const k of keys)if(!map[k]||!map[k][1])map[k]=[now,1];for(const [k,v]of Object.entries(map))if(v[1]&&!keys.has(k))map[k]=[now,0];};
 sync(s.ex,snap.exclusions,exKey);sync(s.tr,snap.trainees,trKey);sync(s.aj,snap.adjust||[],adjKey);
 for(const f of MERGE_FIELDS){const h=fieldHash(f,snap[f]);if(s.h[f]!==h){s.h[f]=h;s.at[f]=now;}}
 saveStamps(date,s);return s;
}
function stampsForShare(){const d=state.daily?.date;if(!d)return null;const s=stampLocal(d,snapDom());return {ex:s.ex,tr:s.tr,aj:s.aj,at:s.at};}
function mergeInputs(L,Ls,S,Ss){
 Ss=Ss||{ex:{},tr:{},aj:{},at:{}};const out={},st={ex:{},tr:{},aj:{},at:{},h:{}};
 for(const f of MERGE_FIELDS){const la=Number(Ls.at?.[f])||0,sa=Number(Ss.at?.[f])||0,useS=sa>=la;out[f]=useS?S[f]:L[f];out[f+'From']=useS?'S':'L';st.at[f]=Math.max(la,sa);st.h[f]=fieldHash(f,out[f]);}
 const list=(lList,sList,lMap,sMap,keyf,name)=>{
  const items=new Map();for(const r of sList||[])items.set(keyf(r),r);for(const r of lList||[])if(!items.has(keyf(r)))items.set(keyf(r),r);
  const lHas=new Set((lList||[]).map(keyf)),sHas=new Set((sList||[]).map(keyf)),keys=new Set([...Object.keys(lMap||{}),...Object.keys(sMap||{}),...items.keys()]),res=[];
  for(const k of keys){const l=lMap?.[k],s=sMap?.[k],la=l?l[0]:0,sa=s?s[0]:0;let alive,at;
   if(sa>la){alive=s[1]===1;at=sa;}else if(la>sa){alive=l[1]===1;at=la;}else{alive=sHas.has(k)||(!s&&lHas.has(k))||(s?s[1]===1:false);at=la;}
   if(!items.has(k))alive=false;st[name][k]=[at,alive?1:0];if(alive)res.push(items.get(k));}
  return res;
 };
 out.exclusions=list(L.exclusions,S.exclusions,Ls.ex,Ss.ex,exKey,'ex');
 out.trainees=list(L.trainees,S.trainees,Ls.tr,Ss.tr,trKey,'tr');
 out.adjust=list(L.adjust||[],S.adjust||[],Ls.aj||{},Ss.aj||{},adjKey,'aj');
 return {values:out,stamps:st};
}
function applyMerged(v,S,p){
 if(v.rawFrom==='S'){$('raw').value=S.raw||'';if(S.calculationAt){fillTime(S.calculationAt);$('use-now').checked=false;}}
 else{$('raw').value=v.raw||'';if(p){$('date').value=p.date||$('date').value;$('time').value=p.time||$('time').value;$('use-now').checked=p.now!==false;}}
 state.rawSample=false;setFields(v.exclusions.slice(0,10));$('training').value=(v.training||[]).join('\n');$('exceptions').value=(v.exceptions||[]).join('\n');
 setTraineesUI(v.trainees||[],true);setEduReport(v.eduReport||null);state.adjust=(v.adjust||[]).slice(0,20);try{renderAdjustList();}catch{}
}
function adoptSharedStamps(date,Ss){
 const s=loadStamps(date);Ss=Ss||{};
 for(const n of ['ex','tr','aj']){s[n]=s[n]||{};for(const [k,v]of Object.entries(Ss[n]||{}))if(!s[n][k]||v[0]>s[n][k][0])s[n][k]=v;}
 for(const f of MERGE_FIELDS){if((Number(Ss.at?.[f])||0)>(Number(s.at[f])||0))s.at[f]=Number(Ss.at[f]);}
 const snap=snapDom();for(const f of MERGE_FIELDS)s.h[f]=fieldHash(f,snap[f]);
 const sync=(map,list,keyf)=>{const keys=new Set(list.map(keyf));for(const k of keys)if(!map[k])map[k]=[0,1];};sync(s.ex,snap.exclusions,exKey);sync(s.tr,snap.trainees,trKey);sync(s.aj,snap.adjust||[],adjKey);
 saveStamps(date,s);
}

/* 2) 공유 저장 연결 상태 */
async function refreshShareConn(){
 const chip=$('share-conn');if(!chip)return;
 if(!APP_CONFIG.sharedEnabled){chip.classList.add('hidden');return;}
 try{
  const h=AUTO.shareDir||await shareHandleGet();
  if(!h){chip.className='conn-chip bad';chip.textContent='공유 저장 연결 안 됨 · 연결하기';chip.dataset.act='connect';return;}
  const perm=await h.queryPermission({mode:'readwrite'});
  if(perm==='granted'){AUTO.shareDir=h;chip.className='conn-chip ok';chip.textContent='공유 저장 연결됨 · '+h.name;chip.dataset.act='';}
  else{chip.className='conn-chip warn';chip.textContent='공유 저장 권한 확인 필요 · 클릭';chip.dataset.act='perm';}
 }catch{chip.className='conn-chip bad';chip.textContent='공유 저장 연결 확인 실패 · 다시 연결';chip.dataset.act='connect';}
}
$('share-conn').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
 const act=$('share-conn').dataset.act;
 try{if(act==='perm'){const h=AUTO.shareDir||await shareHandleGet();if(h)await h.requestPermission({mode:'readwrite'});}else if(act==='connect')await shareConnect();}catch{}
 refreshShareConn();
});
setTimeout(refreshShareConn,800);setInterval(refreshShareConn,60000);

function renderSharedSrc(){
 const chip=$('shared-src');if(!chip)return;
 const S=state.lastSharedInput,hm=iso=>{const ms=Date.parse(iso);return Number.isFinite(ms)?new Date(ms+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16):'—';};
 if(!APP_CONFIG.sharedEnabled||!S||!state.daily||S.date!==state.daily.date){chip.className='conn-chip hidden';return;}
 const same=fieldHash('raw',$('raw').value)===fieldHash('raw',S.raw);
 if(same){chip.className='conn-chip ok';chip.textContent='공유 원문 '+hm(S.calculationAt)+' 기준';}
 else{chip.className='conn-chip warn';chip.textContent='내 화면 원문 사용 중 · 공유 원문 '+hm(S.calculationAt);}
}

/* ===== 60분 미만 OT (짧은 OT 확인 보정) =====
   데일리 OT에 이름이 있지만 실제로는 짧게만 한 인원을 팀장이 확인해 등록합니다.
   등록한 시각만 OT로 보고, 그 분만큼 투입률에 더하며, 그 시간에는 OT 미출근으로 띄우지 않습니다. */
state.adjust=state.adjust||[];
function adjKey(r){return [String(r.name||'').trim().toLowerCase(),ginjoNormalizeClock(r.start||''),ginjoNormalizeClock(r.end||'')].join('|');}
function renderAdjustList(){
 const box=$('adj-list');if(!box)return;
 box.innerHTML=state.adjust.length?state.adjust.map((r,i)=>`<div class="issue-item adj"><div><b>${esc(r.name)}</b> <span>${esc(String(r.start).replace(':',''))}~${esc(String(r.end).replace(':',''))}</span></div><button type="button" class="adj-del" data-i="${i}" aria-label="60분 미만 OT 삭제" title="삭제">×</button></div>`).join(''):'<div class="issue-empty">없음</div>';
 $('adj-count').textContent=state.adjust.length?state.adjust.length+'건':'';
}
async function registerAdjust(){
 try{
  const name=$('adj-name').value.trim(),start=ginjoNormalizeClock($('adj-start').value),end=ginjoNormalizeClock($('adj-end').value),ok=v=>/^(?:[01]?\d|2[0-8]):[0-5]\d$/.test(v)||v==='29:00';
  if(!name)throw Error('60분 미만 OT: 이름 또는 사번을 입력하세요.');
  if(!ok(start)||!ok(end)||start===end)throw Error('60분 미만 OT: 시작·종료를 1800처럼 입력하세요.');
  if(!state.daily)throw Error('데일리를 먼저 불러오세요.');
  const found=(state.daily.schedule||[]).filter(r=>String(r.id||'').trim().toUpperCase()===name.toUpperCase()||String(r.name||'').replace(/\s+/g,'')===name.replace(/\s+/g,''));
  if(new Set(found.map(r=>String(r.idKey??r.id).toLowerCase())).size!==1)throw Error('60분 미만 OT: 데일리에서 '+name+'을(를) 한 명으로 찾지 못했습니다. 동명이인은 사번을 입력하세요.');
  const toMin=v=>{const [h,m]=v.split(':').map(Number);return h*60+m;},len=((toMin(end)-toMin(start))%1440+1440)%1440;
  if(len>=60)throw Error('60분 미만 OT: 60분보다 짧게 입력하세요. 60분 이상이면 데일리 OT 그대로 두면 됩니다.');
  if(state.adjust.length>=20)throw Error('60분 미만 OT는 최대 20건입니다.');
  const row={name,start,end};if(state.adjust.some(r=>adjKey(r)===adjKey(row)))throw Error('이미 등록된 시간입니다.');
  state.adjust.push(row);logChange('60분 미만 OT 등록 · '+name+' '+start+'~'+end);$('adj-name').value='';$('adj-start').value='';$('adj-end').value='';
  autoMarkUnsaved();state.dirty=true;renderAdjustList();
  if($('raw').value.trim())await run();else{renderTodayOver();await autoShareAfterRun();}
 }catch(error){fail(error);}
}
async function deleteAdjust(i){const gone=state.adjust[i];if(gone)logChange('60분 미만 OT 삭제 · '+gone.name+' '+gone.start+'~'+gone.end);state.adjust.splice(i,1);autoMarkUnsaved();state.dirty=true;renderAdjustList();if($('raw').value.trim())await run();else{renderTodayOver();await autoShareAfterRun();}}
$('adj-add').addEventListener('click',registerAdjust);
for(const id of ['adj-name','adj-start','adj-end'])$(id).addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();registerAdjust();}});
$('adj-list').addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('.adj-del');if(b)deleteAdjust(Number(b.dataset.i));});
renderAdjustList();

$('trainee-save').addEventListener('click',async()=>{logChange('신입 교육 제외 저장');autoMarkUnsaved();state.dirty=true;if($('raw').value.trim())await run();else{renderTodayOver();await autoShareAfterRun();}toast('신입 교육 제외를 저장했습니다.');});

/* ===== OT 확보 (날짜별 시간대 확보 인원) =====
   05~08시(5·6·7시 구간): 데일리 1명 이상이면 0, 아무도 없으면 1명
   08~10시: 최대치까지 1명 이상 필요할 때만 1명
   24~29시: 미달일 때만 최소치까지, 60분(1명) 단위로 올림
   그 외: 최대치까지 인원, 소수점 내림(과투입 방지) */
function otSecureNeed(hour,target,count){
 if(!(target>0)||!Number.isFinite(count))return {n:0,rule:'목표 없음'};
 let m;try{m=E.metrics(count*60,target);}catch{return {n:0,rule:'계산 불가'};}
 if(hour>=5&&hour<=7)return count>=1?{n:0,rule:'1명 이상 출근'}:{n:1,rule:'출근 인원 없음'};
 if(hour===8||hour===9){const x=Math.floor(otToMax(count*60,m.upper));return x>=1?{n:1,rule:'1명 필요'}:{n:0,rule:'여유 없음'};}
 if(hour>=24)return m.add>0?{n:Math.ceil(m.add/60),rule:'미달 '+format(m.add,0)+'분 → 최소치'}:{n:0,rule:'미달 아님'};
 const x=Math.floor(otToMax(count*60,m.upper));return {n:x,rule:x?'최대치까지 '+otToMax(count*60,m.upper)+'명 → 내림':'최대치 여유 없음'};
}
function todayFcItem(){const d=state.daily;const cnt=new Map();for(const row of d.ot||[]){const h=Math.floor(Number(row.minute)/60);if(!Number.isFinite(h))continue;const hh=h<5?h+24:h;cnt.set(hh,(cnt.get(hh)||0)+(row.names||[]).length);}
 return {date:d.date,sourceSheet:d.sourceSheet||'',today:true,hasOT:true,hourly:(d.hourly||[]).map(r=>({...r,otCount:cnt.get(r.hour)||0}))};}
function otSecDays(){
 const d=state.daily;if(!d)return [];
 const days=[todayFcItem()];
 for(const f of d.forecast||[])if(!f.error&&f.hourly&&f.hourly.length)days.push({date:f.date,hourly:f.hourly,hasOT:f.hasOT});
 return days;
}
function otSecRows(day){
 const rows=[];for(let h=5;h<=28;h++){const r=day.hourly.find(x=>x.hour===h),target=Number(r?.target),daily=Number(r?.dailyCount),ot=Number(r?.otCount)||0,count=Number.isFinite(daily)?Math.max(0,daily-ot):daily,need=r?otSecureNeed(h,target,count):{n:0,rule:'자료 없음'};rows.push({daily,ot,hour:h,start:String(h).padStart(2,'0')+':00',label:String(h%24).padStart(2,'0')+'~'+String((h+1)%24).padStart(2,'0')+'시',target,count,...need});}
 return rows;
}
function otSecTSV(date,withHeader){const day=otSecDays().find(x=>x.date===date);if(!day)return '';const rows=otSecRows(day);return (withHeader?'시작시간\t확보 인원\n':'')+rows.map(r=>r.start+'\t'+r.n).join('\n');}

/* ===== 변경 기록 */
function changesKey(date){return 'ginjo-changes-'+date;}
function loadChanges(date){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(changesKey(date))||'[]');return Array.isArray(v)?v:[];}catch{return [];}}
function saveChanges(date,list){try{list.sort((a,b)=>b[0]-a[0]);localStorage.setItem(changesKey(date),JSON.stringify(list.slice(0,150)));for(let i=localStorage.length-1;i>=0;i--){const k=localStorage.key(i);if(k&&k.startsWith('ginjo-changes-')&&k<changesKey(new Date(Date.parse(date+'T00:00:00Z')-7*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10)))localStorage.removeItem(k);}}catch{}}
function myName(){try{return (localStorage.getItem('ginjo-me')||'').trim();}catch{return '';}}
function logChange(what){try{const date=state.daily?.date;if(!date)return;const list=loadChanges(date);list.unshift([Date.now(),myName()||'이름 미설정',String(what).slice(0,160)]);saveChanges(date,list);renderChanges();}catch{}}
function changesSharedPart(){const date=state.daily?.date;if(!date)return null;const list=loadChanges(date);return list.length?{date,list:list.slice(0,150)}:null;}
function mergeChanges(shared){if(!shared||shared.date!==state.daily?.date)return;const list=loadChanges(shared.date),seen=new Set(list.map(x=>x.join('|')));for(const x of shared.list||[])if(!seen.has(x.join('|'))){list.push(x);seen.add(x.join('|'));}saveChanges(shared.date,list);renderChanges();}
function renderChanges(){const box=$('changes-list');if(!box)return;const date=state.daily?.date;const list=date?loadChanges(date):[];$('changes-count').textContent=list.length?list.length+'건':'—';
 box.innerHTML=list.length?list.slice(0,80).map(([at,who,what])=>`<li><time>${new Date(at+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16)}</time><b>${esc(who)}</b><span>${esc(what)}</span></li>`).join(''):'<li><span></span><span></span><span class="tiny">오늘 변경 기록이 없습니다.</span></li>';
 const me=$('me-name');if(me&&document.activeElement!==me){me.value=myName();}if(me)me.classList.toggle('need',!myName());}
$('me-name').addEventListener('change',e=>{try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-me',e.target.value.trim());}catch{}renderChanges();});
setTimeout(renderChanges,1500);setInterval(renderChanges,30000);

/* ===== OT 3분 미출근 · 다음 시간 미리 경고 */
function otLateList(){
 const r=state.result,d=state.daily;if(!r||!d||r.date!==d.date||!Array.isArray(r.otEntries))return [];
 const now=E.businessClock(new Date().toISOString());if(now.date!==d.date)return [];
 const present=new Set((r.people||[]).filter(p=>p.present).map(p=>p.idKey)),out=[],seen=new Set();
 for(const o of r.otEntries){const el=((now.clock-o.start)%1440+1440)%1440;if(el<3||el>=o.len||present.has(o.id)||seen.has(o.id))continue;seen.add(o.id);out.push({...o,el});}
 return out;
}
function otLateText(list){const hm=m=>String(Math.floor(m/60)%24).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(m%60).padStart(2,'0');const src=state.result?.at?new Date(Date.parse(state.result.at)+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16):'';
 return 'OT 미출근 확인 · '+list.map(o=>o.name+' ('+hm(o.start)+' 시작, '+o.el+'분 지남)').join(', ')+(src?' · 원문 '+src+' 기준':'');}
function nextHourWarn(){
 const r=state.result,n=r?.next;if(!r||!n)return null;const now=E.businessClock(new Date().toISOString());
 if(now.minute<55||r.hour!==now.hour||r.date!==now.date||n.status==='정상')return null;
 const h=String(n.hour%24).padStart(2,'0')+'~'+String((n.hour+1)%24).padStart(2,'0');
 return n.status==='미달'?{over:false,text:`다음 시간(${h}) 미달 예상 · ${n.add}분 부족 (${percentage(n.rate)}%) · OT·복귀 인원 확인`}:{over:true,text:`다음 시간(${h}) 과투입 예상 · ${n.remove}분 초과 (${percentage(n.rate)}%) · 제외 가능 인원 확인`};
}
function renderOtLateBox(){const b=$('ot-late');if(!b)return;const l=otLateList();b.textContent=l.length?otLateText(l):'';b.classList.toggle('hidden',!l.length);}
setInterval(()=>{try{renderTraineeSoon();renderOtLateBox();}catch{}},20000);

/* ===== 화면 배치 (개인별 저장) */
const LAY_KEY='ginjo-layout-v1';
function layLoad(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LAY_KEY)||'null');return v&&typeof v==='object'?{order:v.order||{},hidden:Array.isArray(v.hidden)?v.hidden:[]}:{order:{},hidden:[]};}catch{return {order:{},hidden:[]};}}
function laySave(v){try{localStorage.setItem(LAY_KEY,JSON.stringify(v));}catch{}}
const LAY_NAMES={pgrid:'우선 확인 카드',away:'자리에 없음',training:'교육·식사·휴식',meal:'OT 인원',check:'지각·미출근 확인',people:'전체 상태 명단',preview:'수기 추가 미리보기',changes:'최근 변경 기록',history:'시간대 이력',trainee:'신입 교육',daycheck:'하루 전체 시프트 점검'};
function layContainers(){const board=document.querySelector('.operations-board'),grid=board?.querySelector('.priority-grid');return [['board',board],['grid',grid]].filter(x=>x[1]);}
function layTag(){
 const board=document.querySelector('.operations-board');if(!board)return;
 const g=board.querySelector('.priority-grid');if(g)g.dataset.lay='pgrid';
 const set=(sel,id)=>{const el=board.querySelector(sel);if(el&&!el.dataset.lay)el.dataset.lay=id;};
 set('.priority-away','away');set('.priority-training','training');set('.priority-meal','meal');set(':scope>.check-panel','check');set(':scope>#preview-board','preview');
 for(const d of board.querySelectorAll(':scope>details.secondary-board')){if(d.dataset.lay)continue;const s=d.querySelector('summary')?.textContent||'';if(s.includes('전체 상태 명단'))d.dataset.lay='people';else if(s.includes('하루 전체'))d.dataset.lay='daycheck';}
 for(const el of board.querySelectorAll('[data-lay]')){if(el.querySelector(':scope>.lay-bar,:scope>summary>.lay-bar'))continue;const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='lay-bar';bar.innerHTML=`<span>⠿ ${esc(LAY_NAMES[el.dataset.lay]||el.dataset.lay)}</span><button type="button" data-lay-hide>숨기기</button>`;const sm=el.tagName==='DETAILS'?el.querySelector(':scope>summary'):null;if(sm)sm.append(bar);else el.prepend(bar);}
}
function layApply(){
 layTag();const v=layLoad(),edit=document.querySelector('.operations-board')?.classList.contains('lay-edit');
 for(const [cid,box] of layContainers()){const ord=v.order[cid];if(!Array.isArray(ord))continue;const kids=[...box.querySelectorAll(':scope>[data-lay]')];const anchor=kids[kids.length-1]?.nextSibling||null;
  const sorted=kids.slice().sort((a,b)=>{const ia=ord.indexOf(a.dataset.lay),ib=ord.indexOf(b.dataset.lay);return (ia<0?999+kids.indexOf(a):ia)-(ib<0?999+kids.indexOf(b):ib);});
  for(const el of sorted)box.insertBefore(el,anchor);}
 for(const el of document.querySelectorAll('.operations-board [data-lay]')){const h=v.hidden.includes(el.dataset.lay);el.classList.toggle('lay-hidden',h&&!edit);el.classList.toggle('lay-off',h&&edit);const b=el.querySelector(':scope>.lay-bar [data-lay-hide],:scope>summary>.lay-bar [data-lay-hide]');if(b)b.textContent=h?'보이기':'숨기기';el.draggable=Boolean(edit);}
}
function layOrderNow(){const v=layLoad();for(const [cid,box] of layContainers())v.order[cid]=[...box.querySelectorAll(':scope>[data-lay]')].map(e=>e.dataset.lay);laySave(v);}
(function(){
 const board=document.querySelector('.operations-board');if(!board)return;let drag=null;
 const clear=()=>board.querySelectorAll('.lay-over-before,.lay-over-after').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('lay-over-before','lay-over-after'));
 $('lay-edit').addEventListener('click',()=>{const on=!board.classList.contains('lay-edit');board.classList.toggle('lay-edit',on);$('lay-edit').textContent=on?'배치 완료':'화면 배치';$('lay-reset').classList.toggle('hidden',!on);layApply();if(!on)toast('화면 배치를 이 PC에 저장했습니다.');});
 $('lay-reset').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{localStorage.removeItem(LAY_KEY);}catch{}location.reload();});
 board.addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-lay-hide]');if(!b)return;e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();const id=b.closest('[data-lay]').dataset.lay,v=layLoad();v.hidden=v.hidden.includes(id)?v.hidden.filter(x=>x!==id):[...v.hidden,id];laySave(v);layApply();},true);
 board.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(board.classList.contains('lay-edit')&&e.target.closest('[data-lay]>summary'))e.preventDefault();},true);
 board.addEventListener('dragstart',e=>{if(!board.classList.contains('lay-edit'))return;const el=e.target.closest?.('[data-lay]');if(!el)return;drag=el;el.classList.add('lay-dragging');e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='move';try{e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',el.dataset.lay);}catch{}e.stopPropagation();});
 board.addEventListener('dragend',()=>{drag?.classList.remove('lay-dragging');drag=null;clear();});
 const target=e=>{if(!drag)return null;let el=e.target.closest?.('[data-lay]');while(el&&el.parentElement!==drag.parentElement)el=el.parentElement?.closest('[data-lay]');return el&&el!==drag?el:null;};
 board.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{const el=target(e);if(!el)return;e.preventDefault();const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),horiz=el.parentElement.classList.contains('priority-grid'),after=horiz?e.clientX>r.left+r.width/2:e.clientY>r.top+r.height/2;clear();el.classList.add(after?'lay-over-after':'lay-over-before');});
 board.addEventListener('drop',e=>{const el=target(e);if(!el)return;e.preventDefault();const after=el.classList.contains('lay-over-after');el.parentElement.insertBefore(drag,after?el.nextSibling:el);clear();layOrderNow();});
 layApply();
})();

/* ===== 매크로 검색 */
function macroLoad(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ginjo-macros')||'[]');return Array.isArray(v)?v.filter(x=>x&&x.id):[];}catch{return [];}}
function macroSave(list){try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-macros',JSON.stringify(list));}catch{}}
function macroLive(){return macroLoad().filter(x=>!x.del);}
function macroSharedPart(){const cut=Date.now()-14*86400000;const l=macroLoad().filter(x=>!(x.del&&(x.at||0)<cut));return l.length?{macros:l.map(x=>[x.id,x.no,x.desc,x.at,x.del?1:0,x.cat||''])}:null;}
function mergeMacros(arr){if(!Array.isArray(arr))return;const map=new Map(macroLoad().map(x=>[x.id,x]));let ch=false;for(const r of arr){if(!Array.isArray(r))continue;const it={id:String(r[0]),no:String(r[1]??''),desc:String(r[2]??''),at:Number(r[3])||0,del:Boolean(r[4]),cat:String(r[5]??'')};const mine=map.get(it.id);if(!mine||it.at>mine.at){map.set(it.id,it);ch=true;}}if(ch){macroSave([...map.values()]);if(state.view==='macro')renderMacros();}}
function macroTerms(q){return String(q||'').toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);}
function macroHL(text,terms){let html=esc(text);for(const w of terms){const re=new RegExp(w.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&'),'gi');html=html.replace(/(<[^>]*>)|([^<]+)/g,(m,tag,txt)=>tag?tag:txt.replace(re,s=>`<mark>${s}</mark>`));}return html;}
let macroCat='';
const macroNone='__none';
function macroCatSort(a,b){return String(a).localeCompare(String(b),'ko');}
function macroHits(){
 const terms=macroTerms($('macro-q').value),all=macroLive();
 const inCat=x=>macroCat===''||(macroCat===macroNone?!(x.cat||'').trim():(x.cat||'').trim()===macroCat);
 const hit=all.filter(x=>inCat(x)&&terms.every(w=>(x.no+' '+x.desc+' '+(x.cat||'')).toLowerCase().includes(w))).sort((a,b)=>{const q=terms.join('');const ae=a.no.toLowerCase()===q?0:1,be=b.no.toLowerCase()===q?0:1;return ae-be||a.no.localeCompare(b.no,'ko',{numeric:true});});
 return {terms,all,hit};
}
function renderMacros(){const box=$('macro-list');if(!box)return;
 const {terms,all,hit}=macroHits();
 const counts=new Map();for(const x of all){const c=(x.cat||'').trim();counts.set(c,(counts.get(c)||0)+1);}
 if(macroCat!==''&&macroCat!==macroNone&&!counts.has(macroCat))macroCat='';
 const named=[...counts.keys()].filter(Boolean).sort(macroCatSort);
 $('macro-cat-list').innerHTML=named.map(c=>`<option value="${esc(c)}">`).join('');
 const bar=$('macro-cats');
 if(named.length){bar.classList.remove('hidden');bar.innerHTML=`<button type="button" class="macro-chip${macroCat===''?' on':''}" data-cat="">전체 <i>${all.length}</i></button>`+named.map(c=>`<button type="button" class="macro-chip${macroCat===c?' on':''}" data-cat="${esc(c)}">${esc(c)} <i>${counts.get(c)}</i></button>`).join('')+(counts.get('')?`<button type="button" class="macro-chip${macroCat===macroNone?' on':''}" data-cat="${macroNone}">미분류 <i>${counts.get('')}</i></button>`:'');}
 else{bar.classList.add('hidden');bar.innerHTML='';}
 const filtering=terms.length>0||macroCat!=='';
 const bb=$('macro-bulkcat');bb.classList.toggle('hidden',!(filtering&&hit.length));
 $('macro-bulkcat-n').textContent=filtering?`지금 보이는 ${hit.length}개를`:'';
 {const sub=$('macro-sub');if(sub){const d=globalThis.__mcDropped&&Date.now()-globalThis.__mcDropped<600000;sub.textContent=d?'매크로가 많아서 공유 용량이 부족해 최근 저장에서 매크로가 공유에서 빠졌습니다. 쓰지 않는 매크로를 삭제해 주세요.':'번호나 설명·키워드 한 단어만 입력해도 찾아요.';}}
 $('macro-count').textContent=filtering?`${hit.length} / ${all.length}`:`${all.length}개`;
 if(!all.length){box.innerHTML='<div class="macro-empty"><b>아직 등록된 매크로가 없어요</b>＋ 매크로 추가로 첫 매크로를 넣어보세요.</div>';return;}
 if(!hit.length){box.innerHTML=`<div class="macro-empty"><b>${terms.length?`"${esc($('macro-q').value)}" `:''}결과가 없어요</b>다른 단어로 찾아보거나 새로 추가하세요.</div>`;return;}
 const card=x=>`<div class="macro-card" data-id="${esc(x.id)}"><button type="button" class="macro-no" data-copy="${esc(x.no)}" title="눌러서 번호 복사">${macroHL(x.no,terms)}<span class="copy">복사</span></button><div class="macro-desc">${x.desc.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).map(w=>`<span>${macroHL(w,terms)}</span>`).join('')}</div><input class="macro-cat-in" list="macro-cat-list" maxlength="20" placeholder="대분류 지정" value="${esc(x.cat||'')}" title="대분류를 고르거나 새로 입력하고 Enter" autocomplete="off"><div class="macro-tools"><button type="button" data-edit>수정</button><button type="button" class="del" data-del>삭제</button></div></div>`;
 if(!filtering&&named.length){
  let html='',left=300;
  const groups=[...named.map(c=>[c,hit.filter(x=>(x.cat||'').trim()===c)]),['미분류',hit.filter(x=>!(x.cat||'').trim())]];
  for(const [c,list] of groups){if(!list.length||left<=0)continue;const part=list.slice(0,left);left-=part.length;html+=`<div class="macro-group"><b>${esc(c)}</b><small>${list.length}개</small></div>`+part.map(card).join('');}
  box.innerHTML=html;return;
 }
 box.innerHTML=hit.slice(0,300).map(card).join('');}
let macroEditId='';
function macroOpenForm(it){$('macro-save').disabled=false;$('macro-form-msg').style.color='';$('macro-bulk').classList.add('hidden');$('macro-form').classList.remove('hidden');macroEditId=it?.id||'';$('macro-no').value=it?.no||'';$('macro-desc').value=it?.desc||'';$('macro-cat').value=it?(it.cat||''):(macroCat&&macroCat!==macroNone?macroCat:'');$('macro-form-msg').textContent=it?'수정 중':'';$('macro-save').textContent=it?'수정 저장':'저장';setTimeout(()=>$('macro-no').focus(),0);}
async function macroShare(){try{autoMarkUnsaved();await autoShareAfter();}catch{}}
$('macro-q').addEventListener('input',renderMacros);
$('macro-q').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'){e.target.value='';renderMacros();}});
$('macro-add-btn').addEventListener('click',()=>macroOpenForm(null));
$('macro-cancel').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('macro-form').classList.add('hidden');macroEditId='';});
$('macro-desc').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')$('macro-save').click();});
$('macro-no').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')$('macro-desc').focus();});
$('macro-no').addEventListener('input',e=>{const nk=s=>String(s).replace(/\s+/g,'').toLowerCase();const v=nk(e.target.value);const dup=v&&macroLive().find(x=>nk(x.no)===v&&x.id!==macroEditId);$('macro-form-msg').textContent=dup?`이미 있는 번호예요 · ${dup.desc}`:(macroEditId?'수정 중':'');$('macro-form-msg').style.color=dup?'#b54340':'';$('macro-save').disabled=Boolean(dup);});
$('macro-save').addEventListener('click',async()=>{const no=$('macro-no').value.trim(),desc=$('macro-desc').value.trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');if(!no||!desc){$('macro-form-msg').textContent='번호와 설명을 모두 입력하세요.';return;}
 const list=macroLoad();const nk=s=>String(s).replace(/\s+/g,'').toLowerCase();const dup=list.find(x=>!x.del&&nk(x.no)===nk(no)&&x.id!==macroEditId);if(dup){$('macro-form-msg').textContent=`${no} 번호는 이미 있어요 · ${dup.desc}`;$('macro-q').value=dup.no;renderMacros();return;}
 if(macroEditId){const it=list.find(x=>x.id===macroEditId);if(it){it.no=no;it.desc=desc;it.cat=$('macro-cat').value.trim().slice(0,20);it.at=Date.now();}logChange('매크로 수정 · '+no);}
 else{list.push({id:Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6),no,desc,cat:$('macro-cat').value.trim().slice(0,20),at:Date.now(),del:false});logChange('매크로 추가 · '+no);}
 macroSave(list);$('macro-form').classList.add('hidden');macroEditId='';renderMacros();toast('저장했습니다. 다른 팀장님 화면에도 공유됩니다.');await macroShare();});
$('macro-bulk-btn').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('macro-form').classList.add('hidden');$('macro-bulk').classList.remove('hidden');$('macro-bulk-text').focus();});
$('macro-bulk-cancel').addEventListener('click',()=>$('macro-bulk').classList.add('hidden'));
$('macro-bulk-save').addEventListener('click',async()=>{const lines=$('macro-bulk-text').value.replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);const pairs=[];
 for(let i=0;i<lines.length;i++){const tab=lines[i].split('\t');if(tab.length>=2&&tab[0].trim()&&tab[1].trim()){pairs.push([tab[0].trim(),tab.slice(1).join(' ').trim()]);continue;}if(i+1<lines.length){pairs.push([lines[i],lines[i+1]]);i++;}}
 if(!pairs.length){$('macro-bulk-msg').textContent='읽을 수 있는 매크로가 없어요.';return;}
 const nk=s=>String(s).replace(/\s+/g,'').toLowerCase();const list=macroLoad(),have=new Set(list.filter(x=>!x.del).map(x=>nk(x.no)));let n=0;const skipped=[];for(const [no,desc] of pairs){const d=desc.replace(/\s+/g,' ');if(have.has(nk(no))){skipped.push(no);continue;}have.add(nk(no));list.push({id:Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8),no,desc:d,at:Date.now(),del:false});n++;}
 if(!n){$('macro-bulk-msg').textContent='모두 이미 있는 번호예요: '+skipped.slice(0,8).join(', ');return;}macroSave(list);logChange('매크로 '+n+'개 붙여넣기');$('macro-bulk-text').value='';$('macro-bulk').classList.add('hidden');renderMacros();toast(n+'개 추가했습니다.'+(skipped.length?` 번호 중복 ${skipped.length}개는 건너뜀: ${skipped.slice(0,8).join(', ')}${skipped.length>8?' …':''}`:''));await macroShare();});
$('macro-list').addEventListener('click',async e=>{const card=e.target.closest('.macro-card');if(!card)return;const list=macroLoad(),it=list.find(x=>x.id===card.dataset.id);if(!it)return;
 if(e.target.closest('[data-copy]')){try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(it.no);toast(it.no+' 복사했습니다.');}catch{toast('복사하지 못했습니다.');}return;}
 if(e.target.closest('[data-edit]')){macroOpenForm(it);window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'});return;}
 if(e.target.closest('[data-del]')){if(!confirm(`${it.no} 매크로를 삭제할까요?`))return;it.del=true;it.at=Date.now();macroSave(list);logChange('매크로 삭제 · '+it.no);renderMacros();await macroShare();}});

$('macro-cats').addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-cat]');if(!b)return;macroCat=b.dataset.cat;renderMacros();});
$('macro-list').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'&&e.target.classList&&e.target.classList.contains('macro-cat-in')){e.preventDefault();e.target.blur();}});
$('macro-list').addEventListener('change',async e=>{
 const inp=e.target;if(!inp.classList||!inp.classList.contains('macro-cat-in'))return;
 const card=inp.closest('.macro-card');if(!card)return;
 const list=macroLoad(),it=list.find(x=>x.id===card.dataset.id);if(!it)return;
 const cat=inp.value.trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,20);
 if((it.cat||'')===cat)return;
 it.cat=cat;it.at=Date.now();macroSave(list);
 try{logChange('매크로 대분류 · '+it.no+' → '+(cat||'비움'));}catch{}
 renderMacros();await macroShare();
});
$('macro-bulkcat-go').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
 const {hit}=macroHits();if(!hit.length)return;
 const cat=$('macro-bulkcat-in').value.trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,20);
 if(!confirm(cat?`지금 보이는 ${hit.length}개 매크로의 대분류를 "${cat}"(으)로 지정할까요?`:`지금 보이는 ${hit.length}개 매크로의 대분류를 비울까요?`))return;
 const ids=new Set(hit.map(x=>x.id)),list=macroLoad(),now=Date.now();let n=0;
 for(const it of list){if(ids.has(it.id)&&(it.cat||'')!==cat){it.cat=cat;it.at=now;n++;}}
 macroSave(list);try{logChange('매크로 대분류 일괄 · '+n+'개 → '+(cat||'비움'));}catch{}
 $('macro-bulkcat-in').value='';macroCat=cat&&macroCat!==''&&macroCat!==macroNone?cat:macroCat;renderMacros();toast(n+'개의 대분류를 '+(cat?'"'+cat+'"(으)로 지정했습니다.':'비웠습니다.')+' 다른 팀장님 화면에도 공유됩니다.');await macroShare();
});

/* ===== 외부 팀즈 멘션 */
function extMapLoad(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ginjo-ext-map')||'[]');return Array.isArray(v)?v.filter(x=>x&&x.k):[];}catch{return [];}}
function extMapSave(l){try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-ext-map',JSON.stringify(l));}catch{}}
function extSrcLoad(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ginjo-ext-src')||'null');return v&&typeof v.text==='string'?v:{text:'',at:0};}catch{return {text:'',at:0};}}
function extSrcSave(v){try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-ext-src',JSON.stringify(v));}catch{}}
const extKey=s=>String(s||'').replace(/^@+/,'').replace(/[​-‍﻿⁠\s]/g,'');
function extSharedPart(){const m=extMapLoad(),s=extSrcLoad(),o={};if(m.length)o.extMap=m.map(x=>[x.k,x.e,x.at,x.del?1:0]);if(s.at)o.extSrc={text:s.text.slice(0,8000),at:s.at};return Object.keys(o).length?o:null;}
function mergeExt(input){let ch=false;
 if(Array.isArray(input.extMap)){const map=new Map(extMapLoad().map(x=>[extKey(x.k),x]));for(const r of input.extMap){if(!Array.isArray(r))continue;const it={k:String(r[0]??''),e:String(r[1]??''),at:Number(r[2])||0,del:Boolean(r[3])};const k=extKey(it.k);if(!k)continue;const mine=map.get(k);if(!mine||it.at>mine.at){map.set(k,it);ch=true;}}if(ch)extMapSave([...map.values()]);}
 if(input.extSrc&&Number(input.extSrc.at)>extSrcLoad().at){extSrcSave({text:String(input.extSrc.text||''),at:Number(input.extSrc.at)});ch=true;}
 if(ch&&state.view==='ext')renderExt(true);}
// 엑셀에서 복사한 한 열(여러 줄 셀은 "…" 로 감싸짐)을 줄 목록으로
function extLines(text){const s=String(text||'').replace(/\r\n?/g,'\n');const out=[];let i=0;while(i<=s.length){if(s[i]==='"'){let j=i+1,cell='';while(j<s.length){if(s[j]==='"'){if(s[j+1]==='"'){cell+='"';j+=2;continue;}break;}cell+=s[j++];}out.push(...cell.split('\n'));i=j+1;while(i<s.length&&s[i]!=='\n')i++;i++;}else{const n=s.indexOf('\n',i);out.push(n<0?s.slice(i):s.slice(i,n));i=n<0?s.length+1:n+1;}}return out.map(x=>x.split('\t')[0]);}
function extNowHour(){const d=new Date(Date.now()+9*3600000);return d.getUTCHours()+d.getUTCMinutes()/60;}
let extLast=null;
function extIsDept(l){l=String(l||'').trim();return l!==''&&!/[:：]/.test(l)&&!l.includes('@');}
function extMentions(lines){const out=[];for(const l of lines)for(const m of String(l).match(/@[^@]+/g)||[])out.push(m.replace(/\s+\S*_$/,'').trim());return out;}
function extGroups(arr){const g=[];for(const l of arr||[]){if(extIsDept(l))g.push({dept:l.trim(),lines:[]});else if(String(l).trim()&&g.length)g[g.length-1].lines.push(l);}return g;}
function renderExt(fromShared){const ta=$('ext-src');if(!ta)return;const src=extSrcLoad();if(fromShared||(document.activeElement!==ta&&ta.value!==src.text&&!ta.dataset.dirty))ta.value=src.text;
 $('ext-src-at').textContent=src.at?'원문 저장 '+new Date(src.at+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(5,16).replace('T',' ').replace('-','/'):'';
 const map=extMapLoad().filter(x=>!x.del);const pairs=map.map(x=>[x.k,x.e]);let o;try{o=GinjoExternal.build(extLines(ta.value),pairs,extNowHour());}catch(e){o={f:[],hDay:[],hNight:[],iDay:[],iNight:[],j:[],missing:[],err:e.message};}extLast=o;
 const show=(id,arr)=>{const key=id.slice(4);let gi=-1;$(id).innerHTML=arr.map(l=>{if(extIsDept(l)){gi++;return `<span class="dept">${esc(l)}</span><button type="button" class="ext-dcopy" data-p="${key}" data-g="${gi}" title="이 부서 멘션만 복사">멘션 복사</button>`;}return esc(l);}).join('\n');};
 show('ext-j',o.j);show('ext-f',o.f);
 const now=new Date(Date.now()+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16);$('ext-now').textContent=now+' 기준';
 const mb=$('ext-missing');mb.innerHTML=o.err?'변환 실패: '+esc(o.err):o.missing.length?`영문 이름이 없어 빠진 사람 ${o.missing.length}명: `+o.missing.map(n=>`${esc(n)}<button type="button" data-ext-fill="${esc(n)}">＋</button>`).join(' · '):'';mb.classList.toggle('hidden',!o.err&&!o.missing.length);
 $('ext-map-count').textContent=map.length+'명';renderExtMap();}
function renderExtMap(){const box=$('ext-map-list');if(!box)return;const q=extKey($('ext-map-q').value).toLowerCase();const list=extMapLoad().filter(x=>!x.del&&(!q||(extKey(x.k)+' '+x.e).toLowerCase().includes(q))).sort((a,b)=>a.k.localeCompare(b.k,'ko'));
 box.innerHTML=list.length?list.slice(0,600).map(x=>`<div class="ext-map-item"><b>${esc(x.k)}</b><span title="${esc(x.e)}">${esc(x.e)}</span><button type="button" data-ext-del="${esc(x.k)}" title="삭제">✕</button></div>`).join(''):'<div class="tiny">등록된 이름이 없습니다.</div>';}
async function extShare(){try{autoMarkUnsaved();await autoShareAfter();}catch{}}
function extAddPairs(pairs){const map=new Map(extMapLoad().map(x=>[extKey(x.k),x]));let n=0,u=0;const ts=Date.now();for(const [k0,e0] of pairs){const k=String(k0||'').trim(),e=String(e0||'').trim().replace(/^@+/,'');if(!extKey(k)||!e)continue;const old=map.get(extKey(k));if(old&&!old.del&&old.e===e)continue;if(old&&!old.del)u++;else n++;map.set(extKey(k),{k,e,at:ts,del:false});}extMapSave([...map.values()]);return {n,u};}
// "홍길동	Gildong Hong" (엑셀 두 칸) 또는 "홍길동 Gildong Hong" (한 칸/한 줄) 모두 읽기. 한글이 없는 머리글 줄은 건너뜀
function extParsePairs(text){const out=[];for(const raw of String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n')){const l=raw.trim();if(!l)continue;let k,e;const c=l.split('\t').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);if(c.length>=2){k=c[0];e=c.slice(1).join(' ');}else{const m=l.match(/^(\S+)\s+(.+)$/);if(!m)continue;k=m[1];e=m[2];}if(!/[가-힣]/.test(k)||/[가-힣]/.test(e))continue;out.push([k,e.replace(/^@+/,'').trim()]);}return out;}
let extTimer=0;
$('ext-src').addEventListener('input',e=>{e.target.dataset.dirty='1';clearTimeout(extTimer);extTimer=setTimeout(async()=>{extSrcSave({text:$('ext-src').value,at:Date.now()});delete $('ext-src').dataset.dirty;renderExt();await extShare();},900);renderExt();});
$('ext-clear').addEventListener('click',async()=>{if(!confirm('외부 명단 원문을 지울까요? 다른 팀장님 화면에서도 지워집니다.'))return;$('ext-src').value='';extSrcSave({text:'',at:Date.now()});renderExt();await extShare();});
$('ext-view').addEventListener('click',async e=>{const dc=e.target.closest('.ext-dcopy');if(dc&&extLast){const g=extGroups(extLast[dc.dataset.p])[Number(dc.dataset.g)];const text=g?extMentions(g.lines).join(' '):'';if(!text){toast('복사할 멘션이 없습니다.');return;}try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);toast(g.dept+' 멘션 복사했습니다.');}catch{toast('복사하지 못했습니다.');}return;}
 const c=e.target.closest('[data-ext-copy]');if(c&&extLast){const text=(extLast[c.dataset.extCopy]||[]).join('\n');try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);toast('복사했습니다.');}catch{toast('복사하지 못했습니다.');}return;}
 const f=e.target.closest('[data-ext-fill]');if(f){$('ext-map-box').open=true;$('ext-map-k').value=f.dataset.extFill;$('ext-map-e').focus();return;}
 const d=e.target.closest('[data-ext-del]');if(d){if(!confirm(d.dataset.extDel+' 매핑을 삭제할까요?'))return;const l=extMapLoad();const it=l.find(x=>extKey(x.k)===extKey(d.dataset.extDel));if(it){it.del=true;it.at=Date.now();extMapSave(l);logChange('외부 팀즈 이름 삭제 · '+it.k);renderExt();await extShare();}}});
$('ext-map-q').addEventListener('input',renderExtMap);
$('ext-map-add').addEventListener('click',async()=>{const r=extAddPairs([[$('ext-map-k').value,$('ext-map-e').value]]);if(!r.n&&!r.u){toast('한글 이름과 영문 이름을 모두 넣으세요.');return;}logChange('외부 팀즈 이름 추가 · '+$('ext-map-k').value.trim());$('ext-map-k').value='';$('ext-map-e').value='';renderExt();await extShare();});
$('ext-map-e').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')$('ext-map-add').click();});
$('ext-map-k').addEventListener('paste',e=>{const txt=(e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData)?.getData('text')||'';const pairs=extParsePairs(txt);if(pairs.length>1||(pairs.length===1&&/[\t]|\s[A-Za-z]/.test(txt))){e.preventDefault();$('ext-map-paste').value=txt;$('ext-map-save').click();}});
$('ext-map-save').addEventListener('click',async()=>{const pairs=extParsePairs($('ext-map-paste').value);if(!pairs.length){$('ext-map-msg').textContent='"한글이름 영문이름" 형식의 줄이 없습니다.';return;}const r=extAddPairs(pairs);$('ext-map-paste').value='';$('ext-map-msg').textContent=`새로 ${r.n}명 · 바뀜 ${r.u}명`;logChange('외부 팀즈 이름 붙여넣기 '+(r.n+r.u)+'명');renderExt();await extShare();});
setInterval(()=>{if(state.view==='ext')try{renderExt();}catch{}},60000);
function extEngineSource(){for(const s of document.scripts){const x=s.textContent||'';if(x.includes('globalThis.GinjoExternal=(function(){'))return x;}return '';}
