const RT_DEFAULT_RULES=[['주중','5','14','8','10-11 100%'],['주중','6','15','8',''],['주중','7','16','8',''],['주중','8','17','8','13-14 100%'],['주중','9','18','8',''],['주중','10','19','8','14-15 100%'],['주중','11','20','8','14-15 100%'],['주중','12','21','8','15-16 100%'],['주중','13','22','8','16-17 100%'],['주중','14','23','8',''],['주중','15','24','8','20-21 100%'],['주중','16','25','8',''],['주중','17','26','8','21-22 50%/22-23 50%'],['주중','18','27','8','23-24 100%'],['주중','19','28','8','24-25 100%'],['주중','20','29','8','25-26 100%']];
const RT_DEFAULT_LIST=[[5,14,8],[6,15,8],[8,12,4],[8,17,8],[9,18,8],[10,13,3],[10,14,4],[10,19,8],[11,15,4],[11,20,8],[11,16,5],[12,21,8],[13,22,8],[15,24,8],[17,26,8],[18,22,4],[18,27,8],[19,23,4],[19,28,8],[20,29,8],[23,27,4]].map(r=>r.map(String));
function rtRulesLoad(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ginjo-rt-rules')||'null');if(v&&Array.isArray(v.rows)&&v.rows.length)return {at:Number(v.at)||0,rows:v.rows,preg:Array.isArray(v.preg)?v.preg:['권성주','권여원'],list:Array.isArray(v.list)&&v.list.length?v.list:RT_DEFAULT_LIST};}catch{}return {at:0,rows:RT_DEFAULT_RULES.map(r=>r.slice()),preg:['권성주','권여원'],list:RT_DEFAULT_LIST.map(r=>r.slice())};}
function rtRulesSave(v){try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-rt-rules',JSON.stringify(v));}catch{}}
function rtSnapLoad(date){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ginjo-rt-'+date)||'null');return v&&Array.isArray(v.hours)?v:null;}catch{return null;}}
function rtSnapSave(date,v){try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-rt-'+date,JSON.stringify(v));for(let i=localStorage.length-1;i>=0;i--){const k=localStorage.key(i);if(k&&/^ginjo-rt-\d{4}-/.test(k)&&k<'ginjo-rt-'+new Date(Date.parse(date+'T00:00:00Z')-7*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10))localStorage.removeItem(k);}}catch{}}
function rtSharedPart(){const out={};const d=state.daily?.date;if(d){const list=[];for(let i=0;i<localStorage.length;i++){const k=localStorage.key(i);const m=k&&k.match(/^ginjo-rt-(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})$/);if(m&&m[1]>=d){const sn=rtSnapLoad(m[1]);if(sn)list.push({date:m[1],snap:sn});}}list.sort((a,b)=>b.snap.at-a.snap.at);if(list.length)out.rt=list.slice(0,4);}const r=rtRulesLoad();if(r.at)out.rtRules=r;return Object.keys(out).length?out:null;}
function mergeRT(input){let ch=false;if(input.rtRules&&Number(input.rtRules.at)>rtRulesLoad().at){rtRulesSave({...input.rtRules,list:input.rtRules.list?.length?input.rtRules.list:RT_DEFAULT_LIST});ch=true;}
 const today=state.daily?.date||'';for(const it of (Array.isArray(input.rt)?input.rt:input.rt?[input.rt]:[])){if(!it||!it.snap||String(it.date)<today)continue;const mine=rtSnapLoad(it.date);if(!mine||Number(it.snap.at)>Number(mine.at)){rtSnapSave(it.date,it.snap);ch=true;}}
 if(ch){if(state.view==='rt')setTimeout(()=>{try{renderRT();}catch{}},0);try{renderRtDuty();}catch{}}}
function rtStaffRows(date){const d=state.daily;if(!d)return [];if(!date||date===d.date){if(Array.isArray(d.staffRows)&&d.staffRows.length)return d.staffRows;return (d.schedule||[]).map(r=>[r.name,'',r.shift,'',r.leader||'',r.flag]);}
 const f=(d.forecast||[]).find(x=>x.date===date);return f&&Array.isArray(f.staff)?f.staff.map(r=>[r[0],'',r[1],'','',r[2]]):[];}
const RT_WD=['일','월','화','수','목','금','토'];
function rtDateLabel(date){const [y,m,dd]=date.split('-').map(Number);return `${m}/${dd} (${RT_WD[new Date(Date.UTC(y,m-1,dd)).getUTCDay()]})`;}
function rtDates(){const d=state.daily;if(!d)return [];const out=[{date:d.date,tag:'오늘'}];for(const f of d.forecast||[])if(Array.isArray(f.staff)&&f.staff.length)out.push({date:f.date,tag:f.date===rtAddDay(d.date,1)?'내일':''});return out;}
function rtAddDay(date,n){return new Date(Date.parse(date+'T00:00:00Z')+n*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10);}
let rtSelDate='';
function rtSel(){const list=rtDates();if(!list.length)return '';if(rtSelDate&&list.some(x=>x.date===rtSelDate))return rtSelDate;const tm=list.find(x=>x.tag==='내일');rtSelDate=(tm||list[0]).date;return rtSelDate;}
function renderRTDates(){const sel=$('rt-date');if(!sel)return;const list=rtDates(),cur=rtSel();const html=list.map(x=>`<option value="${x.date}"${x.date===cur?' selected':''}>${rtDateLabel(x.date)}${x.tag?' · '+x.tag:''}</option>`).join('')||'<option>데일리 없음</option>';if(sel.dataset.html!==html){sel.innerHTML=html;sel.dataset.html=html;}sel.value=cur;}
function rtHash(s){let h=5381;for(let i=0;i<s.length;i++)h=((h<<5)+h+s.charCodeAt(i))|0;return (h>>>0).toString(36)+'.'+s.length.toString(36);}
let rtCache={key:'',res:null};const rtCacheMap=new Map();
function rtCompute(date){const rules=rtRulesLoad(),rows=rtStaffRows(date);if(!rows.length)throw Error('이 날짜의 데일리 명단이 없습니다. 데일리 스크립트를 최신으로 바꿨는지 확인하세요.');const key=JSON.stringify([rows,rules.rows,rules.preg]);if(rtCacheMap.has(key))return rtCacheMap.get(key);
 GinjoRealtime.setPregnancy(rules.preg);const res=GinjoRealtime.plan(rules.rows.map(r=>[r[1],r[2],r[3],r[5]||'',r[4]]),rows);res.hash=rtHash(key);rtCacheMap.set(key,res);if(rtCacheMap.size>6)rtCacheMap.delete(rtCacheMap.keys().next().value);return res;}
const rtShowLive={on:false};const rtEdit={on:false,date:''};let rtRulesEditing=false;
function rtHM(iso){const t=Date.parse(iso||'');return Number.isFinite(t)?new Date(t+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16):'—';}
function rtNorm(s){return String(s||'').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).sort().join(' ');}
function rtCurrent(){const d=state.daily;if(!d)return null;const date=rtSel()||d.date;let live=null,err='';try{live=rtCompute(date);}catch(e){err=e.message||String(e);}const snap=rtSnapLoad(date);const useLive=!snap||rtShowLive.on;return {d,date,live,snap,err,data:useLive?live:snap,useLive};}
function rtQCounts(data,list){return list.map(([s,e,h])=>{const S=Number(s),H=Number(h);if(!Number.isFinite(S)||!Number.isFinite(H)||s===''||h==='')return '';return (data.shiftCounts||[]).filter(x=>x.start===S&&x.hours===H).reduce((a,x)=>a+x.count,0);});}
function renderRT(){
 const box=$('rt-body');if(!box)return;const c=rtCurrent();const rules=rtRulesLoad();
 renderRTDates();if(!rtRulesEditing)renderRTRules(rules);
 {const n=rtDates().length;const hint=$('rt-date-hint');if(hint)hint.classList.toggle('hidden',n>1);}
 if(!c){box.innerHTML='<div class="empty">데일리를 먼저 읽어주세요.</div>';$('rt-shift').innerHTML='';return;}
 const {d,date,live,snap,err,data,useLive}=c;const st=$('rt-state'),tg=$('rt-toggle');
 const changed=Boolean(snap&&live&&snap.hash!==live.hash);
 if(!snap){st.textContent='확정 전 · 자동 계산';st.className='rt-state live';}
 else if(useLive){st.textContent='새 배정 미리보기 (확정 안 됨)';st.className='rt-state live';}
 else if(changed){st.textContent=`확정본 ${rtHM(new Date(snap.at).toISOString())} ${snap.who||''} · 데일리 바뀜`;st.className='rt-state changed';}
 else{st.textContent=`확정본 ${rtHM(new Date(snap.at).toISOString())} ${snap.who||''}`;st.className='rt-state ok';}
 tg.classList.toggle('hidden',!(snap&&(changed||useLive)));tg.textContent=useLive?'확정본 보기':'새 배정 보기';
 $('rt-sub').textContent=`${rtDateLabel(date)} 배정 · 데일리 ${rtHM(d.capturedAt)} 기준`+(live?` · 배정 대상 ${live.staffCount}명`:'')+(snap&&!useLive?` · 확정 당시 데일리 ${rtHM(snap.dailyAt)}`:'');
 const warn=$('rt-warn');const wl=err?['배정 계산 실패: '+err]:(data?.warnings||[]);warn.textContent=wl.join('\n');warn.classList.toggle('hidden',!wl.length);
 if(rtEdit.on&&rtEdit.date!==date)rtEdit.on=false;
 $('rt-edit').textContent=rtEdit.on?'수정 취소':'✎ 수기 변경';$('rt-edit').classList.toggle('on',rtEdit.on);$('rt-confirm').textContent=rtEdit.on?'수기 변경 확정':'배정 확정';$('rt-edit-note').classList.toggle('hidden',!rtEdit.on);$('rt-view').classList.toggle('rt-editing',rtEdit.on);$('rt-recalc').disabled=rtEdit.on;
 if(snap&&snap.manual&&!useLive){st.textContent+=' · 수기 변경';}
 if(!data){box.innerHTML='<div class="empty">배정을 계산하지 못했습니다.</div>';return;}
 if(rtEdit.on&&box.querySelector('.rt-in'))return;
 const now=E.businessClock(new Date().toISOString());const cmp=state.rtCmp||{};
 const rows=data.hours.filter(h=>h.hour>=6&&h.hour<=27).map(h=>{
  const dl=h.hour>=9&&h.hour<=25,ml=h.hour>=10;const dTxt=h.delay.join(' '),mTxt=h.meal.join(' ');
  const cC=cmp.c?.[h.hour],cL=cmp.l?.[h.hour];const dDiff=dl&&cC!==undefined&&rtNorm(cC)!==rtNorm(dTxt),mDiff=ml&&cL!==undefined&&rtNorm(cL)!==rtNorm(mTxt);
  return `<tr class="${now.date===date&&now.hour===h.hour?'now':''}"><td class="hr">${h.hour}</td><td class="num">${h.present||''}</td><td class="num">${h.mealCount||''}</td><td class="num">${h.present?h.present-h.mealCount:''}</td><td class="${dl?'dl':'na'}${dDiff?' diff':''}">${rtEdit.on&&dl?`<input class="rt-in" data-h="${h.hour}" data-k="delay" value="${esc(dTxt)}" data-orig="${esc(dTxt)}">`:esc(dTxt)+(dl&&h.delay.length?`<span class="cnt">${h.delay.length}명</span>`:'')}${dDiff?`<small>엑셀: ${esc(cC||'(빈칸)')}</small>`:''}</td><td class="${ml?'ml':'na'}${mDiff?' diff':''}">${rtEdit.on&&ml?`<input class="rt-in" data-h="${h.hour}" data-k="meal" value="${esc(mTxt)}" data-orig="${esc(mTxt)}">`:esc(mTxt)}${mDiff?`<small>엑셀: ${esc(cL||'(빈칸)')}</small>`:''}</td></tr>`;}).join('');
 box.innerHTML=`<table class="data rt-table"><thead><tr><th>시간</th><th>출근</th><th>식사</th><th>실투입</th><th>시간대 별 단독 담당자</th><th>식사</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table>`;
 const q=rtQCounts(data,rules.list);const listed=new Set(rules.list.map(r=>Number(r[0])+'|'+Number(r[2])));const extra=(data.shiftCounts||[]).filter(x=>!listed.has(x.start+'|'+x.hours));
 $('rt-shift').innerHTML=`<table class="data rt-shift-table"><thead><tr><th>start</th><th>End</th><th>H</th><th>인원</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rules.list.map((r,i)=>`<tr class="${q[i]===0?'zero':''}"><td>${esc(r[0])}</td><td>${esc(r[1])}</td><td>${r[2]!==''?esc(r[2])+'H':''}</td><td><b>${q[i]}</b></td></tr>`).join('')}<tr><td colspan="3">합계</td><td><b>${q.reduce((a,x)=>a+(Number(x)||0),0)}</b></td></tr>${extra.map(x=>`<tr class="extra" title="목록에 없는 시프트"><td>${x.start}</td><td>${x.end}</td><td>${x.hours}H</td><td><b>${x.count}</b></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>${extra.length?'<div class="tiny">노란 줄은 시프트 인원 목록에 없는 조합입니다.</div>':''}`;
}
function renderRTRules(rules){const box=$('rt-rules');if(!box)return;const cnt=new Map();try{for(const x of (rtCompute(rtSel()).shiftCounts||[]))cnt.set(x.start+'|'+x.hours,(cnt.get(x.start+'|'+x.hours)||0)+x.count);}catch{}
 box.innerHTML=`<table class="rt-rules-table"><thead><tr><th>구분</th><th>start</th><th>End</th><th>hrs</th><th>인원</th><th>식사시간</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rules.rows.map(r=>`<tr><td>${esc(r[0])}</td><td>${esc(r[1])}</td><td>${esc(r[2])}</td><td>${esc(r[3])}</td><td class="cn${String(r[5]??'').trim()!==''?' ovr':''}"${String(r[5]??'').trim()!==''?' title="직접 입력한 인원 (비우면 데일리 기준 자동)"':''}>${String(r[5]??'').trim()!==''?esc(r[5]):(cnt.get(Number(r[1])+'|'+Number(r[3]))||0)}</td><td class="meal">${esc(r[4])}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`;
 const pr=$('rt-preg');if(pr&&document.activeElement!==pr)pr.value=(rules.preg||[]).join(', ');
 const sl=$('rt-shiftlist-paste');if(sl&&document.activeElement!==sl&&!sl.dataset.dirty)sl.value=rules.list.map(r=>r.join('\t')).join('\n');
 $('rt-rules-at').textContent=rules.at?'저장 '+rtHM(new Date(rules.at).toISOString()):'기본값 사용 중';}
function rtParseRules(text,base){const lines=String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n');while(lines.length&&!lines[lines.length-1].trim())lines.pop();if(!lines.length)return null;
 const cells=lines.map(l=>l.split('\t').map(x=>x.trim()));const num=x=>/^\d{1,2}$/.test(x||'');
 const full=cells.filter(c=>c.length>=4&&(num(c[1])&&num(c[2])||num(c[0])&&num(c[1])));
 if(full.length)return full.map(c=>num(c[0])?['주중',c[0],c[1],c[2],c[4]??'']:[c[0]||'주중',c[1],c[2],c[3],c[5]??'']);
 const rows=base.map(r=>r.slice());for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i++)rows[i][4]=(lines[i]??'').split('\t').pop().trim();return rows;}
function rtParseList(text){const out=[];for(const l of String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n')){const c=l.split('\t').map(x=>x.trim().replace(/H$/i,''));if(c.length<3&&!c.join(''))continue;out.push([c[0]||'',c[1]||'',c[2]||'']);}return out.slice(0,40);}
$('rt-rules-save').addEventListener('click',async()=>{const cur=rtRulesLoad();const txt=$('rt-rules-paste').value;let rows=cur.rows;
 if(txt.trim()){const r=rtParseRules(txt,cur.rows);if(!r){toast('규칙표를 읽지 못했습니다.');return;}rows=r;}
 const list=$('rt-shiftlist-paste').value.trim()?rtParseList($('rt-shiftlist-paste').value):cur.list;
 const preg=$('rt-preg').value.split(/[,\s]+/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
 rtRulesSave({at:Date.now(),rows,preg,list});$('rt-rules-paste').value='';delete $('rt-shiftlist-paste').dataset.dirty;rtCache={key:'',res:null};logChange('실시간 배정 규칙 저장');renderRT();toast('규칙을 저장했습니다.');try{autoMarkUnsaved();await autoShareAfter();}catch{}});

$('rt-body').addEventListener('input',e=>{const i=e.target.closest('.rt-in');if(i)i.classList.toggle('changed',i.value.trim()!==i.dataset.orig.trim());});
$('rt-recalc').addEventListener('click',async()=>{const c0=rtCurrent();if(!c0)return;
 if(c0.snap&&!confirm(`${rtDateLabel(c0.date)} 확정본${c0.snap.manual?'(수기 변경 포함)':''}을 지금 데일리 기준 자동 배정으로 덮어쓸까요?`))return;

  rtShowLive.on=false;logChange('실시간 배정 재산정 · '+rtDateLabel(c.date));renderRT();try{renderRtDuty();}catch{}toast('지금 데일리 기준으로 다시 배정해서 확정했습니다.');try{autoMarkUnsaved();await autoShareAfter();}catch{}},30);});

// 엑셀에서 여러 줄(열) 복사 → 한 칸에 붙여넣으면 아래 칸들로 나눠 넣기. 탭이 있으면 표 한 줄로 보고 마지막 칸(식사시간) 사용
function rtSpreadPaste(e,sel,lastCell){const el=e.target;if(!el.matches(sel))return;const txt=(e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData)?.getData('text')||'';
 const lines=txt.replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n');while(lines.length&&lines[lines.length-1]==='')lines.pop();
 if(lines.length<=1&&!txt.includes('\t'))return;e.preventDefault();
 const pick=l=>{const c=l.split('\t').map(x=>x.replace(/^"|"$/g,'').trim());return lastCell?c[c.length-1]:c.join(' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();};
 const list=[...el.closest('table').querySelectorAll(sel)].filter(x=>!lastCell||true);const kind=el.dataset.k;const same=kind?list.filter(x=>x.dataset.k===kind):list;
 let i=same.indexOf(el);for(const l of lines){const target=same[i++];if(!target)break;target.value=pick(l);target.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));}
 toast(`${Math.min(lines.length,same.length-same.indexOf(el))}칸에 붙여넣었습니다.`);}
$('rt-body').addEventListener('paste',e=>rtSpreadPaste(e,'.rt-in',false));
$('rt-rules').addEventListener('paste',e=>{
 const el=e.target;if(!el.matches||!el.matches('input[data-i]'))return;
 const txt=(e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData)?.getData('text')||'';
 const lines=txt.replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n');while(lines.length&&lines[lines.length-1]==='')lines.pop();
 const cells=lines.map(l=>l.split('\t').map(x=>x.replace(/^"|"$/g,'').trim()));
 const two=cells.length&&cells.every(c=>c.length>=2)&&cells.every(c=>c[c.length-2]===''||/^\d{1,3}$/.test(c[c.length-2]));
 if(!two){if(el.dataset.k==='n'){e.preventDefault();const ns=[...document.querySelectorAll('#rt-rules input[data-k="n"]')];let i=ns.indexOf(el);for(const c of cells){const t=ns[i++];if(!t)break;t.value=c[c.length-1].replace(/[^\d]/g,'');t.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));}toast('인원 '+Math.min(cells.length,ns.length)+'칸에 붙여넣었습니다.');return;}rtSpreadPaste(e,'input[data-i][data-k="m"]',true);return;}
 e.preventDefault();
 const ns=[...document.querySelectorAll('#rt-rules input[data-k="n"]')],ms=[...document.querySelectorAll('#rt-rules input[data-k="m"]')];
 let i=Number(el.dataset.i);let cnt=0;
 for(const c of cells){const nIn=ns[i],mIn=ms[i];if(!nIn||!mIn)break;nIn.value=c[c.length-2];mIn.value=c[c.length-1];nIn.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));mIn.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));i++;cnt++;}
 toast(cnt+'줄(인원·식사시간)을 붙여넣었습니다. 등록을 눌러 저장하세요.');
});

$('rt-confirm').addEventListener('click',async()=>{const c=rtCurrent();
 if(rtEdit.on){if(!c?.data){toast('배정이 없습니다.');return;}const ins=[...document.querySelectorAll('#rt-body .rt-in')];const split=s=>String(s||'').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  const hours=c.data.hours.map(h=>{const o={hour:h.hour,present:h.present,mealCount:h.mealCount,delay:h.delay.slice(),meal:h.meal.slice()};for(const i of ins)if(Number(i.dataset.h)===h.hour){if(i.dataset.k==='delay')o.delay=split(i.value);else{o.meal=split(i.value);o.mealCount=o.meal.length;}}return o;});
  const n=ins.filter(i=>i.value.trim()!==i.dataset.orig.trim()).length;
  rtSnapSave(c.date,{at:Date.now(),who:myName()||'',hash:c.live?.hash||c.snap?.hash||'',dailyAt:c.d.capturedAt||'',warnings:c.data.warnings||[],hours,shiftCounts:c.data.shiftCounts||[],manual:true});
  rtEdit.on=false;rtShowLive.on=false;logChange('실시간 배정 수기 변경 확정 · '+rtDateLabel(c.date)+' ('+n+'칸)');renderRT();try{renderRtDuty();}catch{}toast('수기 변경을 확정했습니다. 다른 팀장님 화면에도 공유됩니다.');try{autoMarkUnsaved();await autoShareAfter();}catch{}return;}

 const l=c.live;rtSnapSave(c.date,{at:Date.now(),who:myName()||'',hash:l.hash,dailyAt:c.d.capturedAt||'',warnings:l.warnings,hours:l.hours.map(h=>({hour:h.hour,present:h.present,mealCount:h.mealCount,delay:h.delay,meal:h.meal})),shiftCounts:l.shiftCounts});
 rtShowLive.on=false;try{renderRtDuty();}catch{}logChange('실시간 배정 확정 · '+rtDateLabel(c.date));renderRT();toast('배정을 확정했습니다. 다른 팀장님 화면에도 공유됩니다.');try{autoMarkUnsaved();await autoShareAfter();}catch{}});
document.querySelector('.rt-copy').addEventListener('click',async e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-rt-copy]');if(!b)return;const c=rtCurrent();if(!c?.data){toast('배정이 없습니다.');return;}const data=c.data,k=b.dataset.rtCopy;const at=h=>data.hours.find(x=>x.hour===h)||{delay:[],meal:[],mealCount:0};let lines=[];
 if(k==='C')for(let h=9;h<=25;h++)lines.push(at(h).delay.join(' '));
 else if(k==='L')for(let h=10;h<=28;h++)lines.push(at(h).meal.join(' '));
 else if(k==='W')for(let h=5;h<=27;h++)lines.push(at(h).mealCount?String(at(h).mealCount):'');
 else if(k==='Q'){const q=rtQCounts(data,rtRulesLoad().list);for(let i=0;i<21;i++)lines.push(q[i]===undefined?'':String(q[i]));}
 else if(k==='ALL')lines=rtSheetGrid({date:c.date},data);
 try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(lines.join('\n'));toast(k==='ALL'?'복사했습니다. 실시간 탭 A1을 누르고 Ctrl+Shift+V 하세요.':b.textContent.replace('→','복사 →')+' 에 붙여넣으세요.');}catch{toast('복사하지 못했습니다.');}});
function rtSheetGrid(d,data){
 // 실시간 탭 A1:W33과 같은 배치 (A=0 ... W=22)
 const G=Array.from({length:33},()=>Array(23).fill(''));const col=s=>s.charCodeAt(0)-65;const put=(addr,v)=>{const m=addr.match(/^([A-W])(\d+)$/);G[Number(m[2])-1][col(m[1])]=v===undefined||v===null?'':String(v);};
 const [y,mo,da]=d.date.split('-').map(Number),label=`${mo}월 ${da}일`;const at=h=>data.hours.find(x=>x.hour===h)||{delay:[],meal:[],mealCount:0,present:0};
 const rules=rtRulesLoad(),q=rtQCounts(data,rules.list);
 put('L1',(data.warnings||[]).join(' / '));put('B2',label);
 put('C6','STW + 인앱재주문 = 각 2시간씩');put('G6','*8H 외 인원은 재주문 정각 ~ 30분 투입');
 put('B7','시간');put('C7','집중 투입 업무');put('L7','식사');put('N7','start');put('O7','End');put('P7','H');put('Q7',label);put('T7','Hour');put('U7',label);
 put('C9','시간대 별 단독 담당자');put('U9','출근');put('V9','실투입');put('W9','식사');
 for(let h=6;h<=27;h++)put('B'+(5+h),h);
 for(let h=9;h<=25;h++)put('C'+(5+h),at(h).delay.join(' '));
 for(let h=10;h<=28;h++)put('L'+(5+h),at(h).meal.join(' '));
 for(let i=0;i<21;i++){const r=rules.list[i];if(!r)continue;put('N'+(8+i),r[0]);put('O'+(8+i),r[1]);put('P'+(8+i),r[2]!==''?r[2]+'H':'');put('Q'+(8+i),q[i]);}
 put('Q29',q.reduce((a,x)=>a+(Number(x)||0),0));
 let meals=0;for(let h=5;h<=27;h++){const x=at(h);put('T'+(5+h),h);put('U'+(5+h),x.present);put('V'+(5+h),x.present-x.mealCount);put('W'+(5+h),x.mealCount||'');meals+=x.mealCount||0;}
 put('W33',meals);
 return G.map(r=>r.map(v=>v.replace(/[\t\r\n]+/g,' ')).join('\t'));
}
function rtCmpUpdate(){const pc=$('rt-cmp-c').value,pl=$('rt-cmp-l').value;const split=s=>s.replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n');const cmp={};
 if(pc.trim()){cmp.c={};split(pc).slice(0,17).forEach((v,i)=>cmp.c[9+i]=v.replace(/^"|"$/g,''));}
 if(pl.trim()){cmp.l={};split(pl).slice(0,19).forEach((v,i)=>cmp.l[10+i]=v.replace(/^"|"$/g,''));}
 state.rtCmp=cmp;const c=rtCurrent();const res=$('rt-cmp-result');if(!c?.data||(!cmp.c&&!cmp.l)){res.textContent='엑셀에서 두 범위를 복사해 붙여넣으면 다른 칸을 표에 표시합니다.';res.className='rt-cmp-result';renderRT();return;}
 let n=0,t=0;for(const h of c.data.hours){if(cmp.c&&h.hour>=9&&h.hour<=25&&cmp.c[h.hour]!==undefined){t++;if(rtNorm(cmp.c[h.hour])!==rtNorm(h.delay.join(' ')))n++;}if(cmp.l&&h.hour>=10&&h.hour<=28&&cmp.l[h.hour]!==undefined){t++;if(rtNorm(cmp.l[h.hour])!==rtNorm(h.meal.join(' ')))n++;}}
 res.textContent=n?`${t}칸 중 ${n}칸이 다릅니다. 표에 빨간 테두리로 표시했습니다.`:`${t}칸 모두 같습니다.`;res.className='rt-cmp-result '+(n?'bad':'good');renderRT();}
$('rt-cmp-c').addEventListener('input',rtCmpUpdate);$('rt-cmp-l').addEventListener('input',rtCmpUpdate);

/* ===== 이번 시간 실시간 담당자 (현황) */
function rtDutyNow(){
 const d=state.daily;if(!d)return {msg:'데일리를 먼저 불러오세요.',rows:[]};
 const now=E.businessClock(new Date().toISOString());if(now.date!==d.date)return {msg:'오늘 데일리가 아닙니다.',rows:[]};
 const snap=rtSnapLoad(d.date);if(!snap)return {msg:'금일 실시간 배정이 아직 확정되지 않았습니다.',rows:[],hour:now.hour};
 const h=(snap.hours||[]).find(x=>x.hour===now.hour);const names=h?h.delay||[]:[];
 if(!names.length)return {msg:`${String(now.hour%24).padStart(2,'0')}시에는 담당자가 없습니다.`,rows:[],hour:now.hour,snap};
 const sched=d.schedule||[],nk=s=>String(s||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').toLowerCase();
 const statusOf=new Map((state.result&&state.result.date===d.date?state.result.people||[]:[]).map(p=>[String(p.idKey??p.id).toLowerCase(),p.status]));
 const rows=[];for(const name of names){const k=nk(name);let hit=sched.filter(x=>nk(x.name)===k);if(!hit.length)hit=sched.filter(x=>{const n=nk(x.name);return n.length>=2&&(n.startsWith(k)||k.startsWith(n));});
  const ids=[...new Set(hit.map(x=>x.id).filter(Boolean))];if(!ids.length)rows.push({name,id:'',status:''});else for(const id of ids)rows.push({name,id,status:statusOf.get(String(id).toLowerCase())||''});}
 return {rows,hour:now.hour,snap};
}
function renderRtDuty(){const body=$('rtduty-body');if(!body)return;let r;try{r=rtDutyNow();}catch(e){r={msg:'확인 실패: '+(e.message||e),rows:[]};}
 const hl=r.hour!==undefined?String(r.hour%24).padStart(2,'0')+'~'+String((r.hour+1)%24).padStart(2,'0')+'시':'';
 $('rtduty-sub').textContent=r.snap?`${hl} · 확정본 ${new Date(r.snap.at+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16)}${r.snap.who?' '+r.snap.who:''}`:(hl?hl+' · 확정본 없음':'금일 실시간 배정 확정본 기준');
 $('rtduty-count').textContent=r.rows.length?r.rows.filter(x=>x.id).length+'명':'—';
 $('rtduty-copy').disabled=!r.rows.some(x=>x.id);
 body.innerHTML=r.rows.length?r.rows.map(x=>`<tr><td class="nm">${esc(x.name)}</td><td class="st">${x.status?`<span class="pill ${statusClass(x.status)}">${esc(x.status)}</span>`:'<span class="tiny">—</span>'}</td><td class="cp">${x.id?`<button type="button" class="rtduty-one" data-id="${esc(x.id)}" title="이 사번만 복사">${esc(x.id)} ⧉</button>`:'<span class="tiny" style="color:#b54340">사번 없음</span>'}</td></tr>`).join(''):`<tr><td colspan="3" class="tiny">${esc(r.msg||'—')}</td></tr>`;}
$('rtduty-body').addEventListener('click',async e=>{const b=e.target.closest('.rtduty-one');if(!b)return;try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(b.dataset.id);b.classList.add('done');setTimeout(()=>b.classList.remove('done'),1200);toast(b.dataset.id+' 복사했습니다.');}catch{toast('복사하지 못했습니다.');}});
$('rtduty-copy').addEventListener('click',async()=>{const r=rtDutyNow();const ids=r.rows.map(x=>x.id).filter(Boolean);if(!ids.length)return;try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(ids.join('\n'));toast(`사번 ${ids.length}개를 복사했습니다.`);}catch{toast('복사하지 못했습니다.');}});
setTimeout(renderRtDuty,1600);setInterval(()=>{try{renderRtDuty();}catch{}},20000);

/* ===== 데일리 수동 불러오기 */
function manualDailyLoad(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ginjo-manual-daily')||'null');return v&&v.data?v:null;}catch{return null;}}
function manualDailyNewer(embedded){const m=manualDailyLoad();if(!m)return null;const a=Date.parse(m.data.capturedAt||''),b=Date.parse(embedded?.capturedAt||'');if(Number.isFinite(a)&&(!Number.isFinite(b)||a>b)&&m.data.date>=String(embedded?.date||''))return m.data;try{localStorage.removeItem('ginjo-manual-daily');}catch{}return null;}
function manualState(){const m=manualDailyLoad();$('manual-state').textContent=m?'수동 데일리 사용 중 · 운영일 '+m.data.date+' · 생성 '+new Date(Date.parse(m.data.capturedAt)+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(11,16)+' (플로우 새 데일리가 오면 자동으로 그쪽을 씁니다)':'';}
function manualApply(text){let s=String(text||'').replace(/^﻿/,'');const a=s.indexOf('{'),b=s.lastIndexOf('}');if(a<0||b<a)throw Error('붙여넣은 글자에서 JSON을 찾지 못했습니다. 출력 칸 전체를 복사했는지 확인하세요.');s=s.slice(a,b+1);
 const data=B.decodeJSON(s);if(data.type!=='ginjo-daily-blocks'&&data.type!=='ginjo-daily')throw Error('데일리 스크립트 출력이 아닙니다.');if(!data.capturedAt)data.capturedAt=new Date().toISOString();
 receive(data,false);try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-manual-daily',JSON.stringify({at:Date.now(),data}));}catch{toast('브라우저 저장 공간이 부족해 새로고침하면 다시 붙여넣어야 합니다.');}
 manualState();logChange('데일리 수동 불러오기 · '+data.date);toast('데일리를 불러왔습니다 · 운영일 '+data.date);}
$('manual-load').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{manualApply($('manual-json').value);$('manual-json').value='';}catch(e){fail(e);}});
$('manual-clear').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{localStorage.removeItem('ginjo-manual-daily');}catch{}manualState();toast('수동 데일리를 해제했습니다. 새로고침하면 내장 데일리를 씁니다.');});
$('manual-file').addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{if(window.showOpenFilePicker){const [h]=await showOpenFilePicker({types:[{description:'JSON',accept:{'application/json':['.json','.txt']}}]});manualApply(await (await h.getFile()).text());}else toast('이 브라우저는 파일 선택을 지원하지 않습니다. 붙여넣기를 쓰세요.');}catch(e){if(e?.name!=='AbortError')fail(e);}});
setTimeout(manualState,1500);$('manual-last').addEventListener('click',()=>$('nav-last-daily').click());
function manualB64(str){const bytes=new TextEncoder().encode(str);let bin='';for(let i=0;i<bytes.length;i+=0x8000)bin+=String.fromCharCode.apply(null,bytes.subarray(i,i+0x8000));return btoa(bin);}
$('manual-publish').addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{
 let text=$('manual-json').value.trim();let data;

 if(!tpl.includes(key))throw Error('이 파일은 빈 템플릿이 아닙니다. 데일리가 들어가기 전 템플릿(인앱귀책웹_템플릿.txt)을 고르세요.');
 let shared=null;try{shared=JSON.parse(AUTO.lastShared||'null');}catch{}const pub={...data,sharedInput:shared&&typeof shared==='object'?shared:{Payload:null}};
 const html=parent.__shellPublish(tpl,APP_CONFIG.dataKey,manualB64(JSON.stringify(pub)));
 toast('② 동기화 폴더에 있는 대시보드 HTML 파일을 골라 덮어쓰세요');
 const sh=await showSaveFilePicker({suggestedName:APP_CONFIG.name+' 대시보드.html',types:[{description:'HTML',accept:{'text/html':['.html']}}]});
 const w=await sh.createWritable();await w.write(new Blob([html],{type:'text/html'}));await w.close();

}catch(e){if(e?.name!=='AbortError')fail(e);}});


/* ===== 지각·미출근 확인 인원이 있으면 우선 확인 카드보다 위로 (일시적) */
let boardDefaultOrder=null;
function checkUrgent(on){const board=document.querySelector('.operations-board');if(!board)return;const cp=board.querySelector(':scope>.check-panel'),pg=board.querySelector(':scope>.priority-grid');if(!cp||!pg)return;
 if(!boardDefaultOrder){try{layTag();}catch{}boardDefaultOrder=[...board.querySelectorAll(':scope>[data-lay]')].map(e=>e.dataset.lay);}
 cp.classList.toggle('urgent',on);
 if(board.classList.contains('lay-edit'))return;
 if(on){if(cp.compareDocumentPosition(pg)&Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING)return;board.insertBefore(cp,pg);}
 else if(cp.dataset.lay&&!(cp.compareDocumentPosition(pg)&Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_PRECEDING)){let saved=null;try{saved=layLoad().order.board;}catch{}const ord=Array.isArray(saved)&&saved.length?saved:boardDefaultOrder;const kids=[...board.querySelectorAll(':scope>[data-lay]')];const anchor=kids[kids.length-1]?.nextSibling||null;kids.sort((a,b)=>{const ia=ord.indexOf(a.dataset.lay),ib=ord.indexOf(b.dataset.lay);return (ia<0?999:ia)-(ib<0?999:ib);}).forEach(el=>board.insertBefore(el,anchor));}
}

function otSeenList(date){try{const v=loadOT(date);return [...Object.keys(v.seen||{}),...Object.entries(v.man||{}).filter(([,x])=>x>0).map(([k])=>k),...Object.entries(v.done||{}).filter(([,x])=>x>0).map(([k])=>k)];}catch{return [];}}
(function(){const calc=E.calculate;E.calculate=function(daily,raw,opts){const o=opts||{};return calc.call(this,daily,raw,o.otSeen?o:{...o,otSeen:otSeenList(daily?.date||'')});};})();

 // 마우스 올리면 펼침 · 마감업무 글자를 클릭하면 고정(다시 클릭하면 해제)

 window.__navCloseSync=v=>{g.classList.toggle('has-active',['rt','edu'].includes(v));};})();
function lastDailySave(data){try{if(!data||data.sample||!['ginjo-daily','ginjo-daily-blocks'].includes(data.type))return;const cur=lastDailyLoad();if(cur&&String(cur.capturedAt||'')>String(data.capturedAt||''))return;const copy={...data};delete copy.sharedInput;localStorage.setItem('ginjo-last-daily',JSON.stringify(copy));}catch{}}
function lastDailyLoad(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ginjo-last-daily')||'null');return v&&v.type?v:null;}catch{return null;}}
function lastDailyUse(silent){const c=lastDailyLoad();if(!c){if(!silent)toast('이 PC에 보관된 데일리가 없습니다.');return false;}try{receive(c,false);const tm=c.capturedAt?new Date(Date.parse(c.capturedAt)+9*3600000).toISOString().slice(5,16).replace('T',' ').replace('-','/'):'시각 없음';$('folder-state').textContent='마지막 정상 데일리 사용 중 · 운영일 '+c.date+' · 생성 '+tm;if(!silent)toast('마지막 정상 데일리('+tm+')로 열었습니다.');setTimeout(()=>{try{tryAdvanceDaily();}catch{}},500);return true;}catch(e){if(!silent)fail(e);return false;}}

/* ===== 글자 크기 · 사이드바 순서 (개인별) */
(function(){
 const steps=[0.8,0.9,1,1.1,1.2,1.3,1.4,1.5];let z=1;try{z=Number(localStorage.getItem('ginjo-zoom'))||1;}catch{}
 const apply=()=>{z=Math.min(1.5,Math.max(0.8,Math.round(z*10)/10));document.body.style.zoom=z===1?'':String(z);$('zoom-val').textContent=Math.round(z*100)+'%';try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-zoom',String(z));}catch{}};
 $('zoom-in').addEventListener('click',()=>{z=steps.find(s=>s>z+0.001)??1.5;apply();});
 $('zoom-out').addEventListener('click',()=>{z=[...steps].reverse().find(s=>s<z-0.001)??0.8;apply();});
 $('zoom-val').addEventListener('click',()=>{z=1;apply();});
 apply();
 const nav=$('nav');if(!nav)return;const KEY='ginjo-nav-order';
 const btns=()=>[...nav.querySelectorAll(':scope>button[data-view]')];
 try{const ord=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)||'null');if(Array.isArray(ord)){const list=btns();list.sort((a,b)=>{const ia=ord.indexOf(a.dataset.view),ib=ord.indexOf(b.dataset.view);return (ia<0?99+list.indexOf(a):ia)-(ib<0?99+list.indexOf(b):ib);}).forEach(b=>nav.appendChild(b));}}catch{}
 let drag=null;const clear=()=>btns().forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('nav-over-before','nav-over-after'));
 const setEdit=on=>{nav.classList.toggle('nav-editing',on);for(const b of btns()){b.draggable=on;b.title=on?'끌어서 순서 변경':'';}$('nav-edit').textContent=on?'완료':'메뉴 순서';$('nav-edit').classList.toggle('on',on);$('nav-reset').classList.toggle('hidden',!on);};setEdit(false);
 $('nav-edit').addEventListener('click',()=>{const on=!nav.classList.contains('nav-editing');setEdit(on);if(on)toast('메뉴를 끌어서 원하는 자리에 놓으세요.');});
 $('nav-reset').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{localStorage.removeItem(KEY);}catch{}location.reload();});
 nav.addEventListener('dragstart',e=>{if(!nav.classList.contains('nav-editing'))return;const b=e.target.closest?.('button[data-view]');if(!b)return;drag=b;b.classList.add('nav-dragging');e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='move';try{e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',b.dataset.view);}catch{}});
 nav.addEventListener('dragend',()=>{drag?.classList.remove('nav-dragging');drag=null;clear();});
 nav.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{const b=e.target.closest?.('button[data-view]');if(!drag||!b||b===drag)return;e.preventDefault();const r=b.getBoundingClientRect();clear();b.classList.add(e.clientY>r.top+r.height/2?'nav-over-after':'nav-over-before');});
 nav.addEventListener('drop',e=>{const b=e.target.closest?.('button[data-view]');if(!drag||!b||b===drag)return;e.preventDefault();nav.insertBefore(drag,b.classList.contains('nav-over-after')?b.nextSibling:b);clear();try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(btns().map(x=>x.dataset.view)));}catch{}});
})();

/* ===== 인수인계 (20261122) */
const HO_EXEC=['조보람','김유라','김성민'],HO_LEAD=['이경윤','하예람'];
const HO_DEF_LEADERS=[['l1','김유진'],['l2','홍예은'],['l3','남문옥'],['l4','이병정'],['l5','노승연'],['l6','김진아'],['l7','류가람']];
let hoEdit=false,hoDropped=0,hoT=0;
function hoJ(key,def){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'null');return v==null?def:v;}catch{return def;}}
function hoPut(key,v){try{localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(v));}catch{}}
function hoRows(){const v=hoJ('ginjo-handover',[]);return Array.isArray(v)?v.filter(x=>x&&x.i):[];}
function hoRowsSave(l){hoPut('ginjo-handover',l);}
function hoLeadersGet(){const v=hoJ('ginjo-ho-leaders',null);if(v&&Array.isArray(v.list)&&v.list.length)return v;return {at:0,list:HO_DEF_LEADERS.map(([id,name])=>({id,name,del:false}))};}
function hoLeadersSave(v){hoPut('ginjo-ho-leaders',v);}
function hoHist(){const v=hoJ('ginjo-ho-hist',[]);return Array.isArray(v)?v:[];}
function hoToday(){const d=new Date();return d.getFullYear()+'-'+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0');}
function hoDiff(a,b){return Math.round((Date.parse(a+'T00:00:00Z')-Date.parse(b+'T00:00:00Z'))/86400000);}
function hoRank(s){s=String(s||'');if(HO_EXEC.some(n=>s.includes(n)))return 'exec';if(HO_LEAD.some(n=>s.includes(n)))return 'lead';return '';}
function hoLead(r){
 if(!r.l||!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(r.l))return null;
 const left=hoDiff(r.l,hoToday());
 if(left<0)return {cls:'over',txt:'⚠ 기한 지남 '+(-left)+'일'};
 if(left===0)return {cls:'warn',txt:'⚠ 오늘 마감'};
 const span=/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(r.d||'')?hoDiff(r.l,r.d):0;
 if(left<=2||(span>=4&&left<=span*0.25))return {cls:'warn',txt:'⚠ D-'+left};
 return {cls:'ok',txt:'D-'+left};
}
function hoShare(){clearTimeout(hoT);hoT=setTimeout(async()=>{try{autoMarkUnsaved();await autoShareAfter();}catch{}},1500);}
function hoBusy(){const b=$('ho-body');return Boolean(b&&document.activeElement&&b.contains(document.activeElement));}

/* 다른 팀장 화면과 공유 (오래된 줄이 많으면 용량 때문에 최근 줄만 공유) */
function handoverSharedPart(){
 const lv=hoLeadersGet(),o={};hoDropped=0;
 const now=Date.now();
 const live=hoRows().filter(r=>!r.x||now-(r.a||0)<14*864e5).sort((a,b)=>(a.x?1:0)-(b.x?1:0)||(b.ct||0)-(a.ct||0));
 const out=[];let size=0;
 for(const r of live){
  const k=Object.entries(r.k||{}).filter(([,v])=>v).map(([a,v])=>a+':'+(v==='확인'?'C':'O')).join('|');
  const arr=r.x?[r.i,'','','','','','',0,r.a||0,1,r.ct||0]:[r.i,r.d||'',r.c||'',r.l||'',r.t||'',r.f||'',k,r.h?1:0,r.a||0,0,r.ct||0];
  const n=JSON.stringify(arr).length+1;
  if(size+n>7000){if(!r.x)hoDropped++;continue;}
  out.push(arr);size+=n;
 }
 if(out.length)o.handover=out;
 if(lv.at)o.hoLeaders={at:lv.at,list:lv.list.map(x=>[x.id,x.name,x.del?1:0])};
 return Object.keys(o).length?o:null;
}
function mergeHandover(input){
 let ch=false;
 if(Array.isArray(input.handover)){
  const map=new Map(hoRows().map(x=>[x.i,x]));
  for(const r of input.handover){
   if(!Array.isArray(r))continue;
   const k={};String(r[6]||'').split('|').forEach(p=>{const [a,b]=p.split(':');if(a&&b)k[a]=b==='C'?'확인':'O';});
   const it={i:String(r[0]),d:String(r[1]||''),c:String(r[2]||''),l:String(r[3]||''),t:String(r[4]||''),f:String(r[5]||''),k,h:r[7]?1:0,a:Number(r[8])||0,x:r[9]?1:0,ct:Number(r[10])||0};
   const mine=map.get(it.i);
   if(!mine||it.a>(mine.a||0)){map.set(it.i,it);ch=true;}
  }
  if(ch)hoRowsSave([...map.values()]);
 }
 if(input.hoLeaders&&Array.isArray(input.hoLeaders.list)){
  const cur=hoLeadersGet();
  if((Number(input.hoLeaders.at)||0)>(cur.at||0)){hoLeadersSave({at:Number(input.hoLeaders.at),list:input.hoLeaders.list.map(r=>({id:String(r[0]),name:String(r[1]||''),del:Boolean(r[2])}))});ch=true;}
 }
 if(ch&&state.view==='handover'&&!hoBusy())hoRender();
}

function hoOpt(v,cur){return '<option value="'+v+'"'+(cur===v?' selected':'')+'>'+(v||'—')+'</option>';}
function hoFromOpts(leaders,cur){
 cur=String(cur||'');
 const names=[...HO_EXEC,...HO_LEAD,...leaders.map(l=>l.name),...(()=>{try{return roLoad().rows.map(p=>p.l);}catch{return [];}})()].map(v=>String(v||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
 const seen=new Set();const list=[];for(const n of names){if(!seen.has(n)){seen.add(n);list.push(n);}}
 if(cur&&!seen.has(cur))list.push(cur);
 return '<option value="">— 선택 —</option>'+list.map(n=>'<option value="'+esc(n)+'"'+(n===cur?' selected':'')+'>'+esc(n)+'</option>').join('');
}
function hoRowHtml(r,leaders){
 const rank=hoRank(r.f),ld=hoLead(r);
 const cls=['ho-r',rank?'ho-'+rank:'',r.h?'ho-hi':'',ld&&ld.cls==='warn'?'ho-warn':'',ld&&ld.cls==='over'?'ho-over':''].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
 return '<tr class="'+cls+'" data-id="'+esc(r.i)+'">'
  +'<td class="ho-c0"><button type="button" class="ho-star'+(r.h?' on':'')+'" data-a="star" title="중요 강조 켜기/끄기">'+(r.h?'★':'☆')+'</button></td>'
  +'<td class="ho-d"><input type="date" class="ho-in" data-f="d" value="'+esc(r.d||'')+'"></td>'
  +'<td class="ho-c"><input class="ho-in" data-f="c" list="ho-cats" maxlength="40" placeholder="분류" value="'+esc(r.c||'')+'"></td>'
  +'<td class="ho-lt"><input type="date" class="ho-in" data-f="l" value="'+esc(r.l||'')+'"><span class="ho-badge'+(ld?' '+ld.cls:' hidden')+'">'+esc(ld?ld.txt:'')+'</span></td>'
  +'<td class="ho-t"><textarea class="ho-in ho-text" data-f="t" rows="1" maxlength="500" placeholder="내용">'+esc(r.t||'')+'</textarea></td>'
  +'<td class="ho-f"><select class="ho-in ho-from" data-f="f" aria-label="전달자">'+hoFromOpts(leaders,r.f)+'</select><span class="ho-rank '+rank+(rank?'':' hidden')+'">'+(rank==='exec'?'실장':rank==='lead'?'선임팀장':'')+'</span></td>'
  +leaders.map(l=>{const v=(r.k||{})[l.id]||'';return '<td class="ho-k"><select class="ho-sel'+(v==='O'?' sO':v==='확인'?' sC':'')+'" data-k="'+esc(l.id)+'" aria-label="'+esc(l.name)+'">'+hoOpt('',v)+hoOpt('O',v)+hoOpt('확인',v)+'</select></td>';}).join('')
  +'<td class="ho-act"><button type="button" class="report-btn" data-a="save" title="이 줄을 이력에 따로 남깁니다">별도 저장</button><button type="button" class="report-btn ho-del" data-a="del" title="이 줄 전체를 지웁니다">삭제</button></td></tr>';
}
function hoRender(){
 const leaders=hoLeadersGet().list.filter(x=>!x.del);
 const q=($('ho-q').value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
 let rows=hoRows().filter(r=>!r.x).sort((a,b)=>(b.ct||0)-(a.ct||0));
 if(q)rows=rows.filter(r=>[r.d,r.c,r.l,r.t,r.f].some(v=>String(v||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)));
 const heads=leaders.map(l=>hoEdit?'<th class="ho-lh"><input class="ho-ln" data-lid="'+esc(l.id)+'" value="'+esc(l.name)+'" maxlength="12" aria-label="팀장 이름"><button type="button" class="ho-lx" data-lid="'+esc(l.id)+'" title="이 팀장 칸 숨기기">✕</button></th>':'<th class="ho-lh">'+esc(l.name)+'</th>').join('');
 $('ho-body').innerHTML='<table class="data ho-table"><thead><tr><th class="ho-c0">★</th><th>기재일</th><th>분류</th><th>리드타임</th><th>내용</th><th>전달자</th>'+heads+'<th class="ho-act">관리</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
  +(rows.length?rows.map(r=>hoRowHtml(r,leaders)).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="'+(7+leaders.length)+'" class="ho-empty">'+(q?'검색 결과가 없습니다.':'아직 인수인계가 없습니다. 오른쪽 위 “+ 인수인계 추가”를 눌러 시작하세요.')+'</td></tr>')
  +'</tbody></table>';
 const all=hoRows().filter(r=>!r.x);
 const uniq=a=>[...new Set(a.map(v=>String(v||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
 $('ho-cats').innerHTML=uniq(all.map(r=>r.c)).map(v=>'<option value="'+esc(v)+'">').join('');
 $('ho-froms').innerHTML=uniq([...HO_EXEC,...HO_LEAD,...leaders.map(l=>l.name),...(()=>{try{return roLoad().rows.map(p=>p.l);}catch{return [];}})(),...all.map(r=>r.f)]).map(v=>'<option value="'+esc(v)+'">').join('');
 $('ho-lead-add').classList.toggle('hidden',!hoEdit);$('ho-lead-edit').textContent=hoEdit?'팀장 편집 완료':'팀장 편집';$('ho-lead-edit').classList.toggle('on',hoEdit);
 handoverSharedPart();
 hoFitSoon();const w=$('ho-warn');w.classList.toggle('hidden',!hoDropped);w.textContent=hoDropped?'공유 용량이 가득 차서 오래된 '+hoDropped+'줄은 다른 팀장님 화면에 보이지 않습니다. 끝난 오래된 줄을 삭제해 주세요.':'';
 hoRenderHist();
}
function hoFit(ta){if(!ta||!ta.offsetParent)return;ta.style.height='auto';ta.style.height=(ta.scrollHeight+2)+'px';}
function hoFitAll(){document.querySelectorAll('#ho-body .ho-text').forEach(hoFit);}
let hoFitT=0;function hoFitSoon(){cancelAnimationFrame(hoFitT);hoFitT=requestAnimationFrame(hoFitAll);}
function hoRefreshRow(tr,r){
 const rank=hoRank(r.f),ld=hoLead(r);
 tr.className=['ho-r',rank?'ho-'+rank:'',r.h?'ho-hi':'',ld&&ld.cls==='warn'?'ho-warn':'',ld&&ld.cls==='over'?'ho-over':''].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
 const b=tr.querySelector('.ho-badge');if(b){b.className='ho-badge'+(ld?' '+ld.cls:' hidden');b.textContent=ld?ld.txt:'';}
 const k=tr.querySelector('.ho-rank');if(k){k.className='ho-rank '+rank+(rank?'':' hidden');k.textContent=rank==='exec'?'실장':rank==='lead'?'선임팀장':'';}
 const s=tr.querySelector('.ho-star');if(s){s.classList.toggle('on',Boolean(r.h));s.textContent=r.h?'★':'☆';}
}
function hoRenderHist(){
 const list=hoHist().sort((a,b)=>(b.s||0)-(a.s||0));
 $('ho-hist-n').textContent='('+list.length+'건)';
 const fmt=ms=>{const d=new Date(ms);return (d.getMonth()+1)+'/'+d.getDate()+' '+String(d.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');};
 $('ho-hist').innerHTML=list.length?'<div class="table-wrap"><table class="data ho-hist-table"><thead><tr><th>저장</th><th>기재일</th><th>분류</th><th>리드타임</th><th>내용</th><th>전달자</th><th>팀장 확인 상태</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>'
  +list.map(h=>'<tr data-hid="'+esc(h.i)+'"><td>'+esc(fmt(h.s))+(h.by?'<br><small>'+esc(h.by)+'</small>':'')+'</td><td>'+esc(h.d||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(h.c||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(h.l||'')+'</td><td class="ho-hist-t">'+esc(h.t||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(h.f||'')+'</td><td>'+(h.k||[]).map(p=>esc(p[0])+' <b class="'+(p[1]==='확인'?'ho-ok':'ho-open')+'">'+esc(p[1])+'</b>').join(' · ')+'</td><td><button type="button" class="report-btn ho-del" data-ha="del" title="이력 한 건 지우기">✕</button></td></tr>').join('')
  +'</tbody></table></div>':'<p class="tiny">아직 저장한 이력이 없습니다. 줄 오른쪽 “별도 저장”을 누르면 그 줄 한 건이 이력에 남습니다.</p>';
}
(function(){
 const body=$('ho-body');
 const findRow=tr=>{const id=tr&&tr.dataset.id;const list=hoRows();return {list,row:list.find(x=>x.i===id)};};
 const touch=(list,r)=>{r.a=Date.now();hoRowsSave(list);hoShare();};
 body.addEventListener('input',e=>{
  const f=e.target.dataset&&e.target.dataset.f;if(!f)return;
  const tr=e.target.closest('tr');const {list,row}=findRow(tr);if(!row)return;
  row[f]=e.target.value;touch(list,row);hoRefreshRow(tr,row);if(f==='t')hoFit(e.target);
 });
 body.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{const t=e.target;if(!(t.dataset&&t.dataset.f==='c'))return;t.dataset.prev=t.value;t.dataset.touched='';t.value='';});
 body.addEventListener('input',e=>{const t=e.target;if(t.dataset&&t.dataset.f==='c')t.dataset.touched='1';},true);
 body.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{const t=e.target;if(t.dataset&&t.dataset.f==='c'&&(e.key==='Backspace'||e.key==='Delete')){t.dataset.touched='1';if(t.value===''){const tr=t.closest('tr'),{list,row}=findRow(tr);if(row){row[t.dataset.f]='';touch(list,row);hoRefreshRow(tr,row);}}}});
 body.addEventListener('focusout',e=>{const t=e.target;if(!(t.dataset&&t.dataset.f==='c'))return;if(!t.dataset.touched&&t.value===''&&t.dataset.prev!==undefined)t.value=t.dataset.prev;});
 body.addEventListener('change',e=>{
  const t=e.target;
  if(t.classList.contains('ho-sel')){
   const tr=t.closest('tr');const {list,row}=findRow(tr);if(!row)return;
   row.k=row.k||{};if(t.value)row.k[t.dataset.k]=t.value;else delete row.k[t.dataset.k];
   t.className='ho-sel'+(t.value==='O'?' sO':t.value==='확인'?' sC':'');touch(list,row);
   try{logChange('인수인계 확인 · '+(hoLeadersGet().list.find(x=>x.id===t.dataset.k)?.name||'')+' '+(t.value||'비움'));}catch{}
   return;
  }
  if(t.classList.contains('ho-ln')){
   const lv=hoLeadersGet(),it=lv.list.find(x=>x.id===t.dataset.lid);if(!it)return;
   const name=t.value.trim();if(!name){t.value=it.name;return;}
   it.name=name;lv.at=Date.now();hoLeadersSave(lv);hoShare();hoRender();
  }
 });
 body.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const x=e.target.closest('.ho-lx');
  if(x){
   if(!confirm('이 팀장 칸을 숨길까요? 이미 입력된 O/확인 기록은 남아 있고, 이력에도 그대로 보관됩니다.'))return;
   const lv=hoLeadersGet(),it=lv.list.find(v=>v.id===x.dataset.lid);if(!it)return;
   if(lv.list.filter(v=>!v.del).length<=1){toast('팀장 칸은 최소 한 개는 있어야 합니다.');return;}
   it.del=true;lv.at=Date.now();hoLeadersSave(lv);hoShare();hoRender();return;
  }
  const b=e.target.closest('button[data-a]');if(!b)return;
  const tr=b.closest('tr');const {list,row}=findRow(tr);if(!row)return;
  if(b.dataset.a==='star'){row.h=row.h?0:1;touch(list,row);hoRefreshRow(tr,row);return;}
  if(b.dataset.a==='del'){
   if(!confirm('이 인수인계 줄을 통째로 지울까요?\n(“별도 저장”으로 이력에 남기지 않았다면 복구할 수 없습니다.)'))return;
   row.x=1;touch(list,row);try{logChange('인수인계 삭제 · '+(row.c||'')+' '+String(row.t||'').slice(0,20));}catch{}hoRender();toast('삭제했습니다.');return;
  }
  if(b.dataset.a==='save'){
   const names=new Map(hoLeadersGet().list.map(l=>[l.id,l.name]));
   const k=Object.entries(row.k||{}).filter(([,v])=>v).map(([id,v])=>[names.get(id)||id,v]);
   const h=hoHist();h.push({i:Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6),s:Date.now(),by:(typeof myName==='function'?myName():''),d:row.d||'',c:row.c||'',l:row.l||'',t:row.t||'',f:row.f||'',k});
   hoPut('ginjo-ho-hist',h.slice(-1000));hoRenderHist();toast('이 줄을 이력에 따로 저장했습니다.');
   try{logChange('인수인계 별도 저장 · '+(row.c||'')+' '+String(row.t||'').slice(0,20));}catch{}
  }
 });
 $('ho-hist').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('button[data-ha="del"]');if(!b)return;
  if(!confirm('이력 한 건을 지울까요?'))return;
  const id=b.closest('tr').dataset.hid;hoPut('ginjo-ho-hist',hoHist().filter(h=>h.i!==id));hoRenderHist();
 });
 $('ho-add').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const now=Date.now(),list=hoRows();

  list.push(r);hoRowsSave(list);$('ho-q').value='';hoRender();hoShare();
  const ta=$('ho-body').querySelector('tr[data-id="'+r.i+'"] .ho-text');if(ta)ta.focus();
 });
 $('ho-q').addEventListener('input',()=>{if(!hoBusy()||document.activeElement===$('ho-q'))hoRender();});
 $('ho-lead-edit').addEventListener('click',()=>{hoEdit=!hoEdit;hoRender();});
 $('ho-lead-add').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const lv=hoLeadersGet();const id='l'+Date.now().toString(36);
  lv.list.push({id,name:'새 팀장',del:false});lv.at=Date.now();hoLeadersSave(lv);hoShare();hoRender();
  const inp=$('ho-body').querySelector('.ho-ln[data-lid="'+id+'"]');if(inp){inp.focus();inp.select();}
 });
 try{new ResizeObserver(hoFitSoon).observe($('ho-body'));}catch{}
 setInterval(()=>{if(state.view!=='handover'||hoBusy())return;try{hoRender();}catch{}},60000);
})();

/* ===== 재직 리스트 (20261122) */
let roShareOver=false;
function roLoad(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('ginjo-roster')||'null');return v&&Array.isArray(v.rows)?v:{at:0,rows:[]};}catch{return {at:0,rows:[]};}}
function roSave(v){try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-roster',JSON.stringify(v));return true;}catch{return false;}}
/* 다른 팀장 화면과 공유: 필요한 7개 열만 (C사번 T사번 이름 근무시간 시프트 팀장 번호) */
function rosterSharedPart(){
 const db=roLoad();if(!db.at)return null;
 const rows=db.rows.map(p=>[p.c,p.t,p.n,p.h,p.s,p.l,p.p]);
 roShareOver=JSON.stringify(rows).length>14000;
 if(roShareOver)return null;
 return {roster:{at:db.at,rows}};
}
function mergeRoster(input){
 const r=input.roster;if(!r||!Array.isArray(r.rows))return;
 const cur=roLoad();if((Number(r.at)||0)<=(cur.at||0))return;
 roSave({at:Number(r.at),rows:r.rows.map(a=>({c:a[0]||'',t:a[1]||'',k:'',n:a[2]||'',h:a[3]||'',s:a[4]||'',l:a[5]||'',j:'',m:'',p:a[6]||''}))});
 const act=document.activeElement;
 if(state.view==='roster'&&!(act&&(act===$('ro-q')||act===$('ro-raw'))))roRender();
}
async function roShare(){
 const say=(t,bad)=>{try{const n=$('ro-note');n.textContent=t;n.style.color=bad?'#c0392b':'';}catch{}if(bad){try{toast(t);}catch{}try{devLogAdd('share','재직 리스트: '+t);}catch{}}};
 try{
  if(!APP_CONFIG.sharedEnabled)return say('공유 저장 기능이 꺼져 있어서 이 PC에만 저장됐습니다.',true);
  if(state.demo)return say('샘플 화면이라 공유되지 않습니다.',true);
  if(!state.daily)return say('데일리를 아직 불러오지 못해서 공유되지 않았습니다. 데일리가 불러와진 뒤 다시 저장해 주세요.',true);
  if(state.dailyDraftDirty)return say('데일리 수정 중이라 공유되지 않았습니다. 데일리 수정을 끝낸 뒤 다시 저장해 주세요.',true);
  if(roShareOver)return say('명단이 커서 공유되지 않습니다. (이 PC에만 저장)',true);
  autoMarkUnsaved();await autoShareAfter();
  setTimeout(()=>{try{const s=$('issue-save-state'),t=(s&&s.textContent)||'';if(s&&s.className==='ok')say('공유 저장 완료 · '+t+' (다른 팀장님은 새 버전 화면에서 몇 분 안에 보입니다)');else say('공유 저장이 안 됐습니다: '+(t||'공유 저장 폴더 연결을 확인하세요.')+' — 이 PC에만 저장된 상태입니다.',true);}catch{}},1500);
 }catch(err){say('공유 저장 중 오류: '+(err&&err.message||err),true);}
}
function roPhone(v){
 const raw=String(v||'').trim();let s=raw.replace(/[^\d]/g,'');
 if(!s)return raw;
 if(s.length===10&&s[0]==='1')s='0'+s;
 if(s.length===11)return s.slice(0,3)+'-'+s.slice(3,7)+'-'+s.slice(7);
 if(s.length===10)return s.slice(0,3)+'-'+s.slice(3,6)+'-'+s.slice(6);
 return raw;
}
/* 엑셀 열 순서: C사번 T사번 K사번 이름 근무시간 시프트 담당팀장 입사일 SM투입일 x y z 번호 (x y z는 무시) */
function roParse(text){
 const lines=String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n'),rows=[];let skipped=0,header=0;
 const isPhone=v=>/^0?1\d[\d\s-]{7,10}$/.test(String(v||'').trim());
 for(const line of lines){
  if(!line.trim())continue;
  const cells=line.split('\t').map(x=>x.trim().replace(/^"(.*)"$/s,'$1').trim());
  if(rows.length===0&&(cells.includes('이름')||/사번/.test(cells[0]||''))){header++;continue;}
  const [c,t,k,n,h,s,l,j,m]=cells;
  let p=cells[12]||'';if(!isPhone(p)){const f=cells.slice(9).find(isPhone);if(f)p=f;}
  if(!n&&!c&&!t){skipped++;continue;}
  rows.push({c:c||'',t:t||'',k:k||'',n:n||'',h:h||'',s:s||'',l:l||'',j:j||'',m:m||'',p:roPhone(p)});
 }
 return {rows,skipped,header};
}
function roLeaderMap(){
 const m=new Map();
 for(const r of state.daily?.schedule||[]){
  if(!r.leader)continue;
  for(const key of [r.id,r.idKey]){const k=String(key||'').trim().toLowerCase();if(k&&!m.has(k))m.set(k,r.leader);}
 }
 return m;
}
/* 팀장은 오늘 데일리가 우선, 데일리에 없으면 엑셀 값 */
function roLeaderOf(p,map){
 for(const key of [p.c,p.t,p.k]){const k=String(key||'').trim().toLowerCase();if(k&&map.has(k))return leaderGroup(map.get(k));}
 return leaderGroup(p.l);
}
function roNorm(v){return String(v||'').toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,'');}
function roRender(){
 const db=roLoad(),map=roLeaderMap();
 const all=db.rows.map(p=>({p,lead:roLeaderOf(p,map)}));
 const q=roNorm($('ro-q').value);
 const at=db.at?new Date(db.at):null;
 $('ro-count').textContent=all.length?all.length+'명 · 저장 '+(at.getMonth()+1)+'/'+at.getDate()+' '+String(at.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(at.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0'):'저장된 명단이 없습니다';
 rosterSharedPart();
 const dropped=globalThis.__roDropped&&Date.now()-globalThis.__roDropped<600000;
 const warn=roShareOver?'명단이 커서 다른 팀장님 화면에는 공유되지 않습니다.':dropped?'공유 저장 용량이 부족해서 최근 저장에서 재직 리스트가 빠졌습니다.':'';
 $('ro-warn').textContent=warn;$('ro-warn').classList.toggle('hidden',!warn);
 const box=$('ro-list'),filt=$('ro-team-wrap');
 if(!all.length){filt.classList.add('hidden');box.innerHTML='<p class="tiny">아직 명단이 없습니다. 위 “엑셀 붙여넣기”에서 저장해 주세요.</p>';return;}
 if(q){
  filt.classList.add('hidden');
  const hit=all.filter(x=>roNorm(x.p.n).includes(q)||roNorm(x.p.t).includes(q)||roNorm(x.p.c).includes(q));
  hit.sort((a,b)=>{const ea=[a.p.t,a.p.c].some(v=>roNorm(v)===q)?0:1,eb=[b.p.t,b.p.c].some(v=>roNorm(v)===q)?0:1;return ea-eb||String(a.p.n).localeCompare(String(b.p.n),'ko');});
  const shown=hit.slice(0,20);
  box.innerHTML=(shown.length?shown.map(x=>{const p=x.p;return '<div class="ro-hit"><div class="ro-lead">'+esc(x.lead)+(x.lead==='미지정'?'':' 팀')+'</div><div class="ro-name"><small>이름</small><b>'+esc(p.n||'—')+'</b></div><div class="ro-sep"></div><div><small>T사번</small><b>'+esc(p.t||'—')+'</b></div><div><small>근무시간</small><b>'+esc(p.h||'—')+'</b></div><div><small>시프트</small><b>'+esc(p.s||'—')+'</b></div><div><small>번호</small><b>'+esc(p.p||'—')+'</b></div>'+(p.p?'<button type="button" class="report-btn ro-copy" data-copy="'+esc(p.p)+'">번호 복사</button>':'<span></span>')+'</div>';}).join(''):'<p class="tiny">일치하는 인원이 없습니다.</p>')
   +(hit.length>shown.length?'<p class="tiny">'+hit.length+'명이 일치합니다. 처음 '+shown.length+'명만 보여드립니다. 더 입력해서 좁혀 주세요.</p>':'');
  return;
 }
 const groups=new Map(EDU_LEADERS.map(n=>[n,[]]));
 for(const x of all){if(!groups.has(x.lead))groups.set(x.lead,[]);groups.get(x.lead).push(x);}
 for(const [k,v] of [...groups]){if(!v.length)groups.delete(k);}
 if(groups.has('미지정')){const v=groups.get('미지정');groups.delete('미지정');groups.set('미지정',v);}
 let pick='';try{pick=localStorage.getItem('ginjo-ro-team')||'';}catch{}
 if(!groups.has(pick))pick='';
 filt.classList.remove('hidden');
 $('ro-team').innerHTML='<option value="">전체 팀장</option>'+[...groups.keys()].map(n=>'<option value="'+esc(n)+'"'+(n===pick?' selected':'')+'>'+esc(n)+(n==='미지정'?'':' 팀')+' ('+groups.get(n).length+')</option>').join('');
 const names=pick?[pick]:[...groups.keys()];
 box.innerHTML='<div class="edu-teams ro-teams">'+names.map(n=>{
  const rows=groups.get(n).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.p.n).localeCompare(String(b.p.n),'ko'));
  return '<div class="edu-team"><div class="edu-team-head"><b>'+esc(n)+(n==='미지정'?' (데일리·엑셀 팀장 없음)':' 팀')+'</b><small>'+rows.length+'명</small></div><table class="ro-table"><thead><tr><th>T사번</th><th>이름</th><th>근무시간</th><th>시프트</th><th>번호</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
   +rows.map(x=>'<tr><td>'+esc(x.p.t)+'</td><td><b>'+esc(x.p.n)+'</b></td><td>'+esc(x.p.h)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.p.s)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.p.p)+'</td></tr>').join('')
   +'</tbody></table></div>';}).join('')+'</div>';
}
(function(){
 $('ro-q').addEventListener('input',roRender);
 $('ro-q').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'){e.target.value='';roRender();}});
 $('ro-team').addEventListener('change',e=>{try{localStorage.setItem('ginjo-ro-team',e.target.value);}catch{}roRender();});
 $('ro-save').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const r=roParse($('ro-raw').value);
  if(!r.rows.length){$('ro-note').textContent='읽을 수 있는 줄이 없습니다. 엑셀에서 행을 복사해 그대로 붙여넣어 주세요. (열 순서: C사번 T사번 K사번 이름 근무시간 시프트 담당팀장 입사일 SM투입일 x y z 번호)';return;}
  const cur=roLoad().rows.length;
  if(cur&&!confirm('저장된 '+cur+'명을 새로 붙여넣은 '+r.rows.length+'명으로 바꿉니다. 계속할까요?'))return;
  if(!roSave({at:Date.now(),rows:r.rows})){$('ro-note').textContent='저장하지 못했습니다. 브라우저 저장 공간을 확인하세요.';return;}
  $('ro-raw').value='';$('ro-note').textContent=r.rows.length+'명 저장 완료'+(r.header?' · 제목 줄은 건너뜀':'')+(r.skipped?' · 빈 줄 '+r.skipped+'건 건너뜀':'');
  try{logChange('재직 리스트 저장 · '+r.rows.length+'명');}catch{}
  toast(r.rows.length+'명을 저장했습니다. 다른 팀장님 화면에도 공유됩니다.');roRender();roShare();
 });
 $('ro-clear').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(!roLoad().rows.length){$('ro-raw').value='';return;}
  if(!confirm('저장된 재직 리스트를 모두 지울까요?'))return;
  roSave({at:Date.now(),rows:[]});
  $('ro-note').textContent='비웠습니다.';roRender();roShare();
 });
 $('ro-list').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('.ro-copy');if(!b)return;
  const v=b.dataset.copy||'';
  const done=()=>toast('번호를 복사했습니다.');
  try{navigator.clipboard.writeText(v).then(done,()=>{const t=document.createElement('textarea');t.value=v;document.body.appendChild(t);t.select();try{document.execCommand('copy');done();}catch{}t.remove();});}catch{}
 });
 setInterval(()=>{if(state.view!=='roster')return;const a=document.activeElement;if(a&&(a===$('ro-q')||a===$('ro-raw')))return;try{roRender();}catch{}},60000);
})();

/* ===== 개발 테스트 로그 · 백업/복원 (20261122) */
const DEV_KEY='ginjo-devlog',DEV_LIMIT=300;
const DEV_VIEWS={people:'현황',issues:'문제 예상 인원',ot:'OT 현황',macro:'매크로 검색',ext:'외부 팀즈',handover:'인수인계',roster:'재직 리스트',forecast:'10일 예상',rt:'실시간 배정',edu:'교육 인원',daily:'데일리 수정',settings:'연결·저장',dev:'개발 테스트 로그'};
const DEV_LAST={t:0,b:''};let devFilter='',devCount=0,devSeen=new Map(),devShareT=0;
function devLoad(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(DEV_KEY)||'null');if(v&&Array.isArray(v.list))return {clr:Number(v.clr)||0,list:v.list.filter(x=>x&&x.i)};}catch{}return {clr:0,list:[]};}
function devSave(v){try{localStorage.setItem(DEV_KEY,JSON.stringify(v));}catch{}}
function devDescribe(t){
 try{const el=t.closest&&t.closest('button,summary,a,select,input,textarea,label,[data-view]');if(!el)return '';
  let s=(el.innerText||el.value||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
  if(!s||s.length>40)s=(el.getAttribute('aria-label')||el.title||el.placeholder||el.id||s||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
  return s.slice(0,30);}catch{return '';}
}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=devDescribe(e.target);if(b){DEV_LAST.t=Date.now();DEV_LAST.b=b;}},true);
document.addEventListener('change',e=>{const b=devDescribe(e.target);if(b){DEV_LAST.t=Date.now();DEV_LAST.b=b+' (선택/입력)';}},true);
function devLogAdd(kind,msg){
 try{
  msg=String(msg||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().slice(0,160);if(!msg||/ResizeObserver loop/i.test(msg))return;
  const now=Date.now(),key=kind+'|'+msg;if(now-(devSeen.get(key)||0)<30000)return;devSeen.set(key,now);
  if(++devCount>40)return;
  const db=devLoad();
  db.list.push({i:now.toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6),a:now,w:(typeof myName==='function'?myName():'').slice(0,12),k:kind==='share'?'share':'error',v:state.view||'',b:(now-DEV_LAST.t<20000&&DEV_LAST.b)?DEV_LAST.b:'(자동 실행·버튼 없음)',m:msg});
  db.list=db.list.sort((x,y)=>y.a-x.a).slice(0,DEV_LIMIT);devSave(db);
  if(state.view==='dev')devRender();
  if(kind!=='share'){clearTimeout(devShareT);devShareT=setTimeout(()=>{try{autoMarkUnsaved();autoShareAfter();}catch{}},4000);}
 }catch{}
}
window.__devLive=(kind,msg)=>devLogAdd(kind,msg);
try{for(const [k,m] of (window.__devEarly||[]))devLogAdd(k,m);window.__devEarly=[];}catch{}
function devSharedPart(){const db=devLoad();if(!db.list.length&&!db.clr)return null;return {devLog:{clr:db.clr,rows:db.list.slice(0,60).map(x=>[x.i,x.a,x.w,x.k,x.v,x.b,x.m])}};}
function mergeDevLog(input){
 const r=input.devLog;if(!r||!Array.isArray(r.rows))return;
 const db=devLoad();let ch=false;const clr=Math.max(db.clr,Number(r.clr)||0);if(clr!==db.clr){db.clr=clr;ch=true;}
 const have=new Set(db.list.map(x=>x.i));
 for(const a of r.rows){if(!Array.isArray(a)||!a[0]||have.has(String(a[0])))continue;const at=Number(a[1])||0;if(at<=clr)continue;db.list.push({i:String(a[0]),a:at,w:String(a[2]||''),k:a[3]==='share'?'share':'error',v:String(a[4]||''),b:String(a[5]||''),m:String(a[6]||'')});have.add(String(a[0]));ch=true;}
 const kept=db.list.filter(x=>x.a>clr).sort((x,y)=>y.a-x.a).slice(0,DEV_LIMIT);if(kept.length!==db.list.length)ch=true;db.list=kept;
 if(ch){devSave(db);if(state.view==='dev')devRender();}
}
function devFmt(ms){const d=new Date(ms),p=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');return p(d.getMonth()+1)+'/'+p(d.getDate())+' '+p(d.getHours())+':'+p(d.getMinutes())+':'+p(d.getSeconds());}
function devText(list){return list.map(x=>'['+devFmt(x.a)+'] '+(x.w||'이름 미설정')+' · '+(x.k==='share'?'공유':'오류')+' · 화면: '+(DEV_VIEWS[x.v]||x.v||'-')+' · 버튼: '+x.b+' · '+x.m).join('\n');}
function devRows(){const db=devLoad();return db.list.filter(x=>x.a>db.clr&&(!devFilter||x.k===devFilter)).sort((a,b)=>b.a-a.a);}
function devRender(){
 const box=$('dev-list');if(!box)return;
 const all=devLoad().list,rows=devRows();
 const chip=(v,t,n)=>'<button type="button" class="macro-chip'+(devFilter===v?' on':'')+'" data-f="'+v+'">'+t+' <i>'+n+'</i></button>';
 $('dev-chips').innerHTML=chip('','전체',all.length)+chip('error','오류',all.filter(x=>x.k==='error').length)+chip('share','공유',all.filter(x=>x.k==='share').length);
 const conn=($('share-conn')&&$('share-conn').textContent)||'',lastOk=AUTO.savedAt?devFmt(AUTO.savedAt):'아직 없음',lastBad=all.filter(x=>x.k==='share').sort((a,b)=>b.a-a.a)[0];
 $('dev-share').innerHTML='<div><small>공유 저장 연결</small><b>'+esc(conn||'확인 중')+'</b></div><div><small>이 PC 마지막 공유 성공</small><b>'+esc(lastOk)+'</b></div><div><small>마지막 공유 문제</small><b>'+(lastBad?esc(devFmt(lastBad.a)+' · '+lastBad.m):'없음')+'</b></div><div><small>이 화면 버전</small><b>'+esc(String((document.querySelector('footer span:last-child')||{}).textContent||'').slice(0,40)||'—')+'</b></div>';
 box.innerHTML=rows.length?'<table class="data dev-table"><thead><tr><th>일시</th><th>팀장</th><th>구분</th><th>화면</th><th>누른 버튼</th><th>내용</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.slice(0,200).map(x=>'<tr><td>'+esc(devFmt(x.a))+'</td><td>'+esc(x.w||'-')+'</td><td><span class="dev-k '+x.k+'">'+(x.k==='share'?'공유':'오류')+'</span></td><td>'+esc(DEV_VIEWS[x.v]||x.v||'-')+'</td><td>'+esc(x.b)+'</td><td class="dev-m">'+esc(x.m)+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table>':'<p class="tiny">기록이 없습니다. 오류가 나거나 공유 저장이 실패하면 여기에 쌓입니다.</p>';
}
function devCopy(text,okMsg){const done=()=>toast(okMsg);try{navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done,()=>{const t=document.createElement('textarea');t.value=text;document.body.appendChild(t);t.select();try{document.execCommand('copy');done();}catch{}t.remove();});}catch{}}
const DEV_BK=['ginjo-macros','ginjo-handover','ginjo-ho-leaders','ginjo-ho-hist','ginjo-roster','ginjo-rt-rules','ginjo-ext-src','ginjo-ext-map','ginjo-nav-order','ginjo-me'];
function devBackupBuild(){const keys={};for(const k of DEV_BK){try{const v=localStorage.getItem(k);if(v!==null)keys[k]=v;}catch{}}return {type:'ginjo-backup',version:1,at:Date.now(),by:(typeof myName==='function'?myName():''),keys};}
function devRestoreApply(obj){
 if(!obj||obj.type!=='ginjo-backup'||!obj.keys||typeof obj.keys!=='object')throw Error(APP_CONFIG.name+' 백업 파일이 아닙니다.');
 const now=Date.now();let n=0;
 for(const k of DEV_BK){const raw=obj.keys[k];if(typeof raw!=='string'||raw.length>3000000)continue;let v=raw;
  try{const j=JSON.parse(raw);
   if(k==='ginjo-macros'&&Array.isArray(j))v=JSON.stringify(j.map(x=>({...x,at:now})));
   else if(k==='ginjo-handover'&&Array.isArray(j))v=JSON.stringify(j.map(x=>({...x,a:now})));
   else if((k==='ginjo-ho-leaders'||k==='ginjo-roster'||k==='ginjo-rt-rules')&&j&&typeof j==='object')v=JSON.stringify({...j,at:now});
  }catch{}
  try{localStorage.setItem(k,v);n++;}catch{}}
 return n;
}
(function(){
 $('dev-chips').addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-f]');if(!b)return;devFilter=b.dataset.f;devRender();});
 $('dev-copy').addEventListener('click',()=>{const rows=devRows().slice(0,50);if(!rows.length){toast('복사할 기록이 없습니다.');return;}devCopy('['+APP_CONFIG.name+' 로그 · 최근 '+rows.length+'건 · '+((document.querySelector('footer span:last-child')||{}).textContent||'')+']\n'+devText(rows),'로그 '+rows.length+'건을 복사했습니다. 이슈에 붙여넣으세요.');});
 $('dev-clear').addEventListener('click',async()=>{if(!confirm('쌓인 로그를 모두 지울까요?\n다른 팀장님 화면의 기록도 함께 지워집니다.'))return;devSave({clr:Date.now(),list:[]});devRender();toast('로그를 지웠습니다.');try{autoMarkUnsaved();await autoShareAfter();}catch{}});
 $('dev-backup').addEventListener('click',()=>{const b=devBackupBuild(),d=new Date(),p=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');download(APP_CONFIG.name+'_백업_'+d.getFullYear()+p(d.getMonth()+1)+p(d.getDate())+'_'+p(d.getHours())+p(d.getMinutes())+'.json',JSON.stringify(b),'application/json');$('dev-bk-note').textContent='백업 파일을 내려받았습니다 ('+Object.keys(b.keys).length+'개 항목: 매크로·인수인계·재직 리스트·식사 규칙·외부 팀즈 등).';});
 $('dev-restore').addEventListener('change',async e=>{
  const f=e.target.files&&e.target.files[0];e.target.value='';if(!f)return;
  try{
   if(f.size>6000000)throw Error('파일이 너무 큽니다.');
   const obj=JSON.parse((await f.text()).replace(/^﻿/,''));
   const names=Object.keys(obj.keys||{}).filter(k=>DEV_BK.includes(k)).length;
   if(!confirm('백업 파일('+(obj.by||'이름 없음')+(obj.at?' · '+devFmt(obj.at):'')+', '+names+'개 항목)으로 이 PC의 매크로·인수인계·재직 리스트·식사 규칙 등을 바꿉니다.\n바꾼 내용은 다른 팀장님 화면에도 공유됩니다. 계속할까요?'))return;
   const n=devRestoreApply(obj);
   $('dev-bk-note').textContent=n+'개 항목을 복원했습니다. 화면을 새로고침합니다…';
   try{autoMarkUnsaved();await autoShareAfter();}catch{}
   setTimeout(()=>location.reload(),1200);
  }catch(err){$('dev-bk-note').textContent='복원하지 못했습니다: '+(err&&err.message||err);devLogAdd('error','백업 복원 실패: '+(err&&err.message||err));}
 });
})();

})();

/* ===== 데이터 자리: Power Automate가 아래 두 값을 각각 바꿔 넣습니다 (인앱 / 귀책) ===== */
const SHELL_DATA={"dataInapp":"__INAPP_DAILY_BASE64__","dataGwijaek":"__GWIJAEK_DAILY_BASE64__"};
/* ===== 두 화면 설정 ===== */
const APPS=[
 {key:'inapp',ns:'ia',name:'인앱',full:'인앱전담',dataKey:'dataInapp',ph:'INAPP'},
 {key:'gwijaek',ns:'gj',name:'귀책',full:'귀책전담',dataKey:'dataGwijaek',ph:'GWIJAEK'}
];
const MENU=[
 ['people','현황'],['issues','문제 예상 인원'],['ot','OT 현황'],/* ['handover','인수인계'], */ /* ['roster','재직 리스트'], */['forecast','10일 예상'],
 /* ['rt','실시간 배정'], */['edu','교육 인원'],null,['daily','데일리 수정'],['settings','연결·저장'],['@last','↺ 마지막 데일리']
